- **Maps-manager row selection + placedItems `visible` default (live 2026-11-11 run IF):** every Maps-manager row button carries an EXACT aria-label — `Open "<displayName>"`, `Activate "<name>"`, `Delete "<name>"` etc. — use it; a row-context probe that CLIMBS ANCESTORS from the button reaches the whole list wrapper and its text match picks the FIRST row (opened Battle Arena twice while meaning if-3d — a wrong-map rig ride + a false "create ignores gridSize" lead; `clampGridSize` at `maps.js:148` DOES honor POST `gridSize` ≥5≤100). Escaped-quote selectors (`'[aria-label="Open \\"x\\""]'`) mangle through the MCP code runner — build them inside `page.evaluate` with `String.fromCharCode(34/39)`, and when constructing the attr selector as a string use OUTER single quotes with the inner `"` raw (live 2026-11-11 improve map3d-chip-token-split: outer-double-quote construction throws `Invalid selector`). A settled mounted board ADOPTS whole-doc PUTs live via the map-data echo (the §56 clobber fears don't apply post-settle). RE-PROVED hard (live 2026-11-11 improve-run PT2 re-verify): whole-doc `players[]` add/move/remove adopts on the SAME mounted tab in ~400 ms with the map ACTIVE and with it merely Opened (non-active) — the "indoor players PUT stale-until-remount" backlog lead was a settle-timing/3D-census-race artifact, not an echo-lane gap; wait Open→settle ~2.5 s then PUT, and poll DOM/census VALUES not one-shot reads. RIG LAW: placedItems OMITTING the `visible` key = hidden tier (`isHidden/isVisible/itemOpacity` all read `!item.visible`) — probes wanting a fogged-but-VISIBLE or plain-visible item must write `visible:true` explicitly or the amber `hidden-item-ring` (73bb60655) fires and the item is stripped from LAN views.
- **Popup families + attack-hit Done-gate (live 2026-11-11 run IF, backdrop-void claim CORRECTED by same-day improve hit-popup-backdrop-void-guard):** three distinct popup DOM families — save prompts = `.sp-modal` (the LIVE one uniquely carries `data-testid="active-save-prompt"`, single-active through Roll Save and Done stages; a disabled AoE picker stays mounted behind it inside a `.sp-overlay` and carries NONE); attack/modals = `.sp-overlay`; monster-card attack popups = `.popup-overlay > .popup-modal` ("✓ HIT (n vs AC m)"). The hit popup STAGES damage only when `Done` is pressed. CORRECTION (improve re-ride, live-proved): the shared `Popup` backdrop has been INERT since 57d68ff80 (no overlay click handler; pinned by popup.test.jsx "no click dismissal") — a backdrop click with damage staged leaves the popup mounted and adds ZERO rows (IF's "backdrop SILENTLY VOIDs" claim does not reproduce; their voided damage was the popup merely being abandoned, the trap being the unconditional `click to dismiss` hint + Done-only affordance). Post-improve staged face: hint suppressed, buttons `["Done","Cancel attack"]` — `Cancel attack` (`.popup-close-btn` in `popup-close-row`, `AttackResultPopup.jsx`) logs `{type:'automation', automationType:'attack_cancelled', reason:'staged_damage_cancelled'}` naming attack+target and closes with zero dice-roll-done/hp_change rows; nothing-staged popups keep the old chrome (footer Done, hint, no Cancel). A card-wide popup probe MUST enumerate all three families (`.sp-overlay, .popup-modal`) or reads "no popup" while one is mounted. Results popups ("X — Results … Close") can linger behind later ones — drain `.popup-modal` first when adjudicating a fresh press.

- **Synthetic travel-event + enforcement seeding law (live 2026-11-11 explore run KW):** the event dialog can be armed WITHOUT advance-hunting: POST change-data `travel-pendingEvent` = `{type:'combat', title, description, terrain, encounter:{monsters:[{index,name,qty}], difficultyLabel, totalXP}}` — a mounted tab adopts it via the per-key SSE echo and `.event-dialog-overlay` renders immediately. The `terrain` field is MANDATORY in a synthetic seed: `EventDialog` prints `event.terrain` verbatim (a blank "Terrain: " row on a seeded event is fixture shorthand, not an app bug — `generateRandomEvent` always stamps it for natural events). The dialog's `.event-btn-accept` runs the FULL accept chain. Enforcement arm shape: `POST change-data enforcement {mode:'on', <subFlags>}` — the validator 400s without `mode` ("enforcement.mode must be 'off' or 'on'"); the stored bucket reads back FULLY MERGED (all 12 default flags) — branch on the sub-flag you armed. Enforcement-ON combat-accept face (autoEncounters:true): exactly three rows — `event_accept` (battleMap label), `session_phase` exploration→encounter changedBy `auto-encounter`, `automation/auto_encounter_started` (roster + tracker count + battle map name); engine rolls NPC initiative ONLY (PC initiative cells stay blank until rolled), `activeCreatureName` = top NPC, `sessionState.phase`='encounter', `__map__` moves to the reused "Travel Encounter Stage", `travel-pendingEvent` nulls, travel-mode returns to 'planning'.
- **Throwaway-seeded lane laws (re-lived 2026-11-11 run LZ; all three post-KW improves verified live):** quick-create buttons are `New Arena Battle`/`New Starter Adventure`; modal default name is date-stamped `Arena-YYYYMMDD`/`Starter-YYYYMMDD`, input `input[placeholder="Enter campaign name"]`, submit is the LAST `Create` button — under `CAMPAIGN_LOCK` the off-allowlist default 403s now render INLINE in the modal with the typed name preserved (verified live 2026-11-11, 10d3866ad; the LZ-era full-page "Campaign locked to …" + `Reload Page` screen is RETIRED for quick-create — it survives only for load/select failures; see the §Lock-probe bullet for the verbatim denial copy); fill the canonical `qa-arena`/`qa-starter` name for a success-path create. Starter header carries a `STARTER ADVENTURE` badge and the zero-char wizard auto-opens — its footer has NO `Cancel` button (LZ walked all buttons: only `×` + the step tabs `1 Ruleset`…`9 Feature Choices`, and `×` on an untouched form closes with zero native confirm); its backdrop INTERCEPTS checklist/sidebar clicks (LZ's deep-link click timed out on it) — click `×` first or `page.goto('/')`, then drive steps; `getByText(name,{exact:true})` misses campaign rows — filter containers by `hasText` and `.last().click()`. FirstSessionChecklist steps deep-link OUTDOOR-view targets directly ("Meet the folk" → Settlements row), the regenerate step carries `data-testid="starter-step-regenerate"`; the checklist re-derives generated NAMES live (post-regenerate it read the new dungeon without reload). Regenerate: role=dialog `Yes, Regenerate` → new dungeon slug replaces the old file (old GONE), `starter-seed.json` layer +1, `regeneratedAt` stamped, `entities.pastTitles` keeps the retired title, countryside portal POI `linkedMap` + main-quest description + settlement `rumors` all re-pointed; the campaign log gains EXACTLY ONE `adventure_regenerated` audit row naming retired title(s) → new + layer ("Adventure regenerated: Old → New (adventure layer N)"; first-gen seeds log honest "initial generation — <new>" wording) — written by the client orchestrator `regenerateAdventure()` in `src/services/campaign/seededCampaign.js` via the shared POST /log lane (there is NO server-side regenerate route; corrected in place 2026-11-11 improve adventure-regen-log-row, which retired the old "zero rows" face). Encounters deep state: saved waves live in `data/encounters.json`; the starter checklist's "Join <wave>" step fires `requestEncounterLoad()` (`src/services/ui/encounterLoadSignal.js`: single-shot pending flag consumed on the builder's next mount + sync window event when already mounted — improve 2026-11-11) so the builder's Load modal opens AUTOMATICALLY with rows visible (manual `Load` button still the sidebar lane); rows live in `.encounter-modal-load > .encounter-list`, click target is the `Load "<name>"` aria button — a bare `text=Dungeon Wave 1` click hits the label and silently no-ops. Typed-name Admin delete (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-11 improve delete-typed-name-gate, owner decision): the confirm button (`Delete "<name>"`) now renders natively DISABLED until the typed input exactly matches (raw `===`, no trim, case-sensitive — identical to the handler guard, so button and handler never disagree); the handler guard + inline copy "Campaign name did not match. Deletion cancelled." remain as an unreachable belt-and-braces fallback, so a live probe can no longer surface that copy; judge the gate from `isDisabled()` again (the old "never judge from .disabled" warning is VOID). Shared `DeleteConfirmModal` gained an optional `confirmDisabled` prop (omitted consumers byte-identical). success → reload onto selection with one-shot banner "<name> deleted successfully."; API-lane delete `DELETE /api/campaigns/<name>` 200-equivalent, folder gone, no re-materialization. Arena throwaway: sidebar prunes to Campaigns/Encounters/Initiative/Log/Maps/Music/NPCs/Rules/Admin (campaign-only views hidden), 2 seeded halls + 3-encounter pool via Load.
- **AI Dress structural-smuggle + throwaway-server proof laws (live 2026-11-14 fix ai-dress-wall-as-item):** (a) SNAKE_CASE SMUGGLE PITFALL: `\b` counts `_` as a word char — the model re-smuggled `type:'stone_wall'` past `\bwall\b` mid-fix; structural-noun scans must use `(?<![A-Za-z0-9])…(?![A-Za-z0-9])` boundaries (camelCase sprites like `arrowSlitWall` stay correctly glued). Server lane now refuses structural-noun item TYPES fail-closed (all-or-nothing error lane; registered prop sprites door/stairs/arrowSlitWall stay placeable); a success audit row with `attempts: 2` beside a clean preview = fail-closed refusal + repair fired (refusal detail is NOT stored on the success row). (b) SERVER-LANE LIVE PROOF WITHOUT TOUCHING THE OWNED PAIR: throwaway `PORT=8123 CAMPAIGN_LOCK=… nohup node server.js & disown` → ride the real POST through it → kill only your own PID. (c) MID-EDIT FULL-SUITE TRAP: a `test:run` started while sources are still being edited can collect stale module state and report phantom failures — re-run once files are stable before believing them.
- **browser_close in an isolated context can take the WHOLE browser's tabs with it (live 2026-11-11 run KW):** closing the `isolatedContext:'lan'` tab collapsed the default-context GM tab too (tabs list returned a lone about:blank). Not an app bug — a harness artifact: after closing any isolated tab, re-probe `browser_tabs list` and re-navigate the ride tab before trusting it, and finish stateful GM legs BEFORE opening/closing visitor tabs where cheap.

## 3. Rule data is truth
- Verify from `public/data/monsters.json` (+ `/data/2024/` twins) FIRST; the bug file's "Expected" may itself be wrong. EB join names must match monsters.json exactly; EB search top hit may be wrong species — read autocomplete rows; first join after autocomplete can join WRONG monster (verify cs after every join).
- monsters.json edits are NOT visible in a loaded tab even after reload — browser HTTP cache serves stale /data. Proof: `fetch(cache:'reload')`; fix display = close card × → Campaigns → re-select → reopen (the old `combat-ui-viewingMonster` key-DELETE step is DEAD: the `*-viewingMonster` modal-open keys were retired 2026-11-07 by 579aca8d2 — modal state is per-client useState in Initiative/Map/EncounterBuilder and the stat block never syncs; TV screens never show it); EB-joined combatants keep stale snapshots — remove+re-join.
- monsters.json prose anchors NOT monster-unique (dragons share Pounce/Rend text; "Stone spikes sprout" ×4; gynosphinx byte-identical block; soft-hyphen U+00AD embedded) — anchor edits on monster-unique neighbors + indentation, JSON.parse + full `git diff` after.
- OCR typo family "1dlO"→"1d10" (Silver, Ancient Bronze, yochlol, lizardfolk): `canRollExpression("1dlO")=false` → MV-12 chip suppression; author explicit dice on fix.
- 2024 classes.json Devious Strikes options carry `saveAbility:"DEX"` on CON-save options (Poison/Daze/KO) — NOT a mislabel: `buildSaveDc` reads `saveAbility` as the DC-SOURCE ability and 2024 Cunning Strike DC = 8+PB+DEX by RAW for every option (save TYPE stays CON via `saveType`); flipping it breaks the primary DC (proved 2026-11-07, DS-Poison repeat-save fix).
- D20-conversion features diverge by ruleset in canonical data: 2024 Stroke of Luck = "turn the roll into a **20**" (attack conversions ARE natural-20 crits) while the 5e twin = "turn the **miss into a hit**" (never a 20, never crits); Boon of Combat Prowess hits without becoming a 20 in both (non-crit by design). Gate conversion-crit logic on `playerStats.rules` + `rollType==='attack'`. FT-007 conversion latch folds into the roll CONTEXT, so a spent-boon roll ships `popupHtml` WITHOUT `autoRerollForAttack` (button absent — tests pinning the locked face model flag-off, not a hidden button; live 2026-11-06).

## 4. Live monster-card rig
- **Attack-result popup blocks the board (live CF 2026-11-13):** a monster-card dice-chip press leaves `.popup-overlay` mounted showing the roll face (`…click to dismiss Done`); it INTERCEPTS every subsequent click in the Initiative view — a `force:true` press of the Clear button silently lands on the popup layer and the confirm dialog never arms (no error). Dismiss with `page.locator(".popup-overlay button", {hasText:"Done"}).click()` (or click the popup body) before any board control; audit `!!document.querySelector(".popup-overlay")` after any roll-driven ride.
- EB: Encounters page → search → checkbox → "Join Encounter" (only path into initiative; table resets after; joins mid-round keep `.mc-overlay` open; join can auto-land monster at cs idx 0). EB search top hit may be wrong species — read autocomplete rows; first join after autocomplete can join WRONG monster (verify cs after every join). EXACT locator law (live 2026-11-09 ER): every bestiary checkbox carries `aria-label="Select <exact name>"` — click `input.monster-checkbox[aria-label="Select Goblin"]` instead of the EQ-era name+CR concat index-matching dance (`Goblin0.25` defeating `^Goblin\b` is obsolete; `Select Hobgoblin` can never collide with `Select Goblin`). Join auto-navigates to the Initiative VIEW (`App.jsx onJoinEncounter -> setActiveView('initiative')`) — a side effect with teeth (see Initiative mount law below). Initiative-page "+ NPC" = BARE statless NPC, not a real join. REST witness for a join: cs lives at change-data key `combatSummary` (`GET /api/campaigns/:c/change-data`) — NOT `__initiative__` (gate stamp, reads null there); joined creature carries `monsterIndex` + `size`/`armorClass`/`maxHitPoints` for name/size verification; pre-join baseline can read `{}` until the 2s debounce flushes — poll the aggregate key (live 2026-11-08 improve eb-monster-list-perf). Bestiary first paint is BOUNDED since that improve: empty/un-filtered search renders ≤50 `input.monster-checkbox` rows + `.monster-table-cap-notice` ("Showing first N of M — type to narrow"); any active filter/search renders the full match set (census probes: un-narrowed 50/636). The cap BITES JOIN LOCATORS too (live 2026-11-10 improve gc-oa-chip-leading-space): `input.monster-checkbox[aria-label="Select Goblin"]` is simply ABSENT on an un-narrowed table (alphabetical first-50) — always type into `input[aria-label="Search monsters"]` before the exact-aria checkbox click.
- EB "one-tab monster attack" live rig (live 2026-11-13 improve victory-banner-zero-attack-clear): `Select <monster>` checkbox → `Join Encounter` (auto-navigates to Initiative, auto-rolls initiative) → arm via row `<select aria-label="<Name> target">` → click avatar `img[alt="<Name>"]` → click the action's `.mc-dice-link` → the result popup `[data-testid="popup-overlay"]` needs an explicit `Done` AND the card its `aria-label="Close"` button (Escape dismisses NEITHER) before `button.clear-button` is clickable again.
- **EB Load-picker DOM law (live-proved 2026-11-13 improve arena-load-affordance on test-campaign pool rows POSTed through the identical lane the arena seeder uses):** `button[aria-label="Load encounter"]` opens the picker on its OWN overlay family — `.encounter-modal-overlay` (role=presentation) + `.encounter-modal`, header `Load Encounter` — with ZERO `[role=dialog]`, zero `.modal-overlay`/`.modal-shell-overlay`/`.ct-modal-overlay`, no dropdown/select: generic dialog/dropdown probes read a mounted picker as a "dead affordance" (the EH backlog premise was exactly this locator blind; the lane itself is live, no code change shipped). Rows = `li.encounter-list-item`, name on `.encounter-list-name`; row buttons carry exact aria-labels `Load "<name>"`/`Rename "<name>"`/`Delete "<name>"` — the escaped-quote selector mangles through the MCP runner, build `button[aria-label='Load "…"']` inside evaluate with String.fromCharCode(39/34). Row Load click (evaluate `.click()` works on these buttons) → GET `/encounters/<name>` → modal closes, title/description/Selected Monsters hydrate, `Encounter loaded` toast; an EXTRA `PUT /encounters/<name>` riding right after a load is the effective-XP reconcile write-back BY DESIGN, not harness damage. Save round-trips: a saved row appears in the picker; the toolbar button flips `Save`→`Update` once a row is loaded and Update fires the NATIVE confirm `Overwrite encounter "<name>"? This replaces the saved encounter.` (handle via browser_handle_dialog in the same ride).
- Card: avatar `img.avatar-image.click()` opens `.mc-overlay`; monster alt=name, PC alt=initials; scope card by `:has(input[aria-label="<Name> current HP"])`, never hasText (matches every target-dropdown). Attack chips = `span.mc-dice-link` (text often ONLY "+N" or "3d6" — locate via `.mc-action` container `<strong>` startsWith match, then inner link). Spell chips `.mc-dice-link-spell` (leading space in textContent — startsWith/nth). Legendary gated chip `.mc-dice-link-legendary`; lair chip `span.mc-dice-link-lair[role=button]`. Locate links by row-text + `page.mouse.click` at FRESH boundingClientRect (scrollIntoView first — stale rects silently miss / hit backdrop closing the card). CHIP ANCHOR (live 2026-11-08 improve monster-card-chip-data-kind): every monster-card dice-link chip carries `data-kind` (attack/save/skill/spell/legendary/lair/ability/initiative/damage/reaction/aura/summon/selfbuff/grantreaction/shapeshift/zone/advisory) — anchor automation by `[data-kind=…]`, never by `.mc-dice-link` index. Census myth fixed: plain-`.mc-dice-link` indices 0–5 on a monster card are the SIX ability-check cells (`div.mc-ability-mod`, handleAbilityCheck), NOT saves; save chips render ONLY when `saving_throws` is authored (Goblin/Bugbear/Hobgoblin author none → 0 save chips) — the EO "0–5 saves" claim was ability cells. EB "Join Encounter" mounts only AFTER a checkbox is ticked (wait_for before ticking always times out); `browser_evaluate` sandbox has no `setTimeout` — bounded waits go in `browser_run_code_unsafe` + `page.waitForTimeout`.
- **Monster-card DOM markers (live 2026-11-08 EQ; aria-label lane shipped 2026-11-08 improve monster-card-name-a11y):** BESTIARY monster cards have NO `[data-kind="initiative"]` CHIP — initiative is a bare card input (placeholder `Init`); manual initiative edits write cs but log NOTHING, so post-rename/attribution proofs must ride a `[data-kind="attack"]` chip (arm the card's target-select first) or a join initiative roll (live 2026-11-09 improve eb-series-renumber). PC names render `.creature-name span`; BESTIARY-joined monster cards render the name as `.creature-name > .monster-autocomplete input` and the GM-editable lane now carries `aria-label="<Name> name"` — ONE stable census selector for every initiative card type (PC spans, GM editable monster/NPC inputs, visitor read-only spans, polymorph form spans) is `.creature-name span, .creature-name input[aria-label$=" name"]` (bare `.creature-name span` alone still silently misses every GM-tab monster; LAN tabs render those rows as spans). Stable anchors: card = `.creature-card:has(img.avatar-image[alt="Goblin"])` (alt = exact bestiary name); +NPC placeholder row on the GM tab = `.creature-card:has(.creature-name input[aria-label="NPC 1 name"])`; attack chips = `[data-kind=attack]`; OA chip = `.mc-overlay [data-kind="oa"]` whose textContent is BYTE-EXACT "Opportunity Attack on <mover>" (normalized at source 2026-11-10 improve gc-oa-chip-leading-space — the icon-adjacent JSX text node became ONE expression child, icon gap stays CSS-owned via `.mc-dice-link i` margin; `^Opportunity Attack on <mover>$` strict match now passes — spell chips' leading space is unchanged, §67 trim law still stands for THEM); delegation picker = `[data-testid=control-select]` (aria "Assign who controls <Name>"; stamps cs row `controller`, visitor face renders NO control-select, initiative is a PLAIN-TEXT span `[data-testid="initiative-value"]` (e71405e8f — the permanently-disabled input is retired; `—` placeholder when unrolled, value live-adopts GM edits via the combatSummary echo, holds in TV mode too, verified live 2026-11-11; AWAY rows keep the disabled-input face with its rejoin tooltip), target-select ENABLED). The `mc-overlay` card SURVIVES popup drains — arm once, then chip→Done→chip loops without re-opening (a "blocked by mc-overlay intercepts" avatar click means the card is already open); monster HIT popups are 1-stage (Done applies + closes). EB "Join Encounter" PRESERVES away flags + parked-last placement through its full-replace merge.
- Arm target via initiative-card `[data-testid="target-select"]` Playwright `selectOption` BEFORE clicking chips — unarmed/ungated rolls with `targetName:null` roll forever, no "vs AC", no damage. After re-join arm on the ATTACKER's OWN card select — generic first-empty-select arms wrong combatant → null-target rolls, popup lacks Done, center-click abandons attack (MA-0320). EB melee auto-miss >8 ft — place tokens adjacent (manual mouse.down/move/up; cell px `p.x=gx*40+20` matrixTransform).
- Popups: HIT popup "Done" = `button.dice-roll-reroll-btn` (has child `<i>` — leaf-text search MISSES it; Done applies damage; HIT popups are Done-only — no bg/click dismissal (popup.test.jsx enforces), reroll loops must click Done which APPLIES damage — top up victim HP via scoped PATCH between rolls). Sheet-lane HIT popups are TWO-STAGE: one root/Done click only advances to the stage-2 damage popup and the still-open overlay intercepts the next row click (`rowclickfail`) — drain loops must repeat Done clicks (≤6) per roll; MISS popups close with one root click (live 2026-11-06); plain-damage MISS popup (no reroll stage) renders "Done" as `button.popup-close-btn` instead (KB chip, live 2026-11-05) — probe both. Buttonless second-stage `popup-overlay` survives and intercepts the next chip click — flush via its own click `[data-testid="popup-overlay"] el.click()`. Blocked Playwright clicks RETRY and can leave several stacked overlays; one click per pass may re-surface the next — flush loop: click root AND all descendants of every `[data-testid="popup-overlay"]` until the query is empty (live 2026-11-06). NEVER `element.remove()` any overlay — corrupts React until reload. `offsetParent===null` is FALSE for fixed overlays — poll computedStyle/getBoundingClientRect + count log deltas. Results `.sp-overlay` can linger ~2min intercepting — poll unfiltered, own Close. AoE-stack law (live 2026-11-11 run IE): after a monster AoE confirm the picker `.sp-overlay` STAYS MOUNTED with its button disabled (`<action> (N)(dis)`) and the real save prompt stacks BEHIND it — a `.sp-modal`/`.sp-overlay` FIRST-MATCH probe reads the dead picker (false "no Roll Save leaf" twice; every click before the throw already LANDED — judge by log delta); drain loops must enumerate ALL `.sp-overlay`s per pass and match the leaf button per overlay, and the save result reuses the same `.sp-modal` (Roll Save → Done stage in-place).
- Feature rows with a queued save can emit TWO channels at once (Stunning Strike live 2026-11-06): an `automation_info` popup ("Target X must make a CON saving throw (DC N). / Done") AND the `.sp-overlay` prompt — close the popup's Done to reach Roll Save/Dismiss.
- Save prompts `.sp-modal` "Saving Throw Required (n of N)": Roll Save / Next Save / Done; queues behind result popups; NPC saves often AUTO-ROLL inline (no prompt). One roll per prompt; success rigs need high-stat victims, ≤3 loops. STABLE ANCHOR (shipped 2026-11-11 improve active-save-prompt-testid): the ACTIVE prompt's `.sp-modal` carries `data-testid="active-save-prompt"` — single-active (queue front only), both stages (Roll Save → Done) render through the SAME element, never present on picker/results/evasion overlays, zero matches with no prompt mounted; `[data-testid=active-save-prompt]` RETIRES the `.sp-modal` first-match archaeology and per-overlay leaf enumeration for the prompt stage. A throw in probe code still queues the cast's prompt — judge casts by log delta. Leading-Evasion chooser re-stacks above the prompt ONCE PER SUBSEQUENT SAVE (not just the first) — drain loops must re-check `.sp-overlay--evasion` every iteration (live 2026-11-05). AoE picker confirm now DISABLES at dispatch (SaveAttackAoeModal `dispatched` latch, 2026-11-05) — the CY-era "live confirm until Results closes" double-cast hazard window is closed on the picker lane.
- AoE monster-card SAVE chip picker (live 2026-11-10 PFEG-fix run): pressing a radius-row save chip (Performer Maestro Beguiling Song) opens a `.sp-overlay` picker layer ("<N>-ft Radius … Select creatures", creature checkboxes as `<label>Name(<pct>% HP)`, confirm button labeled `<action> (n)` when ≥1 ticked, decline = `Skip`); after confirm the picker layer STAYS STACKED BELOW the target's Roll Save prompt — `document.querySelector('.sp-overlay')` then grabs the stale picker, so prompt waiters/drain loops must iterate ALL `.sp-overlay` nodes, and the picker's disabled confirm must never be re-clicked. STACK DEPTH (live 2026-11-11 improve active-save-prompt-testid): after Done a third overlay `"<action> — Results"` `.sp-overlay` LINGERS and silently intercepts the next chip click — drain loops must also press its `Close` leaf before the next chip; and Maestro's SECOND save chip (`save|DC 15 Wisdom`) re-opens the SAME AoE picker, so a single-target sanity leg needs a different monster (Succubus Charm).
- PFEG multi-condition ward live rig (canonical faces, live 2026-11-10; seed-corrected in place run HD): seed target bucket PRE-select via full-store POST `{activeBuffs:[{name:'Protection from Evil and Good', effect:'protection_from_evil_and_good'} (BOTH keys REQUIRED — the gate `isProtectionFromEvilAndGoodActive` reads `b.name===SPELL_NAME && b.effect===…`, a row without `name` leaves the ward honestly INERT and all conditions "legitimately" land — false-bug trap), {effect:'warding_bond', saveBonus:-19 (guaranteed-fail dial, folds ALL saves; -30 = auto-fail face incl. nat 20)}], protectionFromEvilAndGoodWardedTypes:[Aberration,Celestial,Elemental,Fey,Fiend,Undead]}`; multi-condition Fiend row = **Lamia Corrupting Touch** (WIS DC 13; live prompt carries saveConditions `['charmed','cursed','poisoned']` — the row's "cursed for 1 hour" clause extracts as its own condition) → post-fix activeConditions `['cursed','poisoned']` + ONE `automation blocked` per warded condition + `condition applied` for the survivors; single-condition warded face = Succubus Charm (Fiend, `save:DC 15 Charisma` chip); non-warded humanoid face = Performer Maestro (Humanoid — charmed lands WITH the ward active). CAVEAT (live 2026-11-11 run IE): the Maestro carrier is Beguiling Song (`save` chip `3d10 + 4`, opens the AoE picker — tick one label, confirm; the dispatched picker stays mounted disabled BEHIND the save prompt) and its charmed face: the IE-era self-strip (`condition removed … took damage (Friends)` — the row's own save damage stripping the just-granted Charmed, filed as `bug-beguiling-song-own-damage-strips-its-charm`) was FIXED 2026-11-11 (58f6e86c3, now in resolved/; LIVE-VERIFIED 3/3 run IF 2026-11-11: the humanoid Charmed LANDS and persists — conds `['charmed']` + badge + log order hp_change→roll→condition — a LATER separate damage source still strips it, and the strip purges the `activeConditionMeta` row in the merged write, no orphan); the GATE face is still best judged by the absence of the `automation blocked` row. Cheapest IF rig for the persistence legs: pre-select full-store POST `{activeBuffs:[{effect:'warding_bond',saveBonus:-30}], activeConditions:[], currentHitPoints:143, hitPoints:143}` (HP keys REQUIRED or the damage lane console.errors `currentHitPoints not found` with zero hp_change) + EB-join Performer Maestro & Goblin + the §"popup families" Done-gate law below. Note: the hag-attribution in rules prose is wrong for THIS data — "Charmed and Poisoned" lives on the Lamia (monsters.json:40929), Green Hag carries no such row.
- **Lion MEASURED-grid exempt face (the GC-owed leg, run HD CLOSED live):** active indoor map + tokens via `placedItems` `{type:'npc',name:'Goblin',gridX,gridY}` for the attacker and `players[]` for PC bearers/targets; a target carrying its own `optionName:'Lion'` stamp stays `mode:"normal"`/1 die/zero aura rows even with tokens placed (pre-loop exemption runs BEFORE measurement); the adjacent un-stamped control target takes the dis AND the `lion_aura_disadvantage` row switches copy to MEASURED prose "…within 5 feet of raging ElderPaladin (Power of the Wilds — Lion)." — the gridless "proximity unmeasured (gridless) — GM-enforced" tail is ABSENT on this face (copy discriminator for measured vs lenient). EB-joined monsters do NOT auto-token: the 3D/map header "0 npcs" with a joined monster on the board is expected (token = separate placedItems entry).
- Initiative "Next →" walks the CREATURES-ARRAY — the mount merge RE-SORTS the array it writes (`initiativeService.js:264` → `sortCreaturesForBoard`: present-first, initiative DESC, blank=−1, ties name-asc), so boards walk initiative-desc regardless of the POSTed order. DOM-vs-walker divergence CLOSED 2026-11-09 (improve board-order-walk-parity): the old FW fingerprint (settled tab adopting a full-store cs POST rendered POST order in the DOM while mirrors walked DESC) was two ingress seams writing two orders — NOW `handleCombatSummaryChange` (sseHandlers.js) also runs `sortCreaturesForBoard` at adoption, so EVERY ingress writes board-sorted order and the card row renders exactly the walk sequence (pointer sweeps left-to-right incl. wrap; pinned by `sseHandlers.board-order.test.js` + `Initiative.board-order.test.jsx`). Seed init values to place rows adjacent in DESC order when walk sequence matters — POST order controls neither display nor walk anymore — top-level `activeCreatureName` is truth (cs mirror lags ~10s) — poll between every click. EB Join mid-round advances activeCreatureName onto the board head (never onto an AWAY row — join re-anchors away-active off the board, live EQ). "Clear" (9ada04af2-era law, copy-pinned): removes joined monsters, resets initiative/round, KEEPS and re-seeds bare +NPC placeholder rows, and DROPS split-party away flags (live EQ 2026-11-08: NPC 1 survived a Clear, HexWarlock/FeyRanger came back present). Banner ride caveat: a carried placeholder NPC is a FOE ⇒ auto-hides the fresh victory banner. `getCurrentCombatRound` MUST thread campaignName (omitted → round pinned 1). Reaching a specific ACTIVE creature via "Next →": budget up to N presses and poll `combatSummary.activeCreatureName` between every press (GB 2026-11-10: an EB board walk stepped Wolf→AasimarTest past the Goblin row once — never assume one press = one expected row).
- Recipe (live 2026-11-09 fix run): ride a Thief's-Reflexes `(Second Turn)` row WITHOUT the EF Thief disk-swap — POST full-store `combatSummary` `[holder, {name:'<H> (Second Turn)', secondTurn:true, holderName:'<H>'}]` before the Initiative mount; the remount merge law keeps the synthetic twin while `holderName` is a campaign character. Sharpened (2026-11-09 improve st-rejoin-title): adding `away:true` to the twin row in that seed renders a REJOIN-state ST card on first mount (greyed, Rejoin toggle showing) — no away-click round-trip to ride rejoin-copy lanes; the mount merge re-adds the other campaign characters around the seed, and a live away→present toggle on a normal PC renders the non-ST rejoin face in the same pass.
- ST-row lane authority (live 2026-11-09 FU, post-c5883e4a4): EffectAdder ADD opens (all 3 tabs) remap to `holderName` — modal titles the holder, writes+audits land on the holder, synthetic bucket stays GET-absent; display resolves the holder's HP/conditions AND — since 2026-11-09 improve st-card-badge-parity — te + concentration badges too (`CreatureCard.jsx` `effectSubjectName = isSecondTurnEntry && holderName ? holderName : name` feeds the te filter, ConditionEffectBadges `creatureName`, and the concentration badge/×; × on the ST card acts on and audits the HOLDER, both cards show the same badges). FIX-VERIFIED 2026-11-09 FV (fixes 583d078ff + 930393775 both PASS: × on the ST card clears the HOLDER's condition + audits the holder; allies editor titles the holder, EXCLUDES second-turn rows from candidates, writes holder `selectedAllies` + holder audit — phantom bucket gained zero keys; concentration badge on the ST card also rides the holder correctly — click rolled +11 with holder CON bonus + aura and audited `EvasiveFighter`). CLICK LANE FIXED 2026-11-09 (d55d4160a, `bug-secondturn-badge-click-synthetic-name`): the remap lives at the `createRollConditionSaveHandler.js` `handleRollConditionSave` chokepoint (holder row feeds roll/remove/audit, mirroring the × precedent — the prop still passes `creature.name`), pinned by `createRollConditionSaveHandler.second-turn.test.js`; face proof: ST-click +11 (holder CON + aura), holder audit, holder cleared, synthetic bucket GET-absent; holder-card click byte-unchanged. Delegation control-select is absent on ST cards and target lists exclude them (guards held). Modal-header provenance hint (2026-11-09 improve st-card-modal-name-mismatch): twin-opened modals append display-only " (second turn)" after the holder name — EffectAdder header via `titleSuffix` from the `openedFromSecondTurn` flag stamped at open (rides the synced `combat-ui-effectAdderTarget` key, so SSE-rehydrated opens keep the hint), allies editor via local `fromSecondTurn` state; holder/normal opens are byte-identical, and a twin without `holderName` gets NO suffix (its raw name already carries the marker).
- **Badge click lane truths (live 2026-11-09 FV; dc-gate CLOSED dad80f67b):** a condition added with the DC input BLANK used to get `dc` coerced to **0** by `buildRuntimeConditions` (displayCreatureUtils.js:16) with `ConditionBadgeRow` wiring the click ungated, so clicking ALWAYS printed “SAVE SUCCESSFUL (DC 0)” and removed the condition (players too — `canRoll = player||localhost`) — a silent rules bypass, now fixed per owner ruling “gate the click — no DC, no roll”: blank-DC badges render as inert `span.creature-badge` (description tooltip only, zero popup/log/change-data on click, pinned by `CreatureCard.noDcBadgeClick.test.jsx`); removal is via × or consumer lanes only. The old dc=0 auto-success face is NO LONGER a deterministic-SUCCESS rig — for guaranteed save-success test faces use the §7 ±19 `warding_bond` dial with a real DC (DC-bearing badges remain `button` and roll honestly, incl. the ST holder-remap).
- **TV up-next ST-seam laws (live 2026-11-09 FV, useTvPresentation.js:114-135):** selector family `.tv-next-up` + `.tv-next-up-label` + `.tv-next-chip`; with TWO `(Second Turn)` twins adjacent in display order (e.g. inits 10 & 11) and a normal active row before them, the strip skips BOTH consecutive ineligible rows and yields the 4 next eligible creatures; with an ST row ACTIVE, the strip never lists it or its twin (eligible-list excludes all secondTurn, ST-anchor cap min(4,len) behaves identically). Curl-POSTing `combatSummary` (full store, `{value}`) onto a SETTLED mounted Initiative tab is adopted live (echo law; the appended twin appeared without remount) — and adoption is a REPLACEMENT: rows the POST omits are evicted from the live board too (a 2-row twin-seed dropped an unlisted ElderPaladin card without remount; a fresh view-navigation re-merges all campaign characters back), so seed via full-store POST only when you intend the whole board. `activeCreatureName` is a top-level key POSTable as `{value:'<Name>'}` and drives the TV anchor immediately.
- **Enforcement/turn-lock ride (live 2026-11-09 FV2; ST-seam hole CLOSED c076dfdf1):** arm with `POST /:c/enforcement {value:{mode:'on',turnLock:true}}` — the route merges and GETs back the FULL default blob (all L5 flags present, false); gate reads `mode==='on'` AND the flag. Denial witnesses: popup `<reason> Done` + top-level log row `{type:'enforcement_denied',creature,bucket,source,reason}` + ZERO roll rows (cleanest no-side-effect refusal probe). `activeCreatureName` truth for the gate = the combatSummary mirror's field (assertCanAct `readActiveCreature` — renamed from `readActiveCreatureName` by the fix; it resolves a `secondTurn:true` row's seam once and exposes `{name, holderName}`), so flip the acting turn by re-POSTing the WHOLE cs `{value:{…,activeCreatureName:'<other>'}}` — a settled sheet tab adopts it live, no reload (see the full-store REPLACEMENT law — omitting rows evicts them). Face-switch rig for the four ST-lock faces (holder-under-twin allow / stranger-under-twin deny / holder-normal / stranger-normal) is pure full-store re-POST, no reloads; the denial popup drains with the §64 root+descendants `[data-testid=popup-overlay]` click loop. Re-bake pitfall (live GC 2026-11-10): a SHEET mount after your flip re-bakes `activeCreatureName` back to the tab's previously-selected character via its §5 full-store snapshot — re-POST + GET-confirm the flip after EVERY sheet open before trusting which name the denial copy prints. Post-fix law: holder AND `companionOf=holder` rows act while their twin is active (action + bonus buckets); every other creature still denies with copy truthfully naming the active twin row. Sheet attack roll face = first `.attacks div.clickable` whose text starts with `+` (hit chip; `.base-action-info` spans are informational text, clicks there are inert).
- Away→Rejoin initiative law (updated in place 2026-11-09 improve st-rejoin-initiative-anchor): NON-second-turn player rows still re-roll to the end-of-round slot on every rejoin (PC drift like `''→3` remains app law — never trust a row's initiative across an away round-trip); second-turn twins instead RE-ANCHOR to the holder's CURRENT initiative − 10 (`creaturePresence.js` `secondTurnAnchorInitiative`, grant-law arithmetic; holder absent/blank ⇒ end-of-round fallback). Audit copy distinguishes them (`re-anchored to <holder>'s second-turn slot (initiative N)` vs `(end of the round)`). MOCK CAVEAT: tests factory-mocking `thiefsReflexesService` that exercise a rejoin must spread `await importOriginal()` (or supply `isSecondTurnEntry`) — `creaturePresence` now imports the predicate. SHIPPED-COPY LIVE-VERIFIED (FW 2026-11-09): the ST card's away→Rejoin TOOLTIP now reads "Rejoin <ST> to the encounter (initiative re-anchors to <holder>'s second-turn slot)" (3f2acaa9f — the FV-era stale "(end of the round)" copy is gone); the twin-opened allies modal titles "Allies of <holder> (second turn)" (219985867) with the ST row excluded from candidates.
- **Economy-on ST matrix in ONE load (verified 2026-11-09 fix-verify 4dc24883b + full-matrix live re-ride FX):** clear → `POST /:c/enforcement {mode:'on',turnLock:true,actionEconomy:true}` → select campaign → Initiative settle (~4s, 13-PC merge) → full-store cs re-POST with holder init 20 / twin init 19 / next init 15 (DESC order so walk adjacency is holder→twin→next) → top-level `POST /activeCreatureName {value:'<holder>'}`; denial/roll popups drain with the §64 root+descendants loop **plus the .sp-overlay rider stack (§255 FX face) — a popup-only drain strands stage-2 and FALSE-DIAGNOSES "damage dropped"**. FX closed every face live: spent-holder pre-seam DENIES (control); holder→twin seam RE-ARMS (actionsUsed 1→0, movement 30→0 from a REAL drag charge, hasMoved false) and spent-holder then ROLLS with the full attack→rider→damage→hp_change chain honest (crit 1d10*2+3+6 HWM = −29, non-crit −11), zero `enforcement_denied`; re-arm ≠ bypass (post-seam second press denies honestly — 2nd row); twin→X seam CLOSES the holder (turnActive:false, spent face) and opens next fresh; Prev→twin rewind re-opens the HOLDER key with NO refund and zero synthetic keys; `movementBudget` ON: token drag while twin-active charges `movementUsedFeet` on the HOLDER key, over-budget drag CLAMPS at the budget edge AND fires the `.map-toast` clamp notice (toast-copy law updated in-place 2026-11-09 improve clamp-toast-copy, live-verified: partial clamp = "X moved only N ft of M requested."; this move charged the ENTIRE per-turn budget = "X's movement used up at S ft." (total-at-cap, S = speedFeet); the old ambiguous "snapped back to N ft of movement" frame is RETIRED — a bare toast number never reads as remaining; log rows unchanged by that improve) (corrected in-place 2026-11-09 improve run: the FX "no toast on the clamp leg" claim was a probe artifact — `usePlayerDragging.js:339` toasts every `budget.snapped` drop, shipped 37dc2c313; the toast dies at 2500 ms so arm a `MutationObserver` on `.map-toast` BEFORE the drag, never query after mouse.up; SHARPENED FY 2026-11-09: a SECOND enforcement advisory REPLACES the mounted sticky toast's textContent IN PLACE — addedNodes never fires again, so observe childList AND live-query `.map-toast` after each leg; a full-block drag (0 ft remaining) honestly toasts "X cannot move — no movement left this turn." (toast copy shipped a3d5d9947 — diverges from the log row BY DESIGN: the log keeps "(0 ft this move)" bytes on every leg) + logs `enforcement_denied` bucket:movement "Out of movement — snapped to the remaining budget (0 ft this move)."); repeat-save te on the holder prompts exactly ONCE at the holder seam ("save-repeat", c:te.source, fail keeps te+cond byte) and ZERO at the twin seam (skips by design; producer has no round latch — fires every genuine turn-end seam). Two-client: LAN-origin tab mounted on Initiative during twin walks = zero `*Second Turn*` cd keys materialized (echo guard holds; visitor wrote ZERO POSTs, even below the `__campaign__` mount baseline, having never opened Maps).
- **OA trigger ride + economy-display faces (live FY 2026-11-09):** full matrix recipe — enforcement ON +`opportunityAttacks`+`actionEconomy`+`movementBudget`; a PLAYER mover only provokes a PLAYER defender with an EDITED ally list (`hasExplicitAllyList` reads the runtime `selectedAllies` — seed `POST /<Mover> {value:{selectedAllies:['<Mover>']}}` BEFORE campaign-select, defector face = everyone off-list hostile; MID-SESSION flip works via scoped `PATCH /:campaign/<Mover> {value:{selectedAllies:[…]}}` — GET-verify, the `applied` counter lies (confirmed again 2026-11-09 fix run); re-arm a mover's movement budget for a second provoke drag with `DELETE turnState` + ~3.5 s SSE settle); sandbox map `POST /maps {name,type:'indoor',gridSize:20}` — SHARPENED (live 2026-11-09 improve movement-clamp-copy): a `players:[]` array in the CREATE body is silently DROPPED (creates with `players:[]`); seed tokens via follow-up whole-doc PUT (unwrapped, §38) while the Maps-manager (non-board) view is open — a settled board adopts them via live echo; a `page.goto` on a stale-empty board tab FLUSHES a save that wipes a just-landed curl PUT (reload-flush clobber). PUT body must keep `revealed` an ARRAY (`revealed:{}` crashes `useFogOfWar.js:14` new-Set TypeError → Map error boundary) → activate → Maps-manager row `Open` → trusted drag via `svg text` exact-name `closest('g')` rect center — px/cell is ZOOM-dependent (this map measured 42.9 px/cell, not 40: derive `grid-cell[1].x − [0].x`, never hardcode), 40 px/cell `steps:8`+120 ms per cell — a single burst drag silently NO-OP'd once, cell-stepped drags 4/4; RE-DERIVE the token grab rect before EVERY drag — a stale `getBoundingClientRect` captured before the previous drag silently no-ops the next one (grab lands on an empty cell; same failure class as the burst no-op, live 2026-11-09 improve oa-multi-defender-toast). Faces all PASS: trigger (sticky toast verbatim + `enforcement_triggered` bucket:opportunityAttack source:token-move + DEFENDER's turnState `reactionsUsed` 0→1 + mover partial charge; advisory-only resolution is §16 authored design); spent deny (bucket:reaction, source:opportunity-attack-trigger); BLOCKED deny (`no_opportunity_attacks` te → bucket:opportunityAttack, te `reason` verbatim, blocker check runs BEFORE the spent-reaction check — proven by ordering with reaction still spent); move-INTO reach = honest zero provoke; a move of EXACTLY the remaining budget completes. RESOLUTION lane (shipped 2026-11-09, bug-monster-oa-resolution-auto-missed-by-melee-range-gate fix — supersedes "advisory-only resolution is §16 authored design"): the trigger now arms one server-first `pendingOAs` change-data marker per triggering defender `{defender,mover,promptId}` (drain-to-evict, pendingSavePrompts precedent, never refunds the charged reaction); the monster card renders a marker-live-only `mc-dice-link[data-kind="oa"]` "Opportunity Attack on <Mover>" row whose press pins the MARKED MOVER as target and measures melee range at TRIGGER time (normal chips still gate click-time positions byte-identically); a PC sheet OA press WITH a live marker routes around the spent-reaction self-deny (proof probe: `assertCanAct('reaction')` on a spent budget honestly returns "No reaction left this turn." — that was the old PC-side block) and pins target to the mover; markers drop on defender turn TAKEN/ENDED, Initiative Clear, and mover away/dead — but the NEW-INITIATIVE-ROLL leg is DEAD in practice (GA 2026-11-10: every `initiative-rolled` producer — EB join arms `encounterToInitiative.js:177/:352`, NPCs-page `npcCombatService.js:60`, `primalCompanionHandler.js:308` — dispatches while the Initiative view is UNMOUNTED; the sole `clearAllPendingOas` consumer is Initiative.jsx's mount listener (:636) and a curl-seeded marker verifiably SURVIVES a real join; the listener itself works — `window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('initiative-rolled'))` from a mounted Initiative tab is the reliable drop-rig; filed bug-new-initiative-roll-pendingoa-drop-never-fires). curl turnState resets land live via SSE echo (~2 s) but COUNT movement from the client's last-known budget or your provoke becomes the 0-ft snap leg. Repeat-save ROUND-CYCLE law (PT1/PT2 CLOSED): the `save_result` rollType:"save-repeat" row is written at prompt RESOLUTION (Done click), not at Roll Save — a mid-prompt log probe reads zero, never conclude "no audit" early; round-2 seam re-prompts with a FRESH promptId; the wrap seam runs `rearmReactions` (spent `reactionsUsed` 1→0) + openTurn fresh on the new round-head. TurnEconomyStrip DOM law: enforcement ON renders ONE strip per card PLUS a header strip anchored to `activeCreatureName` (n+1 strips is correct, not a dupe); spent flips ride the curl-POST echo with no reload; enforcement DELETE ⇒ zero DOM; ST twin/header remap LAW (fixed 7f99e71ee, FZ 2026-11-09 live-verified): both mount points resolve turnState through `economyNameFor`/`effectSubjectName`, so an active twin's card strip AND the header strip show the HOLDER's spent face (15/30 ft) while the display name keeps twin provenance; normal rows omit economyName byte-unchanged. pendingOAs FZ DESCENT LAWS (first app-wide ride): monster defenders ride as `placedItems[]` npc tokens whose `name` matches the cs monster row (findOpportunityDefenders candidates = players[] then placedItems[] — the joined toast names in THAT order, joinNames 2-/3-name faces "A and B"/"A, B and C"); a live marker mounts the whole "Reactions" section on blocks that author none (`sectionRow` filter); the chip press PINS the marked mover (rolls vs the mover's AC even with the card's target-select armed to a THIRD creature — and the armed value survives the press), writes the `automation/opportunity_attack` provenance row BEFORE the roll row, consumes only that defender's key (multi-marker independence), charges nothing (reaction stays at 1 — no refund on consume/seam/away drops, FZ-witnessed at the Next-INTO-defender seam with `reactionsUsed` staying 1 and at EF away-toggle via `prunePendingOas` — pendingOAs goes key-ABSENT at the 1→0 boundary, never `{}` dust); normal chips keep the click-time auto-miss byte-identical ("Target out of melee range (20 ft > 8 ft)" — the 8 ft reach floor is shipped copy); with actionEconomy OFF the trigger toasts WITHOUT "Reaction spent;" and markers STILL arm on turnState {} untouched. PROBE HAZARD: a second rapid OA-row click after the first press locally consumed the marker lands on the GATED plain lane and honestly prints "No reaction left this turn." + `enforcement_denied` — that denial is my double-click artifact, NOT a latch failure; single press per witness, count roll-row deltas. CLAMP TOAST SEMANTICS (FZ): the snap toast number = feet CHARGED THIS MOVE (25-used+15-requested → "snapped back to 5 ft of movement."; full-charge legs read 30), full-block branch (a3d5d9947, FZ-verified) toasts "<name> cannot move — no movement left this turn." while the LOG row keeps "snapped to the remaining budget (0 ft this move)." bytes. DATA LAW: this bestiary's GAZER HAS a melee Bite (findMeleeAttackRow honestly picks it) — the ONLY zero-melee action block app-wide is Archlich CR30; no-melee degradation face is unit-pinned, not cheap-live. Maps-manager rows render the LIVE `displayName` when the create body carried one (lane shipped 3ea14e629 — corrected in place GA 2026-11-10: rows show "GA Movement/OA Sandbox", GET-stored verbatim) and the active row carries a persistent `ACTIVE` chip + flipped `Deactivate` button (f13a90e15, GA-live re-witnessed): anchor row clicks by `li.maps-manager-item` innerText startsWith the DISPLAY name — a bare "Activate" `.first()` activates the WRONG row (FZ briefly activated battle-arena this way; __map__ stamp only, file untouched — Deactivate row/API to undo).
- **Drag-rig laws (GA 2026-11-10):** (1) cell-stepped drags BILL PER LANDING — N single-cell down/up gestures = N charged moves (5 ft each, and OA triggers evaluate start-vs-end per event: a 1-cell APPROACH provokes nothing, the NEXT departure provokes); one burst gesture = one move; (2) SAME-CELL token overlap is DETERMINISTIC since 7cce4cb73 (live-verified GB 2026-11-10): every token sharing a cell fans diagonally top-left by 10px×steps (paint order players-array → monster items → npc items; stack TOP stays pinned at the exact cell centre, buried shift −1/−2 steps, singles render byte-unchanged); the pointer grabs by paint order at the visible pixel — the token you SEE under the cursor is the token that MOVES (cell centre = stack top forever; buried tokens grab on their visible crescent, which can sit just OUTSIDE the cell border; stacked creature tokens swap the 40×40 rect hit for a circle matching the art; a drag started off-centre preserves the grab offset, so the landing cell = pointer-cell + round(offset/50%-of-cell) — a −22px grab landed +1/+1 off); the old GA mover-alternation trap is CLOSED — two consecutive cell-center drags off an occupied cell moved the SAME token each time; (3) the map EDGE clamps silently BEFORE the movement budget does — a "clamp at speed" face needs ≥ speed/5 spare cells along the drag axis (a 50-ft request upward from y=3 "clamped" at the edge after 15 ft, zero toast, log stays clean); (4) an NPC placedItems-token drag PROVOKES PC defenders honestly (markers `mover:<Monster>`, reactions charged, joined toast — no defector flip needed, npc movers are always hostile) but writes NO turnState entry — owner-ratified DESIGN LAW "GM moves are free by design" (1982383ab, header comment in `useItemDragging.js`; the movementBudget clamp is player-token-only by design — do NOT add a charge lane without an owner ruling); non-player rows' movement badges carry the advisory tooltip `GM-driven moves are not charged against this budget — …` (verified verbatim on card strips + the header strip when the ACTIVE row is non-player; player rows keep `<N> ft of movement remaining this turn`; a11y lanes shipped 2026-11-10 improve gm-move-advisory-a11y — every GM movement badge mirrors the full advisory into `aria-label` ("<used/ft> — <advisory>") and the CARD face also renders a visible `(GM-driven)` span `.turn-economy-gm-hint` (header stays compact, hint omitted), player faces keep `aria-label` absent + no hint byte-unchanged; `CreatureBadge` gained the optional `ariaLabel` prop (undefined = attribute omitted, DOM byte-identical for every consumer); the clamp lane resolves speed from the CHAR SHEET, so a mid-session feat grant needs a tab reload before the new speed clamps — GB rode a stale tab honestly clamping at 30); (5) a defector flip (`selectedAllies:[self]`) on the MOVER makes every PC defender react to its moves; the mover itself never appears as a DEFENDER key in `pendingOAs` for its own move (no self-marker witnessed GA).
- **Turn-economy (turnState) ride + ST seam (live FW 2026-11-09):** enforcement arm for economy = `POST /:c/enforcement {value:{mode:'on',turnLock:true,actionEconomy:true}}`. `turnState` surfaces as a TOP-LEVEL change-data key (GET cd.turnState; cd.campaign is null) and accepts full-store `POST /:c/turnState {value:{…}}` (REPLACES the bucket — the cheapest phantom-evict + companionOf stamp: `AasimarTest:{companionOf:'EvasiveFighter', …}` passed the turn-lock while the twin was active, closing the companion face ER/FV deferred). Sheet attacks CHARGE on allow (before popup resolution), and the gate never reads the `turnActive` flag — denials come only from turnLock + bucket exhaustion ("No action left this turn." popup + `enforcement_denied` row). FIXED 2026-11-09 (4dc24883b, `bug-secondturn-seam-starves-holder-economy-phantom-turnstate` in `resolved/`): `applyTurnStateTransition` + `markRewoundTurnActive` (navigationHandlers.js) now resolve the twin via `economyNameFor(summary,name)` (mirrors the c076dfdf1 gate law) — the HOLDER is re-armed at the holder→twin seam (fresh action/bonus budget even with actionEconomy ON, reactions preserved), the holder CLOSES at twin→X, and Prev-rewind-into-twin opens the HOLDER without refund; `expireStaleEffects` is `!isSecondTurnEntry`-guarded in BOTH navigation handlers AND the SSE echo lane (see §16 echo-producer pitfall). Post-fix twin walk leaves ZERO `*Second Turn*` keys anywhere in change-data; pinned by `navigationHandlers.second-turn.test.js`. Still never ridden: movementBudget token-drag under twin-active (`movementUsedFeet` zeroes correctly at the seam, the drag lane itself unridden). Face-switch law unchanged: holder-pass/stranger-deny/companion-pass all switchable by full-store cs re-POST + top-level `activeCreatureName` POST — but the POSTed cs creatures adoption does NOT move a settled tab's turn pointer (that follows the top-level `activeCreatureName` POST), and a FRESH mount merge WIPES the seeded `activeCreatureName` and re-highlights from per-client localStorage `activeCreatureName`. FIXED 2026-11-09 (improve mount-merge-pointer-fidelity): `mergeCombatSummaryWithCharacters` now PRESERVES cs `activeCreatureName` when the named row survives the merge (ST twin counts via its own name), and Initiative mount resolution is `merged.activeCreatureName → getActiveCreatureName() → creatures[0] → null` — a seeded board's pointer survives select→mount→debounce-flush end-to-end; dangling pointers drop and the per-client fallback wins. Correction to the same claim: the fallback is NOT localStorage — `getActiveCreatureName()` reads the in-memory `cachedCombatSummaries` Map in `services/encounters/combatData.js` (per-client, campaign-keyed); no localStorage read for `activeCreatureName` exists anywhere.
- **Initiative mount-test harness recipe (live 2026-11-09 improve mount-merge-pointer-fidelity):** an Initiative mount test can use the REAL `initiativeService` (do NOT mock it) — jsdom imports clean given the standard zero-creature harness mock block, so mount tests exercise the merge-eviction legs for free (see `Initiative.mount-active-creature.test.jsx`).
- Re-arm round-latched features (e.g. Boon of Combat Prowess) inside a loaded tab WITHOUT reload: scoped `PATCH /:campaign/combatSummary {value:{round:N+1}}` (returns the known `applied:0` lie — GET-verify; live-proved 2026-11-06).
- `saveResult-<Target>` change-data key = machine truth `{saveBonus,rawRolls,mode,success}`; mode is the ONLY adv/dis truth; crit nat20 doubles DICE only (flat mod not doubled; secondary shows `secondaryTotal=2×sum`). §82 ledger law RESTORED EVERYWHERE (fix 2026-11-10, `bug-monster-attacks-log-phantom-second-die` resolved): every shared-lane roll row (`useLoggedDiceRollAttack.js buildRollLogEntry`) satisfies `mode==='normal' ? rolls.length===1 : rolls.length===2` — the pre-fix phantom second die hit BOTH monster-card AND PC-sheet presses (GB's "sheet lane logs one die" contrast was stale), plus the EB-join initiative row (`encounterToInitiative.js`, now single-die); `rolls.length` is again a valid adv/dis discriminator. Popup payload intentionally KEEPS the 2-die pool on normal (GM adv/dis toggle + roll-time Lucky/Feats-of-Chaos folds consume it). Genuine monster-lane adv/dis faces proven live: restraint on the ATTACKER's CS ROW (`conditions:[{key:'restrained','name':'Restrained'}]` via scoped PATCH combatSummary) ⇒ `[a,b] disadvantage` keep-low + "Disadv (conditions)" chip; same on the TARGET's cs row ⇒ `[a,b] advantage` keep-high + "Adv (conditions)" chip (the cs-row feed is `extractConditionKeys` reading `creature.conditions` OBJECTS, not the `activeConditions` string list). OA chip press with a curl-seeded `pendingOAs` marker + clean mover ⇒ `rolls:[n] mode:"normal"`, marker drains to absent.
- Log truth: `roll.total` = raw d20 (bonus separate); popup total = +bonus; breath logs' `rolls` can diverge from applied `total` — decisive: `total==finalDamage==|hp_change|` vs authored max. `/half/i` scans false-positive on names; "/hit/i" on "(+N to hit)" — match ✓/✗ glyphs. 0-HP victim = death-clamp `finalDamage:0` — never use for half-damage proofs.
- Crit damage lane log shape (live 2026-11-06): the doubled pool logs as `formula "1d6*2+1 (N)"` + `isCrit:true` — a single roll × dice multiplier with the flat modifier NOT doubled — not a two-dice `rolls` array; pin this shape for crit proofs (attack and converted-nat-20 faces).
- **Crit shape two-lane UNIFIED (2026-11-06 improve, crit-ledger-shape-sheet-lane):** the sheet lane now stamps the same per-dice-group `*2` marker as the monster lane on the base+sneak doubled groups (`buildRollBaseDamageStep`/`buildSneakAttackStep` → `markCritDoubledGroups` in attackRollDamageCalc.js, idempotent via `(?!\*2)` guard, fired only when `wasCrit && !isOverchannel`). Display-string only: `total` stays 2×dice+flat, `rolls` stays single-set. ORIGIN of the old split: `buildPlainDamageLogData` (handlePlainDamage.js) routes sheet crit rows through `formatDamageFormula`, but MULTI-GROUP formulas (`X [a] + Y [b]`) fail its `parseExpression` and fell through unmarked — that was the whole bug; a source-marked formula also fails that parse and passes through verbatim (label-preserving), so the log lane needed no change. Spell lane (`directSpellDamageSteps.js`) deliberately NOT source-marked: single-group formulas already get marked at log time and source-marking would strip the `(N)` roll list. RE-PIN CENSUS TRAP: the only test running attackRollDamageCalc end-to-end against the hook is `char-sheet/useAttackDamageResolution.test.jsx` (real-pipeline lane) — a steps-folder-only run misses it.

- **Devious Strikes sheet-lane chooser law (live 2026-11-06 DH, post-e81bdd985):** the DS chooser mounts ONLY when sneak dice are available for the round — a sneak-LATCHED hit-cycle honestly shows no chooser and lands base `1d6+1` (never call that a jam; re-arm via scoped cs `round` PATCH). Tick options via `.sp-overlay label` startingWith the option name → inner `input.click()`; costs Poison/Trip/Withdraw 1d6, Daze 2d6, Obscure 3d6, Knock Out 6d6; max-2 stacks queue two prompts. Drain loop must handle a VT-SKIP overlay between prompt-resolve Done and the damage popup (sheet HIT popups stay two-stage). Poison material refusal = kitless Apply → `X cannot be used: Requires Poisoner's Kit` notice + Done, zero cost, FULL sneak pool still lands. Poison-save-SUCCESS: healthy fingerprint is 3-row pre-damage lead (`Forgoing 1d6` → `save_result success` → `Devious Strikes/Poison applied … succeeded — no effect`) then `9d6 [Sneak Attack]` with `rolls` length 10; a whole-log `'10d6' in formula` scan (excluding crit `10d6*2` cost-free faces) is the fast refund discriminator. STATUS LIVE 2026-11-07 DI: refund REPRODUCED once on HEAD 616289b98 (`10d6`/11-rolls under the Forgoing row) after being marked disproved 2026-11-06 — treat as FLAKY lane, sample ≥3 success faces, filed `bug-poison-save-success-refund-regressed`. RE-DISPROVED 2026-11-07 fix-verify on HEAD 6293d6243 (post daze-expiry + repeat-save-descriptor fixes): 5 Poison-save-SUCCESS faces (1 crit `9d6*2` + 4 non-crit `9d6`, all rolls-len 10) + Poison-fail control all deducted, whole-log `10d6` grep zero, and a MID-PAUSE GET (after Apply Effects, save prompt unresolved) proved `_cunningStrikeCostUsed:1` persists server-side with the te row present — capture that mid-pause GET + bucket snapshot before ever filing this lane a third time. Trip-success and Poison-fail both deducted correctly in the same tab, so control-per-option is the discriminator. Crit face: value honest (2×dice+flat) and since the crit-ledger-shape improve (2026-11-06) the sheet-lane `roll` row carries the monster-lane `*2` marker on every doubled group (`1d6*2+1 [piercing] + 9d6*2 [Sneak Attack]`); `rolls` stays single-set.
- **Sheet-lane popups print the TARGET'S SHEET AC** (DH: board ac:12, popup "vs AC 19") — don't read the board `ac` field as popup truth for sheet attacks.
- **DS/rider overlay-stack doctrine + expiry-lane truths (live 2026-11-07 DI):** a Rogue-feat-holder sheet hit can park its damage stage behind up to FOUR stacked overlays (DS chooser → Charge chooser → Shield-Bash prompt → Shield-Bash fail-chooser) — drains MUST act on the LAST `.sp-overlay` (querySelectorAll, pop()), and the footer-button roster differs per chooser: DS confirm `Apply Effects` (plural), Charge/Shield-Bash confirm `Apply Effect`, VT confirm `Trip Secondary Target`, declines `Cancel` vs `Skip (do not consume use)` (Shield Bash, honest zero-spend). Repeat-save lane FIXED 2026-11-07 (`buildRiderEffect` now stamps the consumer's `repeatSave` OBJECT descriptor `{saveType, dc: buildSaveDc(option, playerStats), condition}` alongside the authored boolean, grantRepeatSaveEffect shape) — the turn-END roller `repeatSaveService.applyRepeatSaveTurnEnd` (navigationHandlers seam) fires the prompt at the victim's turn-END and success evicts te + condition with `save_result(save-repeat)` + `condition removed` rows. Save-GATED rider legs skip the FT-082 `addExpiration` (`if (!option.saveType)` in attackRiderHandler) → `daze`/`blinded`-class te rows stamped `until_start_of_next_turn` register NO clock and have no duration-string consumer → `dazed` persists and keeps granting `Adv (conditions)` to every later attack (contextBuilder counts dazed/slow; control = scoped-PATCH the victim's activeConditions minus dazed, chip flips next press). FIXED 2026-11-07 (`attackRiderHandler.registerRiderConditionExpiry`): the failed-save leg now registers ONE FT-082-shape clock per grant — `until_start_of_next_turn` attacker-anchored, `until_end_of_next_turn` rounds:2 target-axis (CLA-169) — one merged write carrying `remove_target_effect` (tuple-scoped) + `{type:'condition'}` strip; Trip (persistent-prone) and Poison/KO (1_minute, repeat-save lane) stay clockless by pin — they terminate via the repeat-save. Rider conditions are NO LONGER combat-persistent (except the documented persistent-prone + repeat-save family).

- **Initiative page re-seeds hand-rolled boards (live 2026-11-07 fix-verify):** opening the Initiative view while a hand-rolled (e.g. 3-creature type:player) combatSummary is live appends EVERY campaign character as a board member (blank initiatives) — the walk order/round-wrap then needs a full 13-creature cycle to reach an anchor creature's NEXT turn. Plan anchor-leg verification either with ≥13 bounded Next clicks (poll `activeCreatureName` between clicks, drain per step) or re-POST the small board AFTER the Initiative page has settled (re-POST while it mounts races the seed). Turn boundaries during the walk can open other creatures' prompts (Stunning-style strikes on player turns) — always run the drain pass inside the walk loop. SHARPENED BOTH LEGS (live 2026-11-09 ER, IN PLACE post-fix 7e5ffc2b9): the mount merge (`initiativeService.mergeCombatSummaryWithCharacters`) DELETES every `type:'player'` row whose name matches no campaign character EXCEPT `secondTurn:true` rows, which are kept while their `holderName` is a campaign character and evicted as zombies otherwise, and normalize through the player leg (never the monster HP-10 defaults) — the old silent eviction of Thief's-Reflexes `(Second Turn)` rows is FIXED (`bug-initiative-remount-drops-second-turn-row`, pinned by `initiativeService.second-turn.test.js`); the UNMOUNT writes nothing (row survives a 14s+ away window incl. debounce); so any lane that leaves and re-enters Initiative re-runs the merge — seed boards via curl right before the mount, not after. Recipe for testing survival/eviction board laws: seed ONE multi-facet board (legit synthetic row + ghost player + orphaned secondTurn + NPC + missing PCs) and a single remount exercises every keep/evict/append leg at once — assert byte-identity on the survivor. WALK LAW (fully live-verified FT 2026-11-09): Next steps INTO the row (single `.creature-card.active`, no double-highlight with the holder card), the row card resolves the HOLDER's runtime HP via `holderName` (input showed 103), its target-select lists the holder and excludes secondTurn rows globally, Prev rewinds INTO the row fine, and the row RETIRES at the round-1 wrap (Next past the last creature → round 2, cards−1, zero audit rows for the row). RE-GRANT RIG: a settled tab RE-BAKES `round` from its local mirror on every cs write — a curl scoped-PATCH `{round:1}` alone never re-arms the grant (the next card commit POSTs round 2 back; grant honestly refuses, use stays unspent). To re-ride round 1: Initiative `Clear` → role=dialog Yes (resets round to 1, strips monsters, re-seeds the 13 PCs) → re-arm the use via scoped PATCH `{"thief'sreflexesUses":1}` → card-init commit grants fresh. The ST card KEEPS its `.effect-add-btn` Add button — that is the phantom-bucket bug lane (`bug-effectadder-secondturn-phantom-bucket`), not a test artifact.
- **Chooser footer family unified (2026-11-07 improve):** the DS/Charge/Shield-Bash/Open-Hand choosers and the VT/SF picker confirm labels now render through `ChooserModalFooter` (`modals/shared/`) with canonical strings owned by `chooserFooterLabels.js` — visible labels are UNCHANGED bytes (`Apply Effects` / `Apply Effect` / `Trip Secondary Target` / `Attack Target` / `Apply Fear` / `Cancel` / `Skip (do not consume use)`), so every drain-loop button roster in this playbook stays true; the paid-cost `committed` latch (both faces disable, Cancel title `Cost already committed — resolve the effect instead`) is preserved. Never inline a footer string in a chooser modal — import from `CHOOSER_FOOTER_LABELS`; `ChooserModalFooter.labels.test.jsx` pins the set.
- **Save-dial flips the chooser decline label (live 2026-11-08):** on the EF rig every sheet hit stacks DS chooser → Charge chooser → Shield-Bash prompt → (fail) Shield-Bash chooser; a `warding_bond −19` dial set for the DS save ALSO flips the STR bash save, so the fail-cycle's decline label is `Skip (do not consume use)` — drain loops should match `startsWith('Skip')`, not the exact full string.
- **VT confirm null-action write race (live 2026-11-07, PRE-EXISTING — recommend filing `bug-vt-action-stamp-write-race`):** the first VT confirm can throw `TypeError: Cannot read properties of null (reading 'automation')` at versatileTricksterHandler.js:30 — `stampVersatileTricksterTargets` fires three un-awaited `setRuntimeValue` full-store POSTs in one tick (§5/MA-0809 race) and the runtime key `versatileTricksterAction` can read null server-side at confirm time. Discriminator = GET the char bucket (key null); rig repair = scoped PATCH the canonical `{type:'versatile_trickster', automation:{type:'versatile_trickster', casting_time:'passive'}}` — the identical confirm path then runs clean end-to-end.
- **React controlled inputs need native-setter fill (live 2026-11-07):** Playwright `fill` alone can leave a dependent button (Open Hand flurry `Strike All`) disabled; set value via the native input setter + dispatch an `input` event so React's onChange fires.
- **Stage-2 damage-popup button ORDER (live 2026-11-07 DJ):** on a feat-holder's multi-group sheet hit, the STAGE-2 damage popup renders `[Savage Attacker] [Done]` — `button.dice-roll-reroll-btn` matches Savage Attacker FIRST, so a naive `querySelector('.dice-roll-reroll-btn').click()` or first-`button` drain click FEEDS THE FEAT BUTTON and never closes the popup. Drain loops must select by `textContent.trim()==='Done'`. (UPDATE 2026-11-07 fix-verify: Savage Attacker was a silent no-op on multi-group formulas; FIXED — the guard is now group-aware (`DiceRollResult.handlers.js` censuses ALL formula dice groups against the pool, rerolls the FIRST group = weapon dice only, per RAW feats.json both rulesets), so a mis-fed click now REALLY spends the feat and stamps the round latch — keying Done by text is now safety-critical, not just reliability.)
- **Savage Attacker live rig (live 2026-11-07 fix-verify, `bug-savage-attacker-silent-noop-multi-group-damage` FIXED):** EF Rogue/AT-lv20 rig + 3-creature hand-rolled gridless board arms sneak (round-bump re-arm recipe: scoped `PATCH combatSummary {round:N+1}` + scoped `PATCH <Char> {_Savage_Attacker_usedRound:null}` + `_SneakAttack_usedRound` self-expires when it != current round; GET-verify after 13s debounce). Faces: MULTI-group sneak popup rerolls ONLY the first formula group (audit row `rolled 1 (1) then rerolled 4 (4)`, Keep-Reroll delta +3, popup rolls stay the full merged pool `4, 2, 2, …`); SINGLE-group crit popup (`1d6*2+3`, `*2` display-only, single-set pool) passes the census and behaves as before; the once-per-round latch honestly drops the button on the next popup. Single-group non-sneak face = any hit in a sneak-LATCHED round (cheap control face — no board surgery needed).
- **Death Strike rig (FIXED 2026-11-07, `bug-death-strike-granted-to-any-sneak-attacker`):** producer `maybeStoreDeathStrike` (hitResolution.js) now gates on the character's derived `playerStats.automation` carrying the canonical Assassin-lv17 envelope (`attack_rider` + `first_round_sneak_attack_hit` + `damageDoubled`, byte-identical in both rulesets' classes.json) — non-Assassins store nothing; consumer `resolveDeathStrike` logs `save_result` on BOTH save faces and the prompt carries `sourceName:'Death Strike'`. Rig notes: the CON DC15 prompt fires in the SAME attack cycle that stores the te (attack stage → damage stage), not only the next attack — a mid-pause GET between DS-chooser-Cancel and Roll Save is the cleanest grant-face capture; a Stroke-of-Luck-CONVERTED hit stores no te (producer runs in `resolveHit`, before the popup-time conversion) — rig the grant face with natural hits; and a hand-rolled 2-creature board can NEVER arm sneak (needs a flanking ally or advantage) — Death Strike/DS rigs need ≥3 creatures (a 3rd `type:'player'` combatant suffices, gridless-lenient). FAIL face closed live (EK 2026-11-08, −19 dial): the doubling lands as a SECOND `roll` row whose formula is the original multi-group string prefixed `+1× ` + `saveResult:"failure"` + copy "…attack damage doubled (total N); extra M applied" + a second `hp_change` −M (M = base eager delta = half the doubled total); te evicted either face; the FAIL leg writes NO `save_result` row (success-branch-only by fix design — the paired rider-save `save_result` rows you see nearby are Shield-Bash/etc. and now carry the real attacker's `characterName` (mis-attribution FIXED 2026-11-08, 20894648c). Assassin swap bytes: top-level `level`, `class.name:'Rogue'`, `class.subclass:{name:'Assassin',subtitle:'Practice the Grim Art of Death'}` + backpack "Poisoner's Kit"; direct disk edits show on the `.json` GET route immediately (no debounce dance), reload+re-select derives the sheet rows.

DG-2024-lane addendum (live 2026-11-13 DG): the 2024 walk numbers Magic Items as **Step 15** (step INDEX depends on lane/race — match the `.wizard-content` heading text `Step N: Magic Items`, never a fixed N); gated steps are ONE native `<select>` each (Background, Subclass) — drive via `HTMLSelectElement.prototype` value setter + bubbling `change` inside one evaluate (plain `.value=` skips React 19); the walk's step-14 spurious `.mi-overlay` needs NO close — `Next` walks past it (DG); the ruleset card click + Next advances (cards do NOT auto-advance, EL-corrected law holds for 2024 too); abandoning the finished draft fired NATIVE `Discard changes to "QA DH Ghost"?` and wrote ZERO disk (13-file census byte-exact after) → **RACE step is ALSO a native `<select>`** (Human default-selected; a mechanical `selectOption(index:1)` silently flips to Dragonborn — clean mechanical walk leaves Human, Subrace step then renders no select) → Class card-click auto-advance → (EN correction: in the 5e lane step 5 Class renders as a NATIVE `<select>` (WizardStepClass.jsx), NOT a card-click — pick via select; the card-click/auto-advance law applies to the step-1 ruleset cards `[data-testid="rules-option-5e|2024"]`, which do NOT auto-advance either — click `Next`) → **SUBCLASS/Major step is a native `<select>`** (selectOption label match — card-click walkers stall at NEXT-OFF indefinitely, EL burned a full loop discovering this) → Fighter lv1 steps 7-13 no-op-through → Magic Items (step 14): `.list-item-checkbox-trigger` evaluate-clicks (416 rows, titles `Select <name>`/`Un-tick to remove <name>`), "Add Custom Magic Item" → `#cmi-name/#cmi-type/#cmi-rarity/#cmi-description` + attunement checkbox → "Add Item" → custom row inserts A-Z with `custom` chip + Edit + **auto-checked**. `#cmi-type` is a datalist TEXT INPUT (use `fill`, not selectOption); modal close = `.close-modal-btn` (aria-label Close), save = `.custom-magic-item-save-btn`. **UNTICK GUARD (shipped):** unticking a selected `custom:true` row now arms the shared DeleteConfirmModal — heading `Remove Custom Magic Item?`, buttons `Cancel` / `Yes, Remove`, `titleId="magic-item-custom-delete-title"`; Cancel keeps the row checked/intact (re-tick after cancel does not duplicate); confirm removes the row (removal payload byte-identical to the old ungated toggle). Catalog rows still deselect instantly with no modal, title reverting to `Select <name>`. Pinned by `WizardStepMagicItems.custom-untick-guard.test.jsx`. Save → `<Name>.json` on disk ~15s debounce carries `inventory.magicItems:[{name,type,rarity,requiresAttunement,description,custom:true}]` VERBATIM → sheet Inventory section renders all fields; campaign Inventory view does NOT list customs (matches fieldnote — honest). Throwaway char delete = sheet `char-btn Delete` → NATIVE confirm → 13-file baseline restored same-session.

Versatile Trickster still lets you…" / fail copy keeps "Trip applied to X."); producer witness = EF-bucket key `versatileTricksterPrimarySave {success,saveType,saveDc}`; trip te row evicts on save-SUCCESS (§5 law). POSTing the small hand-rolled board AFTER the Initiative page settles avoids the §72 13-creature re-seed entirely (with `activeCreatureName` = the victim POSTed after both tabs settle, the repeat-save prompt fires on the FIRST `Next` — turn-END seam; corrected in place 2026-11-08, the old "~click 3" claim was from a lagged-mirror walk), and te survives Dismiss for a second same-load face (live 2026-11-08 improve repeat-save-source-label).

With `jsonSchema:false` the SAME instruction returns a real DIFF (EL: Role→"QA Ember-Scrivener" landed in the NPC form; audit `ai_refine` WITHOUT noOp, attempts:2).
