CORRECTION to the EK claim that a cold tab LANDS on the tracker with the sidebar unmounted: `activeView` is NOT persisted — a fresh tab picks a campaign and lands on the character sheet; the tracker-TV arrival was context *reuse*. And the trap is now closed regardless: an app-level `TvExitButton` (bottom-right, z-1400) renders on EVERY view while the pref is ON — click = pref OFF + fullscreen exit + presentation restored (same face as the toggle's OFF); `tvModeStore` gained the reactive pref slot (`getTvPref`/`subscribeTvPref`/`saveTvPref`/`exitTvPresentation`, pure localStorage facade) consumed via `useSyncExternalStore`. Clear `initiative-tv`/`initiative-victory-dismissed` keys when a run leaves TV state. Spotlight = `zoom` (1.875 / 1.44 media-query), NOT transform — read `getComputedStyle().zoom` for the face, and flex `order` is gone (3a527a30e: order is authoritative, active card scales IN PLACE). Stat-block privacy (579aca8d2): MonsterCardModal open state is per-client useState — TV ON means avatar click = ZERO overlays even for localhost GM; the retired `*-viewingMonster` keys must never be asserted in change-data (campaignKeys comment pins the retirement). Delegation: GM-only `[data-testid="control-select"]` (aria `Assign who controls X`) on type:npc cards stamps cs row `controller` + audit "…handed to X — their device can run its card (roll, target, saves) until released." / "…returned to the GM."; release = selectOption('') → controller null. LAN device sim = `browser_tabs {action:'new', isolatedContext:'lan', url:'http://<en0-ip>:5173'}` — isLocalhost follows the HOSTNAME (context isolation doesn't matter); visitor sees read-only nav ("Map" not "Maps", no Encounters/Admin), no `.combat-controls`/control-selects/HP inputs, `YourTurnCue` ("Your turn!") on viewer-controlled active cards, unassigned monster avatar = inert, ASSIGNED card opens + arms via its own row `[data-testid="target-select"]` + rolls through the identical monster lanes (log attrs the monster). Open-gate is `controller!==null` WITHOUT per-device identity check (creatureControl.js LAN-trust D2, accepted): any visitor tab may run any delegated card. FLAKES (2026-11-08 improve tv-music-controls): a LAN dev-host tab's first load can die mid-module-graph with `ERR_NETWORK_CHANGED` (blank body) — bounded reload fixes; and a visitor's first `.tv-toggle-button` OFF click was observed ineffective (pref stayed '1', second click worked) — re-probe pref after every toggle, EK-era face worth re-riding (NOT reproduced live 2026-11-11 run LZ: visitor-context first ON and first OFF clicks both worked from a null-start pref). LZ 2026-11-11 full ride (all PASS except the filed bug): picker stamps `controller` + both audit strings verbatim; visitor census = 14 cards, 0 `.combat-controls`/control-selects/HP-inputs/Next/Clear, ONLY a permanently-disabled `Init` number input on monster cards (backlog), name span (not input); `YourTurnCue` mounts/drops live with the controller stamp; ledger 1:1 (one roll row per press — MISS carries `hit:false` + `targetName`, HIT row + separate damage roll row + one `hp_change`; distinct dice, no replay); cross-client floater re-proven (GM-TV MutationObserver caught `hp-floater-damage::−7` from a visitor hit); RELEASE revokes the open-gate (re-open inert) and the cue, but a card opened BEFORE release stays live and keeps rolling damage ⇒ FILED `bug-released-delegation-stale-open-card-keeps-rolling`; manual GM card HP edit (`input[aria-label="<Name> current HP"]` fill+Enter) DOES write an `hp_change` log row (§89's "logs nothing" is initiative-input-only).
- **EN TV-faces / transport / allies / friendly-fire selectors (live 2026-11-08):** Spotlight zoom BOTH faces — tall viewport: active card `getComputedStyle().zoom` = 1.875, base 1.5; `browser_resize` window height ≤900 flips to 1.44/1.15 via the `@media (max-height:900px)` lane (Initiative.css ~:1615) — probe `.initiative--tv .creature-card.active` and restore size after. Floaters are PURE render HP-diffs (useTvPresentation over displayCreatures): with TV ON, scoped-`PATCH` a victim's `currentHitPoints` and probe `.hp-floater` within ~900ms — classes `hp-floater-heal|hp-floater-damage` + `hp-floater-big` when |delta| ≥ max(10, 25%·maxHp), damage text uses the U+2212 glyph (`−90`), the node self-retires on animationend (zero residue); BLOODIED badge = `.status-badge.bloodied` at HP ≤ half, reads live from runtime `currentHitPoints` seeded before select. TV header transport (23a8adc45) proven: chip mounts in `.combat-header` ONLY while TV ON AND mood armed; buttons `.combat-header button[title="Pause"]`/`[title="Stop"]`; Pause drops itself from the roster (chip+Stop) keeping `mood`, Stop nulls mood → zero chrome; audit rows byte-identical to sidebar lanes (`Paused Tavern ambience`/`Stopped ambience`). Allies editor: `.allies-edit-btn` (aria `Edit allies of <X>`, one per card, GM-only) was once reported carousel-clipped → evaluate-click — SHARPENED IN PLACE (live 2026-11-13 BE): on a settled 14-row board a plain `getByRole('button',{name:'Edit allies of Goblin'})` trusted click LANDS with zero evaluate, so the clip only bites in genuine carousel-overflow states; the modal MOUNTS AS `.sp-modal`/`.sp-overlay` (save-prompt chrome reuse; the controls cluster resolves as `.ally-selection-controls`); checkboxes aria `Select <name>`, header buttons `Select All`/`Clear All`/`Self only (hostile)` (fa-user-slash one-click preset — 7e517015d, verified live 2026-11-11 incl. monster subjects: draft-only, selects exactly `[selfName]`, nothing written until Confirm), confirm label `Confirm Allies (N)` DISABLED at 0 (default/current selection = self-only, the minimum); confirm writes `<Char>.selectedAllies` (self kept) + `ability_use`/`Ally Selection` audit `<X>'s allies set to: <joined>`. Stable arm selector: `[aria-label="<Name> target"]` (PC avatar alts are INITIALS — `:has(img[alt="<PCName>"])` misses). Arming a LISTED ally fires the Friendly-Fire confirm (`Cancel`/`Yes, Arm Target`, FAIL-CLOSED — targetName stays null until accept; shell selectors are `.modal-shell-overlay`/`.modal-shell`/`.modal-shell-actions` since the 2026-11-08 improve modal-shell-css factor — the shared `DeleteConfirmModal` tier rides the same neutral global shell, `maps-manager-modal-*` remains ONLY on Maps-manager-native modals (Description, Generate Dungeon/Terrain); PITFALL: judging the accept leg's `targetName` from an in-page `fetch` fired in the SAME tick as the button click races the un-awaited scoped PATCH (the log row lands first) — re-GET via curl to confirm the arm; accept logs `Target Selection` "…armed X as a target — …"); flipping the list back self-only = the next arm fires NO confirm (engine follows the edit immediately, no reload). Victory banner is click-transparent outside its card since 2026-11-08 (improve victory-banner-click-block): the `.victory-banner` scrim is `pointer-events:none`, only `.victory-card` captures (card click/Continue dismiss) — a post-Clear walk needs NO banner dismiss first, first `Next →` lands; `document.elementFromPoint` on the Next-button center is a one-shot live proof of overlay interception mirroring Playwright's hit-test. Delegation re-ridden post-TV-batch, all PASS: assign audit `control_assigned` "…handed to X — their device can run its card (roll, target, saves) until released."; release `selectOption('')` → `control_released` "…returned to the GM."; the controller stamp survives a GM reload server-side; LAN visitor (isolatedContext tab on `http://<en0-ip>:5173` — vite needs `--host`, plain `npx vite` = ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED on the LAN IP) auto-adopts identity from the first character, sees read-only nav + 0 control-selects/HP inputs/Next/Clear, but on the CONTROLLED monster its card target-select renders and arms, and a chip press rolls honestly (attributed row `characterName:"Goblin"`, raw `total`, `result:false`).
- **Duplicate-overlay identity gate (verified eca20cc5c, EK 2026-11-08):** seed one `savePrompt-<Target>-<pid>` POST → exactly 1 `.sp-overlay`; re-POST identical key/value → still 1; reload + campaign re-select (connect replays all keys) → still exactly 1; DELETE key → unmounts. Exit ritual still: delete every prompt key you mint (§39 replay law).

- **Friendly-fire through the DELEGATION lane (PT2 CLOSED live 2026-11-11 run MA):** the subject monster's ally list is GM-editable like a PC's (`.allies-edit-btn` exists on EB-joined monster cards, aria `Edit allies of <Monster>`, writes `<Monster>.selectedAllies` + `Ally Selection` audit); an arm of a LISTED ally by the VISITOR's tab fires the same `.modal-shell` Friendly-Fire confirm on the visitor context (copy "Friendly fire: <X> is on <Monster>'s ally list. Arm them as the target anyway?", `Cancel`/`Yes, Arm Target`), FAIL-CLOSED both tabs (target-select value stays "" and cs `targetName` null server-side while open); Cancel = zero write + zero audit; Accept = arm lands + `friendlyFire:true` "Target Selection" row attributed `characterName:"<Monster>"` (the SUBJECT, not the visitor identity). Re-arming the same listed ally again after a Cancel re-fires the confirm (no silent latch).

## 5. Fix conventions (code layer)
- **App-wide z-index tier map (live 2026-11-08, `bug-victory-banner-above-confirm-dialogs` FIXED):** page chrome ≤100 (fixed sidebar/party-inventory) · ambient-celebration slot 150 (`.victory-banner` — was 1300, which swallowed dialog buttons) · ambient dialog tier 200 (`popup-overlay` dice results, sheet sub-modals, dice-tray drawer) · primary modal tier 1000 (`.modal-shell-overlay` + all feature modals, inner layers 1100) · `TvExitButton` 1400 (intentional always-reachable corner pin) · adjudication prompts 2000–2100 (save/concentration/death-save, CampaignReloadNotice). Never place a transient/ambient overlay above 200. Cleanest pre/post witness for "overlay swallows dialog button" bugs: `document.elementFromPoint` at the dialog button's rect center (identical to Playwright's hit-test and a human's dead click).
- te registry: register EVERY new te key in `src/services/combat/conditions/targetEffectDefinitions.js` (fields whitelist pinned by test; alphabetical/Defensive ordering enforced); GM add-UI reads it only.
- **Ephemeral shared-UI flags must EVICT on close, never POST null (2026-11-08 improve effect-adder-target-orphan-key):** a `useSyncedState` close that sets `null` persists a GHOST key (`key: null`) in change-data which replays on every connect/re-select; an unmount-while-open orphans the full `{value}`. Evict via `deleteRuntimeKey` (`hooks/runtime/useRuntimeState.js` — server DELETE + null echo, `has()` guard makes repeat deletes idempotent, no 404 churn), mirroring the savePrompt drain-to-evict lane; also evict on component detach so a view-switch while open can't orphan. Pattern pinned by `useEffectAdderTarget.js` + `Initiative.effectAdder-evict.test.jsx` (11 Initiative test mocks needed `deleteRuntimeKey: vi.fn()`). APPLY-path EVICTS TOO (live 2026-11-09 ER): a successful Conditions-tab Apply closes the modal and GETs back `combat-ui-effectAdderTarget` ABSENT (not null) — plus the full honest chain `condition applied` (type:condition, ability stamped) → `creature-badge effect-condition` on the card → badge × sibling `button[title="Remove effect"]` → `activeConditions:[]` + a `condition broken` audit row (action:"broken"). Anchor the affected card via `:has(input[aria-label="<Name> current HP"])` — every card's Target dropdown lists ALL names, so any card-text/hasText probe hits card #0 first (live ER trap). GATE SHIPPED (2026-11-10 owner ruling, corrects the prior face in place): AWAY cards render NO card-level `Add condition, effect…` affordance (absent, not disabled; away→Rejoin restores without reload); `EffectAdder.jsx` filters its candidate list through `isCreaturePresent` INSIDE the component (law lives in the flow for every opener); `Initiative.jsx openEffectAdder` + `createEffectAdderHandlers.js handleApplyEffect` refuse explicitly-away targets (stale/shared-flag backstop — absent rows keep prior semantics); badge-× REMOVAL stays permitted on away cards (cleanup lane). Unit-test recipe: to ride the stale-modal-away race, seed the fake sync store key `combat-ui-effectAdderTarget` as a PLAIN property name (real `useSyncedState` reads `getStore().has(prop)`, not the `${key}-${prop}` composite the mock uses) before mounting Initiative; the `deleteRuntimeKey` call is the eviction witness (the mock can't echo null back) — pinned by `Initiative.effectAdder-away-gate.test.jsx`.
- **Dice-popup payload keys land on `DiceRollResult` with zero wiring (live 2026-11-08, awayNote fix 99c3843a9):** initiative/sheet d20 popups flow producer payload → `AttackResultPopup`'s `DiceRollPopupContent` → `{...popupHtml}` spread → `DiceRollResult` props — a new producer-side key already reaches the renderer, so a render fix is just a file-local notice component mirroring the `HolyAuraSaveNotice`/amber-family idiom (e.g. `awayNote` → `.dice-roll-away-note`, icon `fa-person-walking-arrow-right`), no plumbing. Also: `away` on combatSummary rows survives a full reload + campaign re-select (two-phase pre/post-fix sessions need no re-arming).
- **Shield Bash has TWO byte-twin save-outcome producers (fixed 2026-11-08, bug-attack-rider-log-claims-applied-on-declined-or-saved):** the sheet-hit pipeline step `services/combat/steps/features/shieldBash.js logShieldBashSaveResult` (auto-fires on sheet attacks — the LIVE producer of the observed rows) and the feature-row/router lane `automation/handlers/combat/attackRiderHandler.js logShieldBashSaveOutcome` — duplicate log copy there means fix/verify BOTH lanes. Shipped honest copy: pre-chooser fail leg "X failed the STR save (DC N). Choose an effect." (chooser's commit/decline rows are the only effect claims); rider-save `ability_use` lead branches `attempted on` (success) vs `applied to` (fail) in attackRiderHandler.handleRiderSave AND versatileTricksterHandler secondary row; pinned by `attackRiderHandler.log-honesty.test.js` + save-flow/eviction/shieldBash/VT test pins. EM RE-RIDE LIVE-PASS 2026-11-08: canonical-EF sheet cycle (3-creature board + warding_bond −19/+19 dial) — fail leg row bytes `ElderPaladin failed the STR save (DC 17). Choose an effect.` paired with `Shield Bash: skipped (no effect, use not consumed).`, success leg `ElderPaladin succeeded the STR save (DC 17).` (×2), and the DS Trip save-SUCCESS lead `Trip attempted on ElderPaladin — ElderPaladin rolled 6 on DEX save (DC 15), succeeded — no effect` with cost-deducted sneak `1d6+3 [slashing] + 9d6 [Sneak Attack]`, te evicted, VT picker honest success copy — every face byte-honest; rider prompts carry `Source: Shield Bash` on the shared `.sp-source` lane (the DS Trip DEX prompt rendered no source line).
- Rider save-success eviction (2026-11-06 improve): save-gated rider legs pre-append a descriptor row to campaign-root `targetEffects` (applyRiderEffect / versatileTricksterHandler); on strict `success===true` resolution both lanes now EVICT that row (tuple-scoped target+source+option+effect) — saved Trip/Daze/etc legs leave no root residue, fail legs keep the row byte-identical and grant the condition (`attackRiderHandler.save-success-eviction.test.js`, `versatileTricksterHandler.test.js`). Audit truth is the `ability_use` row, never the te row: descriptor keys (prone/poisoned/daze/…) are UNREGISTERED in the te registry so badges skip them and `conditionToApply` folds are dead outside conditionEffects.js (census 2026-11-06). Dismissed (un-resolved) legs still park the createSaveListener promise → row kept (§5 save-dismiss lane).
- Proven clause trio: parser in `MonsterCardHelpers.js` (armed in `buildAbilitySaveRollContext`) → forwarded prop → `SaveAttackAoeModal` `resolveSaveFailGrant`/`saveProcessing` grant → registered te + ONE `addExpiration` clock (`rounds`: minutes×10, hours×600, seconds×10) + grant log. Single-target rows byte-unchanged (props default undefined = inert).
- Expiry: `addExpiration(..., rounds=undefined, expireOnCreatureName=anchor)` → `expiryRounds:null` (Infinity); anchor leg fires at anchor's NEXT turn-start (`currentRound > appliedRound`) — same-round expiry never fires; long buffs need rounds + DROP the anchor. TWO sequential addExpiration calls RACE (one clock survives) — merge into one write. Clock-fired CONDITION legs now log ONE `condition removed` row each with provenance copy (live 2026-11-07 improve, `clearExpirationEffects` 5th arg = the fired entry): `expireOnCreatureName` set → "round-anchor clock expired at X's turn start", finite `expiryRounds` → "N-round clock expired", no entry (legacy 4-arg caller) → "duration has expired"; absent-condition strips stay silent, te/buff-only clocks write nothing; hand-crafted clock + small board via curl is the cheapest live ride.
- **Rider-condition seam-clear lane truth (sheet-lane race FIXED 2026-11-10, `resolved/bug-sheet-initiative-seam-resurrects-rider-conditions.md`; per-creature law FIXED 2026-11-10, `resolved/bug-combat-seam-clears-board-wide-violate-per-creature-ruling.md`)**: **PER-CREATURE SEAM LAW (owner ruling, Paul 2026-11-10, verbatim: "EB join is not a board wide new initiative roll. EB join can be used in the middle of combat… So an initiative roll, short rest, or long rest signals old combat ending and new combat beginning for the character making the initiative roll, not for everyone")** — an initiative roll / short rest / long rest clears ONLY the actor's own conditions/expirations/self-buffs and NEVER any other creature's state; EB Join is never a clear seam at all (patrols/reinforcements join mid-combat; joiners are fresh, present creatures untouched). Mechanism: roll producers carry `detail.characterName` and every `initiative-rolled` consumer (Initiative.jsx listener, CharSummary wild-magic-surge lane, useInitiativeEffects, BlindnessDeafnessModal) clears ONLY the roller; join lanes (EB Join both branches, saved-encounter load / auto-encounter, Add-NPC-to-Initiative) dispatch DETAIL-LESS events and the per-creature lanes clear nobody on detail-less — only the campaign-level laws still ride any roll (WM-008 `_Vex_appliedTarget` latch + the b570aa359 pendingOAs drop). The board-wide `sweepNewCombatClocks` survives ONLY behind the explicit Initiative "Clear" board wipe (log copy "Combat board cleared: N combat-scoped effects ended (enforcement: zones)"); rests sweep ONLY the resting creature's own store (`sweepRestClocks(campaign, restKind, name)` → ownerName-scoped, log copy "Short rest: X's N combat-scoped effect ended"), and every rest/roll helper touching shared buckets filters to rows INVOLVING the actor (te source OR target): rest te-key clears, summon/globe/polymorph clears (`summonedBy`/`polymorphSource === actor`), Regenerate flag+restore, Wild Shape end, hp_max_reduce restore, and `clearAllExpirationEffects`'s Sanctuary strip; useInitiativeEffects' INITIATIVE_CLEARED_EFFECT_KEYS sweep + regenerate restore are roller-scoped too. Victim-side truth: clock-carried conditions (save-margin riders etc.) genuinely evict at the VICTIM'S OWN seams — short rest (restRules-shortRest atomic batch: `activeConditions:[]` + meta {} + incoming-clock drained) and long rest, and the victim's OWN roll: `clearAllExpirationEffects`'s `clearIncomingExpirations` leg correctly drains clocks living in OTHER stores that TARGET the actor (that inbound leg is victim-scoped, not board-wide); finite-round rider clocks persist across ANOTHER creature's roll and across every EB join (by law now, not merely by code accident). THE SHEET-LANE initiative re-roll (`processInitiativeRoll` → `clearAllExpirationEffects`) previously fired the clock honestly (removed rows + attacker-queue trim) while its same-tick un-awaited full-store sibling writes (clearSelfBuffs/outgoing-stamp snapshots taken pre-strip) resurrected the pre-strip `activeConditions` — MA-0809 §5 race, live-forced 1/1 via `page.route` stale-snapshot delay. Fix = short-rest idiom: the clock sweeps (incoming + self/outgoing) run FIRST so every later snapshot of the victim bucket already carries the stripped state; the condition strip itself is ONE merged `setRuntimeObject` (conditions + `activeConditionMeta` purge together — the meta row previously survived every clock expiry); all self-bucket changes (buffs, flags, queue stamp) ride ONE final merged write; pinned by `clearAllExpirationEffects.write-race.test.js` (snapshot-issue-order harness). Judge eviction by settled state, not the removed rows alone. PC-victim deterministic deep-fail at the monster-chip prompt seam: `warding_bond saveBonus:-25` in the victim's `activeBuffs` (bucket POST BEFORE campaign-select, §49 law) folds into the prompt breakdown (+5 CON +5 aura −25 = −15 shown) — guarantees margin ≥5 in one press even aura-padded. PITFALL: the seam's clearSelfBuffs CONSUMES the dial buff — re-stamp mid-session with scoped `PATCH /:campaign/<Victim> {value:{activeBuffs:[…]}}` (a settled tab adopts it live, the breakdown re-renders −25, proved ×2). NPC victims take the cs `saveBonuses` −15 stamp (inline auto-roll folds, margin ≥5 at DC 11). Per-creature pins: `encounterToInitiative.per-creature-seam.test.js` (join lanes sweep nobody + non-vacuous real-sweep contrast + rest sweep owner-scoping), `initiative-init-rolled-handler.test.jsx` (roller-only clears; detail-less join clears nobody; roller absent from board clears nobody), `CharSummary-EventHandlers.test.jsx` (foreign/join dispatches keep the surge badge), `useInitiativeEffects.effectClearing.test.js` + `.invisibilityRegen.test.js` (actor-involving te rows + roller-only regen restore), rest-suite actor-scope pins. In-view GM-card face: a monster with authored `initiative_details` shows `[data-kind="initiative"]` on its stat card (test-campaign fixtures: Wolf "+2 (12)" HAS it, Goblin has none) — pressing it is the ONLY roll producer that fires `initiative-rolled` WHILE the Initiative view is mounted, and it now clears only the roller.
- **SSE turn-start GM-authority guard (FIXED 2026-11-08, `bug-turn-start-automation-double-fires-with-second-client`): `createSseEventHandler` takes `isLocalhost` (fail-closed default false) and `applyTurnStartPass` early-returns for non-GM clients — the name echo ALWAYS precedes the executor's stamped cs (GM POSTs `activeCreatureName` at navigationHandlers:290 before the stamps), so a mounted visitor's own round gate can never dedupe and must simply not execute; visitors keep mirror reads. Visitor-tab network baseline for this lane: exactly ONE by-design `POST /__campaign__` (MapContextSync mount stamp, pinned in App.state-transitions.test.jsx) — any `/__initiative__`/`/<Char>`/`/log` POST from a visitor IS a regression witness.** SHARPENED 2026-11-08 (`bug-visitor-drains-pending-save-listener-bucket` FIXED): the `/<Char>`-POST-witness law has a pinned EXCEPTION — `expireForCreature` (expirationQueue.js) seeds a missing `pendingExpirations:[]` on every client's turn-start mirror read, visitor included (deliberate mirror-read lane); cheapest witness: seed a char bucket WITHOUT `pendingExpirations`, next seam each mounted visitor POSTs it (200). And for page-singleton `window` handler lanes whose hosts are prop-less (e.g. `useLoggedDiceRollEventHandlers.syncListenerPromptFilters`), gate with the shared `isGmHost()` (`src/services/ui/gmHost.js` — per-origin hostname law, fail-closed) instead of prop-threading: first-installer-wins makes a fail-closed prop default silently kill the GM lane.
- Runtime writes: full-store snapshot per POST — captured AT CALL TIME by setRuntimeValue/setRuntimeObject (the POST body is `Object.fromEntries(store)` at that instant), so multiple un-awaited `setRuntimeValue` in one tick LOSE all but last (MA-0809 sharpen: same-ENDPOINT `/changes/<Name>` replace-route can ALSO reorder network-side, dropping the earlier key's meta while the later key's snapshot lands — zero console errors; the sheet-initiative-seam bug 2/2 hit exactly this with ~12 pre-strip clearSelfBuffs snapshots). Fix = single merged `setRuntimeObject` or sequential awaits, latch stamp awaited BEFORE consumers read; at CLEAR seams order the state-stripping writes FIRST so every sibling snapshot taken afterwards is already honest (short-rest batch idiom, now mirrored by clearAllExpirationEffects). Deterministic race-riding harness: `page.route` the racing endpoint and DELAY every POST whose body carries the STALE content so it lands last — the reorder fires 1/1 that way (live-proved 2026-11-10; natural arrival order in the same session was 3/3 issuance-order, so never conclude "no race" from clean settles — ride the harness). Mock-surface corollary: routing a lane through `setRuntimeObject` breaks every partial `vi.mock` of `useRuntimeState.js` in the graph — a MISSING export surfaces as an UNHANDLED REJECTION (vitest exit 1 with "3187 passed / Errors 2", zero failed tests) — grep the mock factories, not just the failures. In-place array splice/push mutates store ref → dirty-check `new===old` skips POST — always spread new arrays. Same species client-side (live 2026-11-08 TV-escape improve): NEVER persist/notify an external store (`useSyncExternalStore` source or localStorage+listeners) from INSIDE a `setState` updater — React runs updaters during render and sibling subscribers throw "Cannot update a component while rendering a different component"; move side-effects into the click/handler phase.
- **Save-grant vs damage-strip sequencing race (FIXED 2026-11-11, `resolved/bug-beguiling-song-own-damage-strips-its-charm.md`):** `applyDamageToTarget` (applyDamage.js) awaits internally SEVERAL times before `removeCombatConditionsOnDamage` reads `activeConditions` — any caller that applies a save-row's damage and then grants conditions MUST await the damage legs first (SaveAttackAoeModal was calling it un-awaited while the grant ran synchronously, so the engine's Charmed-ends-on-damage strip deterministically deleted the same effect's just-granted charm; the `hp_change`-LAST log order is the fingerprint of this race — the correct order is hp_change → save-damage roll → condition applied). Canonical pattern to mirror: `saveProcessing.applySaveDamage` (awaits damage before dispatching the grant). Test idiom that pins it deterministically in jsdom: mock `applyDamageToTarget` to YIELD, then strip from a store-faithful runtime stub, asserting the grant survives; reusable for sibling grant-order bugs. Side law shipped same pass: `removeCombatConditionsOnDamage` purges the stripped condition's `activeConditionMeta[key]` row in ONE merged `setRuntimeObject` write (stripConditionsMerged idiom) — condition strips never orphan meta rows.
- Round latches `_<Feature>_usedRound` on `playerStats.name`/monster name, round from FRESH `getCombatContext`; clear at round-wrap in BOTH initiative.jsx + navigationHandlers beside `_Slow_Fall_usedRound`; monster latches must NOT rely on PLAYER_ROUND_LATCH_KEYS (player-only clear).
- Log conventions: every resolution logs — spends `ability_use` (characterName+abilityName+description), refusals `automation` + `<feature>_refused` (+reason token) zero-spend, `save_result`, `roll attack/roll damage`, `condition applied` with source meta, `automation blocked`. Special-Actions grid NEVER flushes handler `logEntries` — `addEntry` DIRECTLY in handler. `{type:'modal'}` returns bypass the flusher too — modal's own confirm handler must flush.
- `isWithinRange` (rangeCheck.js) + `rangeToFeet` (rangeValidation.js) for every range gate; gridless/positionless = LENIENT (consulted-and-passes is enough); phantom/tokenless targets CANNOT be gated — record advisory distance in log.
- DCs: `dc_success:"none"` consumed by MonsterCardModal getSaveDcSuccess (default `half` hardcode MV-20); `buildSaveDc` accepts `saveDc:'ability'` token — never bake numeric DC before abilities computed; saveBonuses keys LOWERCASE; EB join strips monsters.json immunities (cs.immunities partial) — look up monsters.json by cs name. Picker `computeNpcSave` reads `saveBonuses[saveType.toLowerCase()]` = `'dexterity'` while live cs keys are `'dex'` → NPC-picker saves roll raw-d20 vs DC; PC-prompt path is the live seam; harness mocks must key `'dexterity'` (MA-0303).
- Re-validate hit/AC honestly: popup may print BASE AC while resolver used buffed `effectiveAc` — judge HIT/MISS boundary + log `targetAc/coverLevel/coverAcBonus`; converted MISS popups still print "✓ HIT" — log `hit:false` decides.
- Complexity ceiling 15 is real (zero warnings) — move parsing to helpers/child components; a null-when-idle file-local wrapper component (friendly-fire/MusicToast idiom) clears the cost of adding inline modal render branches. Initiative.jsx additionally sits at the max-statements cap (60) — pay for a new statement by deleting a redundant one (confirm-bridge factories should BE the useState setter, payload = pending value, not a pass-through wrapper); App.jsx is capped the same way (live 2026-11-11 improve back-to-maps-label: statements inside nested callbacks/effect bodies count against the CALLBACK, only direct-body declarations cost against `App` — consolidating a ref+state mirror into one hook call reclaimed budget); even one `?.` added inside its JSX tips the complexity-15 ceiling — hoist such predicates to module scope (precedent: `isActiveEconomyRowGmDriven`, 2026-11-10). While a DeleteConfirmModal is open over an editor there are TWO "Cancel" buttons — always scope dialog clicks via `within(getByRole('dialog'))`. Adding an export to a heavily-mocked util breaks ~12 partial `vi.mock`s — extend every partial mock (`importActual`); `vi.clearAllMocks()` keeps implementations — use `mockReset`; leaked `mockResolvedValue(false)` kills later tests — re-stub per-key.
- Legendary economy (reusable, MA-0021/0070/0218): header dict must author numeric `uses:N` → `monsterLegendaryUses.js` expend/refuse/regain via turnStartEffects; children auto-gate; prose weapon child `delegates_to:"<Weapon>"`; prose spellcast child needs NUMERIC spell fields copied (delegates_to:"Spellcasting" misfires through save leg); header+children MUST be fixed SAME pass (else "Expend Legendary" chip silently BURNS uses on inert rows, console.error "no resolvable mechanic"). Lair: structured dicts with `name` clickable; raw-string/nameless = inert; zone dict shape `{name,zone:{radius_ft,no_save,effect_key,noun,advisory},duration}`; advisory `{name,advisory:"<snake_key>",description}` → record-only log "initiative 20 (GM-enforced)". No initiative-20/lair-recurring consumer app-wide.
- **Save-dismiss outcome (fixed 2026-11-05)**: the un-rolled branch of SavePromptModal `handleDismiss` dispatches a dedicated `save-dismissed` window event carrying `promptId` — NOT a `save-result` (~40 `createSaveListener().promise` consumers read falsy `success` as "failed → apply damage"; resolving them on dismiss would over-deal damage). `createSaveListener` detaches its window listener on dismissal. Producers opt in by listening for `save-dismissed` (DivineSparkModal template: route to its `refuse()` helper → refusal view + working Done + `<slug>_refused` log row; charge stays spent — §9 pay-early family). Dismiss is the ONLY un-rolled footer button (post-roll swaps to Done/Next Save — `handleDone`'s no-result branch is unreachable). FIXED SAME PASS 2026-11-05: CharReactions beguiling-twist + saveOnlyHandler produce `*_refused`/`save_dismissed` rows and detach both listeners on dismiss. Census: all remaining raw `save-result` producers are leak-only (guid/promptId-gated, can never fire wrong — no wrong-state producers found); modal-lane ones (StepsOfTheFeyTaunt, AreaEffectTargetModalBase, MassSuggestion, SaveAttackAoe, Eyebite family) SWEPT 2026-11-05 (improve save-result-producer-census): full 28-file census classified, 15 modal-parking sites opted into `save-dismissed` via the DivineSparkModal pattern + shared helpers `logSaveDismissed` (savePrompt.js) and `useSaveDismissedListener`/`dismissedNoEffectResult` (AreaEffectSaveFlow.utils.js) — neutral `{dismissed:true, success:true, total:0}` rows drain AoE queues instead of freezing; per-site `.save-dismissed.test.jsx` ledgers guard promptId gating. Test doctrine: listener-ledger tests MUST mint unique promptIds per test — shared mock promptIds poison later ledgers (stale listeners remove/count on later dispatches).
- **createSaveListener ledger-test guid monotonicity (live 2026-11-08, save-result-attribution fix):** a `createSaveListener` test that never dispatches leaves its window handler attached forever — mock `guid()` ids must be **monotonic across the whole file** (resetting the counter in `beforeEach` makes a later dispatch resolve the stale listener and double-log). Sharpens the §save-dismiss promptId-uniqueness doctrine.
- **Second-turn (ST) phantom-bucket law — chokepoints and pinned-bug test audit (fixed 2026-11-09, `bug-secondturn-allies-editor-phantom-bucket` + `bug-secondturn-badge-removal-synthetic-name`):** every per-card lane on a `(Second Turn)` row must remap `isSecondTurnEntry(creature) && creature.holderName → holderName` at its chokepoint before any change-data write or audit (precedents: `openEffectAdder` c5883e4a4; allies editor fixed in `useAllyEditor.js` `openAllyEditor` — one edit there covers modal title, `currentAllies` read, `setAllyList` write AND audit name simultaneously, not `createCreatureHandlers`; condition-badge × fixed in `createAutoBreakConditionHandler.js` — the LIVE × lane is `CreatureCard.jsx ConditionBadgeRow` → `handleAutoBreakCondition(creature.name, cond)` which must remap via the cs row it already looks up before `removeCondition` + `logConditionEvent`; the `ConditionEffectBadges.jsx REMOVE_HANDLERS.condition` branch is DEAD code — grep-zero `removeKey` producers app-wide, don't fix there), and candidate lists must `.filter(c => !isSecondTurnEntry(c))` (law exists in `CreatureCard.jsx` target list / `useTvPresentation.js`). When fixing any ST-law bug, first grep `src/components/initiative/` tests for assertions that ENshRINE synthetic-name behavior — a green suite can mean the buggy face is pinned (the pre-fix `useAllyEditor.test.js` roster test actively asserted "FULL roster including … second-turn entries"). Adjacent lane FIXED same run 2026-11-09 (d55d4160a): the same badge's CLICK (condition-save reroll) now remaps inside `handleRollConditionSave` (`createRollConditionSaveHandler.js`) — see the ST-row lane authority bullet for the face proof.
- **Modal-family dismiss lane + spell-grant rig (live-proved 2026-11-06 run DC, BlindnessDeafnessModal)**: AreaEffectTargetModalBase consumers receive the un-rolled Dismiss through the base's `save-dismissed` listener (attached only while `processing:true`); the consumer's neutral `dismissedNoEffectResult` row satisfies the all-resolved gate → Done appears (no "Resolving…" freeze). LANE-TRANSPORT LAW: spell-modal lanes queue via DIRECT `sendSavePrompt` — only the `savePrompt-<Target>-<promptId>` arrival key materializes; `pendingSavePrompts`/`pendingSaveListenerPrompts` buckets are NEVER armed there (drain proof = arrival-key DELETE; the bucket-evict doctrine is `createSaveListener`-lane only) and the rolled verdict lands as the RESOLVED `roll`/`save-damage` row's `saveResult:"success|failure"`+total+bonus+formula (NO `save_result` row, NO `saveResult-<T>` key — those belong to `sendSaveResult`/`createSaveListener` writers). Double-fire probe: re-dispatch a queue-shaped `save-dismissed` with a non-matching promptId while the resolved modal is open AND after Done — both inert (count refused rows across casts). GRANT RECIPE (sanctioned, e2e-explore pitfall 10): append the spell-name string to the fixture's disk `spells[]` (surgical str.replace + json.loads validate), wait past the ~2s debounce, GET `/<Char>.json` for disk truth, hard reload + campaign re-select → the `td.left.spell-name.clickable` sheet row auto-derives for a 2024 prepared cleric (zero preparedSpells authoring); slot pays PRE-modal at "Cast Spell" (GET the char bucket's `spell_slots_level_N` mid-chooser); the modal's own target picker (`label.abjure-target-row` input → `.sp-roll-btn` "(N target)") means NO cs.targetName arming is needed and gridless = lenient; seed caster AND victim `activeConditions:[]` before first cast post-clear (victim bucket needs full-store POST to CREATE before scoped PATCH works); restore = cp backup over the file + md5-equality (public/campaigns is GITIGNORED — git porcelain there is a weak check) + closed-tab clears restore the paid slots with the bucket.

- **Shared-modal family-convention changes (live 2026-11-13 improve modal-escape-dismiss):** the real work is STALE PINS — consumer wrapper tests may pin the INVERSE convention (`FriendlyFireConfirmModal`/`ClearCombatConfirmModal`/`MapsManager` each asserted "backdrop/Escape do NOT cancel" before the Escape/backdrop ship); grep the overlay class + `Escape` across ALL consumer test folders before declaring a shared-modal change done. Reusable backdrop-drag guard: overlay `onMouseDown` stores `target.closest('.modal-shell')` in a ref, overlay `onClick` dismisses only when NEITHER the click target NOR the recorded mousedown was inside the panel, resetting the flag on every bubbled click (synthetic `fireEvent.click`-only probes still dismiss). Probe gotcha: `expect(screen.getByRole(...)).not.toBeInTheDocument()` THROWS before the assertion can pass — dismissed-state probes need `queryByRole`. LIVE INHERITANCE PROVED (BE descent 2026-11-13): all five ridden consumers answer Escape + a pure backdrop click at (15,15) with zero-write dismiss — reject-draft (row stays `draft:true`, no audit row), Delete Map (row survives), Clear Initiative Board (13-row board intact), Friendly-Fire (arm DISARMS: the subject's `<select aria-label="<Name> target">` value reverts to `''`, cs `targetName` absent), typed-name campaign delete (confirm already gated-disabled; meta stays 200) — while every confirm leg (`Yes, Delete Permanently`/`Yes, Clear Board`/`Yes, Arm Target`) lands normally; drag-guard live rig: `mouse.move` onto the dialog `<p>` → down → move ~30px outside the panel with steps → up — dialog STAYS open; a browser viewport-null `page.viewportSize()` doesn't block the corner-click face.

## 6. Fingerprints (pre-diagnosis — confirm live before fixing)
- Flat integer damage ("Hit: 1 Slashing", no `damage_dice_primary`) → zero damage silently (MA-0322/0364/0332) — FIXED MA-0322 app-wide: `extractFlatHitDamage` (MonsterCardHelpers, dice-paren guard `\(\s*\d+d\d+` — non-dice parentheticals must not block) → autoDamageFormula "N" → `resolveAutoDamageResult` (dice-less log, flat NEVER doubles on crit); constants "N" remain unrollable by rollExpression — Route A data "damage_dice_primary:\"1\"" hits MA-0014 block; siblings just verify live.
- "Varies (WIS,CON,…)" save_type / multi-dice strings → unparseable save-shell, mod-0 "VAR" (MA-0374/0383 family) — all such rows FAIL.
- Unparseable damage chip ("+spell level"): `canRollExpression` gate suppresses; chip w/o parse = click dies silently (MA-0014).
- Damageless saves: `applyFailedSaveConditions` once stranded no-damage rows (MA-0014/0090 family) — NOW damage+condition rows apply fine (MV-27); damageless spellcasting save-legs still route advisory when `spellHasDamage` false (MA-0348); save_effect prose must byte-carry canonical condition word else `extractConditionsFromSaveEffect` → [] (MV-31).
- Non-canonical prose clauses (cursed/fortify/haunted…) → no te unless a parser exists; MA-0090 = FAIL class: failed save leaves ZERO state; proof = `saveResult-<T>` success:false + no te + log holds only `ability_use`.
- Prose-only save DC ("spell save DC 23" in description, no numeric `save_dc`) → prompt "DC Unknown", damage abandoned; prose fallback exists only for attack bonus "+N to hit" (`monsterSpellAttackBonus`) — DATA fix (MA-0237/0318/0328/0362).
- Spell ATTACK misroutes to save path when 5e spells.json lacks `attack_type` (findMonsterSpell 5e-first) → fix `attack_type:"ranged"` + numeric row DC (MA-0245).
- Save-path secondary damage: WAS inert; NOW threaded (MA-0427) via `buildSecondaryDamageTransport` → save options → `applySecondarySaveDamageLeg` (separate roll, per-leg half/full via `computeDamageAfterSave`/`Evasion`, own save-damage log + DualDamageNotice copy). Legacy rows without `damage_dice_secondary` byte-identical. `autoDamageSecondaryFormula` remains the separate ATTACK-path seam — do not conflate.
- Spellcasting chips need `<strong>`/`<em>` markup — plain-text lists = zero affordances (MA-0421); fake chips from prose emphasis ("**Concentration**") cosmetic; tier header must parse "N/Day Each:" — qualifier parenthetical inside header kills gating (MA-0276); 1/Day uses = `monsterSpellUses` key + `automation blocked` refusal; At-Will ungated by design; 1/Day re-arm GM-side (no rest consumer).
- Legendary header fingerprint (MA-0092/0217/0375/0405): "3 (4 in Lair)" is NAME TEXT; no numeric `uses` → no counter, ungated multi-fire, economy consumers exist but unreached = DATA FAIL. Numeric legendary save chips bypass spend when header lacks uses (MA-0184). Multi-defect prose-threshold rows (MA-0352): threshold kill ungated + half-default + name-text "(1/Day)" = three defects one row.
- Grapple: attack-HIT grapple prose only lands via authored `hit_conditions:[...]`+`escape_dc` (MA-0010 seam; plain `conditions:[]` NOT consumed); save-leg constrict grapple WORKS live (MA-0368); sustained grapple state-machine zero producers (MA-0287/0288/0354); monster auto-adv-vs-grappled prose inert (PC-feat-gated).
- Monster reactions: no-affordance rows (no attack_bonus/save_dc/dice) = inert unless automation `{type,trigger,effect}` authored — RAW-unlimited honest sentinel `usage:"At Will"`+`uses:999`+1/round latch (MA-0006/0300/0305); gated reactions key ONLY off `automation.effect`; monster Counterspell fixed MA-0013 (gate RAW lastAttack spell-origin; read RAW not normalized wrapper).
- Recharge IS enforced when authored (`monsterRecharge.js`+`monsterRechargeGate`, spend at picker-open, d6 at owner turn-start, `<slug>_refused`) — do NOT pre-assume inert (MA-0100/0225/0236); recharge on ATTACK rows gated MA-0294; re-confirm of already-open picker bypasses gate (known edge). Missing field per-row still = FAIL. Ungated repeat-fire (MA-0406 decay) = missing spend/spend-gate.
- AoE shapes: Cone/Line/Radius parse (`breathAoeShape`) → SaveAttackAoeModal picker; "N-foot Emanation" does NOT parse → single-target degradation (MA-0317); Line picker gate = MAX feet token (MA-0064); picker PRE-CHECKS can drop targets to 0 → `.dsp-overlay`.
- `dc_success:"half"` default leaks half on condition-only-success rows (MV-20/MA-0218/0229) — fix DATA `dc_success:"none"` (honest copy both surfaces). Trap rows where save gates a non-damage clause need explicit discriminator, `dc_success:none` insufficient (MA-0298 banishing-claw).
- Caster-dependent EB joins (animate-objects Slam MA-0284/0285): no affordance route (chip needs numeric attack_bonus; no action-level advisory; spell_attack_bonus resolves Spellcasting links only) — adjudicated SKIPPED, honest inert preferred. Attack-wording row + null attack_bonus → chip suppressed by `attackRowMissingToHit` (MA-0286).
- Conditional charge damage = `conditional_damage:{dice,modifier,damage_type,condition}` + HIT-popup offer `ChargeBonusOffer` (MA-0007). Variant clauses (choose-one effects) = chooser modal + parser + grants; MA-0275 template below.
- Multiattack = header TEXT row, components are individual chips; count enforcement GM-adjudicated (MA-0009/0223); replace-with-spell cast level parses the Spellcasting ROW description (MA-0223).
- Staged ladders: sleep `staged_sleep:{unconscious_minutes}` (MA-0068), paralysis analogue `staged_paralysis` (MA-0248 paralyzingBreathService), roar `staged_roar`+maxUses stage-swap (MA-0268 — swap description too or re-extraction wrong dice). Frightful Presence = full service `frightfulPresenceService.js` w/ OBJECT `success_immunity`+`repeat_save` (booleans kill it, MA-0147), 24h immunity te, EOT repeat seam.
- Speed/AC/stat clauses live via registered te: `speed_half` (MA-0073), `speed_zero` (MA-0146), `ac_penalty` value:N (MA-0115), `no_reactions`+`no_action_and_bonus_action`+`speed_half` = "Slowed" trio (MA-0087 — slowed is NOT a registered condition, don't add it), `concentration_disadvantage` (MA-0038), `giggling_magic_debuff` subtract-die (MA-0093/0207/0212 per-monster die — fix producer not consumer), `weakening_breath` (MA-0102), `push` marker te (MA-0079/0138 push-only hoist), `no_healing` (MA-0016), `banished_demiplane`/`lair_dream_plane` (MA-0104/0107), `gaze_immunity` (MA-0030), `target_prerequisite:{conditions,by_attacker}` gate (MA-0019), `marked_as_prey`/`pesky_swarm` (MA-0275).
- te `next_attack_advantage`+vexTarget granted BY a hit self-erases in same attack's post-processing (WM-008); PRE-attack te consumed regardless of hit. Attacker-side adv channel = `vexTarget`.
- FT-007 **Boon of Combat Prowess conversion FIXED 2026-11-06** (`bug-boon-combat-prowess-hit-flag-not-persisted`): conversion now writes back `campaign.lastAttack` + `<attacker>.lastAttackRoll` at conversion time (before Done) with `hit:true, missConverted:true, convertedBy` via `persistConvertedAttackHit` (CharSheet.handlers.js, merged re-read/write lane); the damage merge preserves the flags and `lastAttack.hit` riders (Stunning Strike et al.) admit converted hits. Stroke-of-Luck attack-face conversion rides the same helper. Re-arm gotcha for conversion tests: the boon button vanishes once converted for the rest of the round — bump `combatSummary.round` via scoped PATCH to re-arm for a second conversion. The MISS-popup boon button label renders with a LEADING SPACE (` Boon of Combat Prowess`) — exact-text probes must trim (live 2026-11-06). Feature rows that queue a save can ALSO emit the `automation_info` popup AND the `.sp-overlay` prompt together (Stunning Strike, live 2026-11-06) — close the popup's Done to reach Roll Save/Dismiss.
- **Sheet-modal two-channel merge law (FIXED 2026-11-06, `bug-sheet-nick-mastery-hit-drops-damage-stage`):** CharSheet renders modals from `{...modalState, ...spellModalState}` where the parent mirror (`charActionsModalState`, update-REPLACED by the host) merges LAST and wins. A modal opened by the pipeline/hook-internal setter writes `modalState` ONLY, so any lingering same-key close-`null` that a modal forwarded into the mirror permanently vetoes the mount — the offer/log fires (`modal:shown`, correct rider payload) while ZERO overlays mount and the paused damage stage never dispatches (✓ HIT, no damage row, no `hp_change`, no console error; reload clears it, one healthy cycle per load). Diagnose by tracing the offer broadcast vs overlay count, not the rider handler. Rule: opens must round-trip BOTH channels, or a mirror `null` must never veto a live local open — enforced by `mergeModalStates` (`char-sheet/modalStateMerge.js`) at both merge sites. Also watch `_cunningStrikeCostUsed`: a jammed cycle can let a full-store snapshot resurrect its pre-reset value.
- No consumers app-wide (advisory/GM-enforced, do NOT rebuild without ticket): grapple state-machine, light/vision levels, fall damage, grid token move, range bands on spells, lair initiative-20, monster 8h recast-block, wall objects, poison "can't regain HP", monster rest-rearm of spell/recharge keys, prone registers no expiry on damageless cones (persistent-prone standard), "shake awake" 5-ft.
- Healing-pool AMOUNT lane full-HP pool spend + fabricated/self-zero audit rows — LIVE-CONFIRMED 2026-11-13 both faces, FIXED 2026-11-13 (commit 4f8c5d9fa, `bug-amount-heal-lane-charges-pool-on-full-hp-target`): `applyHeal` now re-reads capacity at commit, charges/logs only actual HP restored, and refuses zero-capacity presses (`.healing-restriction-note` `<Target> is already at full HP. No pool spent.`, button disabled on `noHealCapacity` dice-parity, Channel Divinity burn moved after success).

## 7. Victims & rigs
- **Save-margin-rider live face recipe (live 2026-11-10 fix run):** with ElderPaladin on the board EVERY PC victim's save prompt carries `+5 (Aura of Protection)` (AberrantSorcerer CON −1+prof shows = +10 — fail-by-5 math must net it; FeyRanger nets +4 ⇒ d20 ≤ DC−9, ~10%/press RNG). ONE-SHOT instead: §14 deterministic force-fail (cs `saveBonuses.con:-15` + nested `saving_throws`, full-store POST) guarantees FAIL with margin ≥ 5 in one press. Rider-only save chips (e.g. Thri-Kreen Bite `DC 11 Constitution`, `[data-kind="save"]` inside `.mc-action:has(strong:text("Bite"))`): arm via the ATTACKER initiative card's own `[data-testid="target-select"]` — it lives OUTSIDE `.mc-overlay` (probing selects inside the overlay returns [] = false negative). Save RESULTS render inside the `.sp-modal`/`.sp-overlay` itself (Done button), NOT `[data-testid="popup-overlay"]` — an immediate post-Roll DOM read can honestly come back empty; ground-truth every verdict from `type:'save_result'` log rows (success/total/saveDc fields). One chip press queues one prompt; Done-drain before the next press or prompts stack "(1 of N)".
- **Save-prompt direct-injection rig (2026-11-06 improve):** a prompt materializes live from a plain API POST `/api/campaigns/:c/savePrompt-<Target>-<promptId>` `{value:{promptId,targetName,saveType,saveDc,sourceName,dcSuccess:'none'}}` — no combat producer needed; Dismiss/Done drain + DELETE the key. Aura-co-presence faces need a settled client mirror: a full-store combatSummary POST followed within ~2s by a prompt can read `getCombatSummary()==null` (party-split coverage drops the aura chip entirely — transient, not a defect); re-POST, wait ≥3s or reload, and verify the modal names the intended target (queued leftovers render "(1 of N)" with the STALE front prompt's numbers). Phantom-ElderPaladin rig: active map with ZERO positioned tokens (poi-showcase) → isWithinRange gridless-fallback lenient → phantom bearer pads; on a positioned-token map (test-map/battle-arena) an unplaced bearer CANNOT pad (rangeCheck `unplaced → false`) so the "assumed co-present (no token)" tooltip is only observable on the zero-positioned-token face. MIRROR face (live 2026-11-06 run DB, sandbox map with ONLY an ElderPaladin token): a TOKENED bearer still fails to pad an UNPLACED target — aura chip drops entirely ("CON +6 = +6", no bonus, no caveat) — rangeCheck unplaced-ness kills either leg once the map is positioned. Board-member-guard faces: `getCombatSummary` reads combatData `cachedCombatSummaries` — since the App-level fix (`syncCombatSummaryCache` in App.jsx, bug-aura-caveat-asserts-for-on-board-bearer-stale-cache) the shared `change-<campaign>-combatSummary` SSE lane keeps it live on EVERY tab (board echo adopted, null echo evicts; ~1–3s settle), so a sheet-only tab now sees mid-session board writes and no Initiative visit is needed to judge the guard; the Initiative view refreshes it too and its merged write still wins there. LIVE-BREAKDOWN face (2026-11-06 improve): the injected prompt renders the full `sp-preroll-bonus` breakdown line for a real char with ZERO combat (ElderPaladin direct-injection: "DEXTERITY +3 +5 (Aura of Protection) = +8" — her own aura pad lands boardless); mid-prompt buff recipe = create the victim bucket FIRST (full-store POST `{value:{activeBuffs:[]}}`), inject the prompt, then scoped `PATCH /:campaign/:key {value:{activeBuffs:[{effect:'warding_bond',saveBonus:-19,…}]}}` (GET-verify — applied:0 lie) → the header chip+sum re-render WITHOUT any reload (useRuntimeValue subscription), and the roll folds the same store. The resolved save lastAttack record is now `d20+bonus===total` honest (pre-fix it carried a base+aura-only bonus while total said otherwise).
- **Aura drop-note + phantom-caveat board rig (2026-11-06 improve):** the target-unpositioned drop note rides ONLY the zero-envelope `{bonus:0, sourceName:null, targetUnpositionedBearer}` (server-side consumers read `bonus`/`sourceName` and stay byte-inert); displayed via `buildPreRollBreakdown.auraUnmeasuredNote` `.sp-note`. Rig caveat when hand-rolling the combat board for the PHANTOM caveat face: board membership EXEMPTS the bearer (exclude the bearer from `creatures` or the caveat won't render) while the TARGET must be on the board as `type:'player'`; a single POST carrying both silently produces the no-caveat face — re-POST without the bearer, wait ≥3s settle, re-inject the prompt. Stale Playwright refs after a view-swap mis-click nav buttons — re-find fresh refs before destructive clicks.
- ElderPaladin: AC19, saves padded +5 Aura of Protection, has PSYCHIC + RADIANT resistance (never clean half-on-save victim; "reduced from N" = rig not row). Reliable CHA success victim lv20 (+11 vs DC24).

AberrantSorcerer WIS −1 forced-fail; its CON save shows TRUE proficient bonus (+4) — read the prompt.

Plan one conversion per rest; later cycles need natural hits (~55% at +9 vs AC19) or a fresh short rest. Rides the same lane as the warding_bond win/lose save dial for full rider-matrix faces.

Drain per press: Dismiss→Done / Cancel, ≤5 bounded passes.

It also locks the ATTACKER side: sheet-lane damage writes the TARGET's RUNTIME bucket (sheet truth), so hand-topping the board `currentHp` does NOT save you — after ~10 hits the victim's bucket hits 0 and the `.dsp-overlay` intercepts every `.attacks` row click on the attacker's own sheet; cure = `PATCH /<Victim> {value:{currentHitPoints:<sheet max>}}` (read the real max from the bucket's `hitPoints`).

An un-armed press still rolls+logs attack/damage and silently writes a junk `hp_change` `{targetName:null,delta:0}` (live 2026-11-06).

HAM attribution comes from the `wearing_heavy_armor` condition (`getDamageReductionDetails` in automationPassives.js), NOT `auto.name` — 2024 HAM disk rows carry the generic benefit name "Damage Reduction".

## 9. Known-accepted residuals (do not chase; note in reports)
- **Clear REGENERATES bare +NPC placeholders (pinned by tests, by design)**: `handleClear` → `clearCombat(characters, numOfNpc)` re-seeds `numOfNpc` = count of `isBareNpcPlaceholder` rows on the pre-clear board, so "NPC 1" survives every Clear (renamed rows/EB monsters don't carry over); the victory stamp even counts the carried placeholder as a foe. Card-count "unchanged" after a confirmed Clear is NOT a clear bug — remove via `.npc-remove-btn` + native confirm (verified live EP 2026-11-08). Copy honesty improve (2026-11-08): the Clear confirm now SAYS placeholders are kept/re-seeded (old native string promised "removes all monsters"); buttons/contract unchanged, pinned by `ClearCombatConfirmModal.test.jsx`.
- **Victory banner auto-hides the instant foes rejoin the board** (`victoryVisible = lastCombatResult && !trackerHasFoes && undismissed`): the "+NPC then Clear" repro kills the banner before step 2 — ride the z-tier/repro with a FOES-FREE board (verified live EP 2026-11-08, 5007e31dd PASS: banner z=150, dialog confirm hit-tests clean). RECIPE REFINED (2026-11-08 improve banner-controls-overlap): on a PRISTINE foes-free (party-only) board ONE Clear already stamps the result and mounts the banner — the double-Clear is only needed when a placeholder/foe survives the first clear. Layout law now pinned (`VictoryBanner.no-capture.test.jsx`): card rides an upper band (`align-items:flex-start` + vh top offset), card BODY passes pointer events through — only `.victory-close` (×) and Continue capture.
- Free-cast/stamp leak family + AoE-save Cancel keeps paid charge (slot-rollback ticket owed). Concentration spells that skip storage.set stale. `remove_*` expiration types may lack cases → effects persist until rest. Un-authored 1/Day monster keys survive LR. Monster 8h-block advisory. Spell-attack context seam (cover/melee-disadv/range-band) absent — reverse Spell Sniper leak into weapons live. `spiritual_weapon` PC seams. Androsphinx MA-0269-71 + MA-0259 manifest stableKey re-stamps owned by orchestrator.

- Bug files belong in `.opencode/plans/`, never `docs/`.

## 10. Monster feature seams — byte-shape templates
- **Versatile two-handed**: text "or X (dice) if used with two hands" MUST be `damage_dice_two_handed` (chooser arms ONLY on this key, MonsterCardHelpers.js:742). Same text authored as `damage_dice_secondary` = engine ADDS it every hit (additive transport buildSecondaryDamageTransport MonsterCardModal.jsx:831-845, unconditional) = over-dealt FAIL(a). Placement byte-twins: Azer/Drider/Quarterstaff; two-handed leg rides primary type. Examples: half-ogre Battleaxe "2d10 + 3", merfolk Spear modifier-less "1d8", kuo-toa Spear. Chooser variant buttons SHARE class `dice-roll-reroll-btn` with Done — target Done by exact text "Done"; unpicked Done collapses to applied-damage with `one_handed_variant_selected` default log; picked variant logs `two_handed_variant_selected`. Wrong-slot sweep caution: "plus X" additive riders (Azer Warhammer, Drow Mage Staff) are LEGIT secondary — verify conjunction (plus vs or) before flipping. Family census: "if used with two hands" + secondary = 3 wrong-slot hits (MA-1063 sweep).
- **Save-margin two-band**: shallow band in `save_effect` + `save_margin:{fails_by:N,also:"cond"}` (key `fails_by`, NOT failsBy) → parseSaveMarginClause (Helpers:332) → applySaveMarginRider (saveProcessing:665/:679). Deep-band fail-by-≥N grants both conditions + ONE merged clock whose rounds derive from the row's AUTHORED duration (owner ruling 2026-11-10, 1 round = 6 s: minutes×10 / hours×600 — Thri-Kreen/Homunculus "1 minute" → rounds:10, drow/Pseudodragon "1 hour" → 600, duration-less rows fall back 600; parser forwards `duration` incl. the Homunculus composite-row description fallback) + log "failed the save by N". Clock face machine truth (live GC 2026-11-10, Thri-Kreen −19-dial deep fail): the merged clock rides the ATTACKER bucket `pendingExpirations` as `{target, effects:[{type:'condition',condition:'poisoned'},{...rider}], appliedRound, expiryRounds, expireOnCreatureName:null}` — check the ATTACKER, not the victim; victim own sheet `span.clickable "Initiative: +N"` reroll is the clean-evict seam (removed rows carry provenance in `reason`: "Poisoned ends (10-round clock expired)"; settled `activeConditions:[]`+`meta:{}`+attacker queue trimmed, NO pre-strip resurrection post-6aea00d8c; a same-seam bystander's Poisoned survives — per-creature law). WITHOUT save_margin, extractConditionsFromSaveEffect whole-string word-scan grants ALL named conditions on EVERY fail = live OVER-grant (not advisory). Machine proof of spray: lastAttack.saveConditions carries both words. Byte-shape anchor: MA-1000 homunculus / MA-1351 pseudodragon Sting / MA-1584 thri-kreen.
- **Rest lane law + selectors (live run HD 2026-11-10, semantics UPDATED in place 2026-11-10 improve rest-modal-dialog-semantics)**: sheet Short/Long Rest buttons are matched by `title` prefix ("Short Rest: spend Hit Dice…"), NOT text; the ShortRestModal is `.short-rest-overlay.no-print`/`.short-rest-modal` — the shell NOW CARRIES `role="dialog"` + `aria-modal="true"` + `aria-labelledby="short-rest-modal-title"` → the visible `<h3>` "Short Rest" (pinned by `ShortRestModal.dialog-semantics.test.jsx`), so the HD-era `[role=dialog]` FALSE-NEGATIVE is closed and a dialog probe resolves it; for CONTENT assertions still probe `.short-rest-modal` directly (note UPDATED in place 2026-11-10 improve healingpool-hoh-dialog-semantics: `HealingPoolModal`/`HandOfHealingModal` still BORROW `.short-rest-overlay/.short-rest-modal` for non-rest flows, but every borrowed shell now carries the same dialog tier — HP main shell `aria-labelledby="healing-pool-modal-title"` → its `<h3>`; picker shells labelled by `SecondaryTargetModal`'s visible `.sp-header` via its new optional `titleId` prop (additive — other consumers render no id); HoH result/error shells → `hand-of-healing-modal-title` `<h3>` — so a by-class probe and a `[role=dialog]` probe AGREE on this lane (live-verified: `everyClassIsDialog` true while HP picker/main and Short Rest open); the `.short-rest-overlay` backdrops stay role-free, mirroring ShortRestModal). Lane reachability census (2026-11-10 improve): HealingPool rides live via canonical ElderPaladin (`b.clickable` "Lay On Hands:" auto-derives from 2024 classes.json lv1 row, `type:'healing_pool'`, no range → 60ft lenient → picker branch fires on the 13-PC board; Skip/confirm/Done all behave, pool 100→99 + row-then-clear); `HandOfHealingModal` is UNREACHABLE on canonical chars — its only producer is 2024 Monk→Warrior of Mercy→"Hand of Healing" (`healExpression:"martial_arts_die + WIS modifier"`, the sole expression matching healingHandler's `isMonkHealing` predicate in BOTH ruleset datasets; 5e authors none) and the cast's only Monk is Open Hand — semantics pinned at unit level in `HandOfHealingModal.dialog-semantics.test.jsx` (picker/result/error stages). Long Rest has NO bespoke modal — it executes directly via `applyLongRest` (logs the `long_rest` row immediately); its only mountable modal is the shared `CreatureSelectionModal` riding `.sp-overlay/.sp-modal` chrome. Buttons: `Roll One` / `Roll All (N)` / `Complete Short Rest` / `Cancel`. COMPLETE is the only commit point (mount/preview writes nothing): it stamps a `type:'short_rest'` log row (`"<Name> takes a short rest. | Hit Dice: N used | Resources restored: …"`) and clears the RESTING ACTOR's `activeConditions` to `[]` (restRules-shortRest.js:336) while every BYSTANDER's conds bucket stays byte-identical (live 2/2: WSD rested → `[]`, curl-seeded ElderPaladin `['poisoned']` survived — the per-creature seam ruling f15774f9c holds on rest faces too). NOTE the resting actor's runtime bucket also gets re-baked to sheet truth on mount (currentHitPoints ← sheet-derived HP), so any curl HP seed is gone post-mount — GET-verify conds, not HP, for rest-law assertions. LONG REST live faces (first ridden 2026-11-11 run IE): the `Long Rest:` title-prefix button commits INSTANTLY — zero modal mounts inside 1.5 s (bc409413d's "bespoke-modal-free" holds) — actor `activeConditions []` AND `activeConditionMeta {}` (the meta purge sweeps even orphan rows left by other lanes); `LONG_REST_TARGET_EFFECT_CLEAR_KEYS` family live-filtered (a `blur` te ON the resting actor evicts, a bystander-targeted row byte-survives); bystander bucket untouched; honest row `long_rest` message `"<Name> takes a long rest. | Resources restored: … | Star Map free casts: 3 | Cosmic Omen Star Map: 3 → Woe"` (LR re-arm lanes visible in copy). Seed te witnesses via `POST /:campaign/targetEffects {value:[te,…]}` before pressing.
- **monster_summon** (MA-0648/0757 template, byte-shape per galeb-duhr row): `automation:{type:"monster_summon",options:[...]}` + constant numeric `count` (count must skip dice path — rollExpression(2) throws, guard live) + option-level `stat_override:{int:1,cha:1}` + `exclude_actions` + numeric `uses`/`maxUses` (replaces uses-STRING). Self-summon guard (monsterSummon.js:252): summoned index === summoner monsterIndex strips monster_summon actions = "lacks this action" SPAWN-SIDE copy-strip RAW, NOT a summoner refusal. Summon rows CAN ride recharge economy (gateSummonRecharge consults canonical monsterRechargeGate FIRST, spend before spawn; rows without recharge field byte-identical legacy).
