Mounted heal UI is display-stale by design (header/chooser aria only re-resolve at reopen) — judge commit truth by the write monitor + GET, never by the header. HoH/heal result modals DWELL across sheet navigation until their own `Done` — an un-closed overlay makes every sidebar `button` click time out with `short-rest-overlay … intercepts pointer events` (that timeout message is itself the open-modal census probe). Single-cure-tier synthesis (GZ, for `bug-single-cure-tier-unfiltered…` fix-verify): one-line rename `Restoring Touch`→anything in `public/data/2024/classes.json` (single occurrence `/6/class_levels/13/features/0`; the handler looks RT up by NAME in specialActions) flips an L20 paladin's LoH modal to the `!hasRestoringTouch && alsoCures` tier; reverse str.replace restores byte-exact (md5 `8d06a8c2…`, `git diff` proves it) — the same trick rides any fix-verify of the tier.

## 13. Rules-engine mechanics facts
- **Victory-banner law + `lastAttack` shape (shipped 2026-11-13 improve victory-banner-zero-attack-clear):** `campaign.lastAttack` payloads from the monster-card lane can LACK `timestamp` (Goblin Scimitar ride proved it) — attack freshness can never be gated on a timestamp boundary; a whole-object JSON fingerprint is the reliable per-board-generation attack evidence. Clear lane accordingly stamps `campaign.lastCombatResult` with `celebrated` + `attackEvidence`: celebrated = a `lastAttack` exists AND its fingerprint differs from the copy the PREVIOUS clear banked (the Clear is the board-generation seam; the stamp ALWAYS writes, so a zero-attack prep clear advances the boundary and outranks stale banners with `celebrated:false`). Display gate: `useTvPresentation.victoryVisible` requires `celebrated` — and `VictoryBanner` mounts ONLY in `Initiative.jsx` via that one gate (TV and non-TV share it).
- **Split-party presence law (live EP 2026-11-08, ee56b9f65)**: `away:true` on a PLAYER combatSummary row = present=false — turn walk skips it, TargetSelect/spell targets (`getCsAndTargets`)/metamagic picker/EffectAdder/TV up-next all hide it, card greys (`.creature-away`) + parks LAST via `sortCreaturesForBoard`. GM-only authoring: toolbar `Split Party` (role=dialog "Split party", `.secondary-target-row` checkboxes aria-label `Keep <n> present`, `Apply`) and per-card `.away-toggle-btn` (aria-label `Mark|Rejoin <n> …`); visitor tabs see NO toggle, NO toolbar, disabled inputs (live-verified), and adopted live via SSE without reload. Split WIPES initiative; rejoin auto-lands at lowest PRESENT roll ("end of round") or honest `initiative not yet rolled` audit; walk from an AWAY/unknown active creature moves to nearest present neighbour WITHOUT round increment; all-away board falls back to legacy walk (never stalls); audit types `party_split|party_rejoined|initiative_while_away`; enforcement-ON awayDenial text "…is away from the party — the GM must rejoin them…" + `enforcement_denied` log (that lane's vocabulary is `creature:`, not characterName — not an attribution bug). Filed defects: initiative input on away cards is unguarded (type+Tab resurrects slot AND un-parks the card — `setInitiative` raw sort) and away-roll `awayNote` has zero popup consumers. FIXED 2026-11-08 (40a91cb87): presence gate in `handleInitiativeChange`/`setInitiative` (refuses away rows ahead of all writes, Thief's-Reflexes grant face closed too), `sortCreaturesForBoard` replaces the raw sort in the service, `InitiativeControl` input is presence-keyed + disabled on away rows (split/rejoin re-syncs the display), `(Init n)` modal annotation suppressed for away rows. The away-roll `awayNote` zero-consumer defect FIXED same day (99c3843a9): `DiceRollResult` renders the note under the roll summary via a file-local `AwayNotice` in the amber notice family.
- **Spell overlay is visitor-WRITABLE — INTENTIONAL, owner-ratified shared-table authoring (EP 2026-11-08; authority question CLOSED by owner decision 2026-11-08, matrix in AGENTS.md §"Map Lane Write Authority")**: LAN visitor's Spell button → shape panel → real-click on a `.grid-cell` LANDS `POST /spell-overlay {action:'add'}` (2xx — route gates only CAMPAIGN_LOCK, no refuseUnlessGm, unlike the save-listener 403 lane — BY DESIGN, do not file or gate); visitor Clear-All works via role=dialog "Yes, Clear All" ("overlays placed by everyone" copy confirms multi-client authoring is intended). Store lives OUTSIDE change-data (GET /spell-overlay returns `{value:null}` shape; panel "Active (n)" is the truth). Visitor Ruler = 2 real cell-clicks → "75 ft (n cells)" readout (per-client `useState`, never server-shared); visitor 3D mounts canvas zero-errors (toggle likewise local).
- **dc_success**: engine default `action.dc_success ?? 'half'` (MonsterCardModal.jsx:255/:1011/:1032/:1145/:2158, applyDamage.js:88-97, saveProcessing:127) — half-on-silence is app convention; RAW success-zero rows MUST author `dc_success:"none"` (MV-20 half-leak family MA-0481/0622/0781). Picker auto-fabricates "takes half damage" copy whenever dc_success absent — copy + mechanic are ONE fingerprint. `dc_success:"none"` inline zero-success legs STILL emit save-damage entries at finalDamage:0/total:0 with NO hp_change (picker route emits none at all) — adjudicate via saveSuccess:true + finalDamage:0 + ABSENT hp_change, never a dcSuccess stamp (inline seam never stamps it). Half = floor: fd=floor(rolled/2).
- **Save lanes**: EB-NPC saves auto-resolve INLINE (no .sp-modal prompt; DC+type stamped honestly by formatter); damage-bearing AoE monster saves go inline NOT .sp-modal; damageless AoE monster spells early-return without prompt (spellDamagelessSaveCondition Helpers:371). PC prompt = `.sp-modal` "Saving Throw Required" + Roll Save/Dismiss (stage discriminator: picker = "Select creatures…"+Cancel; prompt = "Roll Save"); NPC prompt auto-rolls but stays OPEN — Dismiss only (Roll Save double-adjudicates). "DC Unknown — no success or failure" popup copy is cosmetic while lastAttack/log carry real sdc/saveType. Spellcasting-row save_type = CASTING ability, not the target save axis — adjudicate vs spell-DB dc_type (Entangle RAW = STRENGTH; hunger-of-hadar 2024 = DEX). Monster cast-save rows are ROW-LOCAL: ActionSaveRoll (3-arg) never calls findMonsterSpell — spells.json axes never auto-pulled (unthreaded gap). Save-prompt seam logs save-damage via `logEntry` PROP — harness addEntry captures only direct calls; pin both channels.
- **Save-rig seams are lane-specific**: block-save lane consumes `saving_throws.<abbr>.{modifier}` OBJECT (bare int → NaN); picker lane reads FULL-WORD `saveBonuses.<word>` (abbrev coexists but picker ignores); monster-chip lane uses `ability_score_modifiers.<abbr>` (numeric saving_throws there yields NaN). Deterministic inline NPC save: full-word `saveBonuses.<word>:±19` + nested `saving_throws:{<abbr>:{modifier:±N}}` in ONE full-store cs POST — folds into victim `total` while popup prints "+0" cosmetically — TOTALS are truth, popup modifier text cosmetic. NPC AoE auto-save NEVER consults rigged bonuses (saveBonus:0 even after stamps) — use a PC victim or low DC for success faces. cs `saving_throws:{con:{modifier:N}}` full-store POST = deterministic save-success rig; full-word ±19 flips picker determinism mid-fight, survives initiative walks, shares POST body with HP rig without clobbering (if re-stamped). At DC10, ±5 stamps only ~70% control.
- **Recharge economy**: spend stamped at fire/picker-open (`monsterRecharge {recharged:false,threshold:N}` per-char change-data key `<Monster>.monsterRecharge` — cs stamp inert); re-press before recovery REFUSES (`.mc-recharge-refusal` + `<slug>_refused` log + chip class `mc-dice-link-spell-spent`, zero victim rolls even on inline rows); recovery d6 rolls at owner's own turn-start only — cs round-POST does NOT roll recovery (turn-start engine: walk rounds; recovery lands first Next after array end; d6 can starve ~5 rounds — budget). Range-empty emanation rows still spend recharge at fire-time (inline fire). Refire after re-charge RE-SPENDS with fresh stamp. Judge by recharge/recharge_failed log dice, not gate mirror; reload mid-round may already have wrapped.
- **Legendary economy**: one expend per boundary; spent chip refuses `legendary_use_refused` same-boundary, honest spend at fresh boundary; frozen cs.activeCreatureName mirror BLOCKS the boundary latch release (walk advances `__initiative__` but gate keeps refusing) — sanctioned full-store cs POST setting activeCreatureName=walk-truth unsticks it; that same POST PRESERVES runtime-armed cs.targetName (one POST can set activeCreatureName + HP rigs). Fresh EB join leaves cs.activeCreatureName ABSENT → first press allowed, latch stamps ''. Child `uses` arms a phantom dawn-counter double-economy — economy rides rows[0] header only. Save-chip self-suppresses legendary Expend chip (absence correct).
- **Damage logs / lastAttack**: log entries `type:"roll"` + `rollType:"attack"|"damage"` (e.type=='attack'/'damage' filters FALSE-negative; `type=="damage"` zero); attack entries key `name` not abilityName; `log.attack.total` = RAW NAT (bonus in bonusDetail/targetAc separate) — never rebuild nat+bonus from log rows; the +bonus total lives only in popup chrome + lastAttack. Secondary damage rides the SAME primary damage entry via secondaryFormula/secondaryRolls/secondaryTotal/secondaryDamageType/secondaryFinalDamage — don't await a second rollType:damage entry. hp_change key is `damageBreakdown:[{damageType,amount,resisted,status}]`; no boolean `resisted` key — unreduced proof = resistanceReduction:0 + resistanceNotice:null + victim resistances:[]. SYSTEMIC: post-damage lastAttack mis-stamps secondaryDamage/secondaryDamageType/actualDamage = PRIMARY values on structured-secondary rows (handlePlainDamage :156/:160 order) and attack-log rolls can dupe — damage log + hp ledger are exact; cite family, don't file per-row. Weapon-type labels unreliable BOTH directions (reach-melee→"ranged", javelin→"melee") — judge lane from reach field/damage entry, rangeReason:null = gridless fingerprint; attack entries can stamp NEITHER weaponType nor range. Reach>8 authored fires 4/4 first-click gridless (rangeReason:null). Band "20/60 ft." inert: dual-mode chooser arms ONLY on authored damage_dice_ranged (useLoggedDiceRollAttack.js:153/274); resolveAttackRange default 30, reach-first.
- **Crits**: dice double, flat modifier NEVER doubles ("1d8*2+2 (N)", "2d8*2+4 (6, 2)"; popup per-die chrome "6*2, 2*2 +4"; literal "2d8*2 + 4" with spaces exists on NEITHER surface; single-die crit keeps "1d8" token doubled-in-place). Secondary doubles silently in resolveSecondaryRoll (handlePlainDamage.js:87) while secondaryFormula text stays undoubled — judge secondaryTotal==2×die; crit proof needs sTotal > single-die max. Engine does NOT auto-miss on nat1 — "+14 vs AC12" logs hit:true under a "CRITICAL MISS!" banner; popup total==20 with nat<20 is not crit; judge crit via nat token + isNatural1/isCrit. Popup leading number = TOTAL, `d20 N` token = nat; parse nats `/d20 (\d+)/`; popup glues can fuse numbers ("8 | d20 1 +7", "20d20 13 +7") — parse nat separately. Nat1 vs low AC still logs hit:true with both banners — adjudicate hit by log hit:true/targetAc.
- **Negative-mod dice**: "1d4 - 1" parses ((?:[+-]\d+)+ fold, diceRoller.js:42) → total 0 possible, ZERO clamp-to-1 — hpΔ==fd on fd-0 legs is RAW-honest. Crit doubles dice only, flat stays negative.
- **Expiration clocks**: live under runtime key `pendingExpirations` (turnStartEffects KEY), NOT expirationQueue; addExpiration omitting rounds → `expiryRounds:null` = Infinity clock = NEVER auto-expires (CLA-033 family — features with "1 minute"/"1 hour" text and no rounds: stamp persist forever); anchored clocks (`expireOnCreatureName:caster`) drain at the ANCHOR's next TURN-START (end-of-victim-next-turn RAW = accepted MA-0542/MA-0995/MA-1147 approximation); same-round expiry NEVER fires (next-turn-start anchor is the writer-side approximation). registerTargetEffect merges extraProps verbatim, REPLACE per (target,effect,source) — refire keeps value, does not accumulate (consumers accumulate across DISTINCT sources only). `duration:"1 hour"` + zone → clock recorded appliedRound/expiryRounds with live consumers (weakenedNamesFor/excludeNames/clearExpirationEffects).
- **targetEffects channels**: canonical registry targetEffectDefinitions.js (every effect needs label/desc/icon/group; GM adder reads only it); grants land at CAMPAIGN-ROOT targetEffects; SavePromptModal probes per-characters[].targetEffects which is never populated (te read-lane split — judge save advantage by dice shape/mode in log); endpoint `/api/campaigns/:c/targetEffects` (NOT change-data __campaign__); services writing root te MUST coerce null→[] (null crash aborts cast AFTER slot burn = lying UI); any new save-mode te MUST extend processNpcSave (monster-chip save lane otherwise pins normal mode); forced modes: `forcedMode:'advantage'` + d20Rolls 2d20, adv self-erases same attack; effect te POST key MUST be `target` (consumers match te.target).
- **change-data keys**: char route GET returns `{"<Char>":store}` (bare char object on the `.json` route) — POST must send the SAME shape `{["<Char>"]:merged}` NOT `{value:...}` (extractPropertyValue extracts by key); full-store cs edit = POST `/campaigns/:c/combatSummary` `{value:cs}` (or `{combatSummary:inner}` — double-wrap hazard, unwrap inner first); POSTing to `/change-data` (or `/changes/...` with spaces / encoded names) falls through the generic `/:key` route and writes a LITERAL junk key ("change-data") — invisible via GET exact-match, 200 lies; DELETE it + GET-confirm. char full-store POST `/<CharName> {value:...}` zeroes NPC change-data runtime keys (tempHp). python urllib POST needs explicit `Content-Type: application/json` (default form-encoded = 500). Debounce: writes/log flush lag ~10s — re-read before declaring missing; `getRuntimeValue` returns null for missing keys.
- **Condition channels**: attack lane reads ONLY `hit_conditions`/`hit_target_effect`/`hit_condition_roll`; save lane word-scans `save_effect` against the canonical CONDITIONS list (MonsterCardHelpers.js:50 blinded..unconscious; `cursed` IS canonical now, conditionUtils keys it save-con; `exhaustion` NOT in list — canonical numeric `exhaustionLevel` runtime channel only, cap 6, rest-removed, te cannot express levels — UPDATE MA-2004: exhaustion-lane PRODUCER NOW LIVE: parseExhaustionLevelClause (MonsterCardHelpers.js:185) arms via buildAbilitySaveRollContext (Modal:1893) → grantExhaustionClause (saveProcessing.js:794 fail-only) writes exhaustionLevel from save_effect prose "gains N Exhaustion level" + `condition applied "Exhaustion N"` + meta.level (Weight of Years PASS)). Grants log `type:"condition"`/`condition applied` (rider grant can be type:"condition"+condition field without literal text — count per-HIT); meta `activeConditionMeta.<cond>{source,dc,ability,durationNote}`; durationNote matches `until[^.;]+` only ("for 1 hour" never lands); durationNote stamps the LAST condition's clause on ALL granted (mislabel advisory); multi-victim picker can drop victim2's meta (un-awaited sequential writes). Pure hit_conditions lane carries NO addExpiration clock (clock lives on hit_target_effect lane). Charmed-clear-on-damage (applyDamage.js:429-435) runs BEFORE hit-clause grant (:1154→:1181) — same-hit grant SURVIVES as honest "removed — took damage (Charm)" → "applied" pair; judge by grant-log + meta, not end-state. Canonical-word grants on EB-NPCs write change-data activeConditions but are NEVER mirrored to cs creature.conditions → no initiative badge (EB-NPC attack chips arm attackerConditions from cs creature.conditions ONLY, extractConditionKeys :1023/:1739) — advisory (no cs-badge mirror), machine truth = change-data + log. Damageless failed-save grants via applyDamagelessSaveConditions + condition_clauses_advisory "(GM-enforced)". Repeat-save prose needs structured repeat_save (armRepeatSaveClause saveProcessing.js:623 reads structured keys/FP service only). `applyHitClauseTargetEffect` passes no `ability` payload (ability-scoped riders need `hit_target_effect_ability`).
- **THP**: parseTempHpGrantClause is save_effect-only + static-digit regex → grantFailedSaveTempHp fail-only in inline saveProcessing AND SaveAttackAoeModal resolveSaveFailGrant (replace-if-larger, `temp_hp_granted`, no clock). Attacker-side THP "equal to the damage dealt" = NO expressible transport (grep-zero). Swarms ship only currentHp/maxHp.
- **Spell cast lanes**: spell-ATTACK lane (attack_type:"ranged") has no-target guard UPSTREAM of spend (honest zero-spend refusal); save/AoE lane SPENDS FIRST (`spendMonsterSpellUseIfNeeded` MonsterCardModal.jsx:2110) before every downstream guard — un-armed AoE click burns 1/Day while refusal popup/log claim "Zero spend" (judge ONLY change-data `monsterSpellUses` + chip "(N left)"); refusal seam refuseUnarmedMonsterSaveCast now guards save-lane BEFORE :2110 (byte-identical MA-0049 "Zero spend" prose). Exhausted re-fire = log-only automation blocked, no popup, chip `mc-dice-link-spell-spent`; 1/Day gates per-spell counters independent. Prose-recharge-token-absent-but-field-authored = cosmetic drift, Notes-only. save-lane check-before-spend armed press still spends then opens single-target `.sp-modal` with the VICTIM's own save type winning over row save_type (shapeless range:"" AoE spells never open picker: armed = single-target prompt, unarmed = no-target refusal). Prompt-open can auto-log attacker-dupe roll/save ~30ms pre-Dismiss (resolved:null pendingSavePrompts, only admin-clear removes). Spell-side dc_type printed on dc_success:'none' advisory records is the SPELL's axis — verify vs ROW-authored save_type; never call an authored spell dc "spurious" without checking RAW conditional-save clause. Non-save advisory lane: skipSpendLog=true yet MA-0020 spend still fires — truth = ONE ability_use cast entry with spend note.
- **2024 casing kill-switch**: 2024 spells.json stores casting_time 'Action'/'Bonus Action' — any gate on strict `casting_time === '1 action'` (spellCastService/execution/index.js:61, postCastRiderService.js:247) NEVER arms for 2024 casters; gates MUST casing-normalize (normalizeCastingTime). `lastAttack.spellSchool` now stamped by all spell-save producers. Bonus-casting-time MOVES rows to the bonus lane (separate chooser host needed or pending orphans).
- **Save-resolution clobbers lastAttack**: after a save prompt resolves, top-level `lastAttack.attackerName` becomes the VICTIM (hit/weaponType nulled) — same-round post-hit rider re-arms then honestly deny "Last attack was not made by you." Seed a fresh qualifying hit before each rider press in rigs.
- **Counters/reaction ledger**: counterspell/parry/gated ledgers per §10 templates; `getCurrentCombatRound` campaignName-less stamp pins round=1 app-wide (pass campaignName); once-per-turn gates frequently read STALE cs.activeCreatureName (Next writes top-level only) — register keys in CAMPAIGN_KEYS/PLAYER_ROUND_LATCH_KEYS + Initiative.jsx round-wrap clears; reactions[] rows route through shared MonsterAction renderer. Reaction freshness keys attacks only (spell-save lastAttack reads stale). Turn-order ASCENDING (high init acts last among equals).

- **No nat20 auto-hit**: computeInitialHitState uses strict `total>=AC` (hitResolution.js:291) — low-bonus attackers vs high AC are STRUCTURALLY unhittable; swap the enforcer, don't hunt hopes. Attack ledger can't prove proficiency grants: attackCalc2024 adds +Proficiency UNCONDITIONALLY — prove grants via Proficiencies text row + canonical resulting value.
- **Dice engine census (live 2026-11-06 DG)**: sheet attack lane rolls TWO d20s every attack even in normal mode (`log.rolls:[r1,r2]`, `total=r1`) — r2 is the spare for the popup's post-hoc Advantage/Disadvantage toggle, NOT a silent best-of (probes showed total matching r1 both ways). Suspect skew? `await import('/src/services/dice/diceRoller.js')` inside page.evaluate and loop `rollD20()` ×20k — DG measured uniform (971×nat1, 1049×nat20 / 20k). `rerollOnes` exists but ONLY the monk healing lane opts in (`hasRerollHealingOnes`, healingHandler.js) — attacks never reroll 1s.
- **Rest semantics**: sheet "Long Rest"/"Short Rest" buttons fire INSTANTLY, no confirm (only Admin Full Reset/Clear carry native confirm) — verify by change-data GET deltas. ShortRestModal opens a hit-dice modal needing explicit "Complete Short Rest" — rest unapplied until clicked. Short Rest WIPES `activeConditions`+meta (restRules-shortRest.js:315) — seed conditions AFTER the rest for end-condition-feature probes. Rest-latch lanes are separate: keys in LONG_REST_RESOURCES are NOT reset by Short Rest; "Short or Long Rest" specs need a SHORT_REST(_NULL_FLAG_KEYS) entry too. Pattern: latch stamp + SHORT_REST_RESOURCES null re-arm in ONE merged setRuntimeBatch per target; readers `stored ?? max` — null = re-armed (0→null is the visible proof). Do NOT add consumed keys to SHORT_REST_NULL_FLAG_KEYS when the same handleComplete restamps them (null-then-restamp re-arms instantly). Long Rest nulls `_RendMind_Used` but NOT `_SneakAttack_usedRound` — round-scoped latches re-arm only via initiative round-advance; LR re-arm proof needs a round-walk. Long Rest button grants only the ACTIVE character. LR auto-refreshes portentDice (lv>=14 → 3), sorcery points + free-cast counters (per-spell counters→null, list→[]).
- **Rest-modal labels are cosmetic**: NEVER trust "✓ applied / Resources restored" spans — always verify the change-data counter actually incremented (fixed lane: applyShortRest returns `fontOfInspirationRestored` true only when a numeric<max was written; labels must read the return).
- **App-data canonical values that differ from PHB memory** (recompute from disk, never memory): warlock slot ladder lv5×3@13-16, lv5×4@17+; 2024 Fighter HAS spell slots; 2024 monk martial-arts die d6 lv1-4/d8 5-10/d10 11-16/d12 17-20 (lv20=d12 canonical — "d10 lv20" claims are 5e table); rage lv20 = 6 rages, +4 rage damage; Primal Champion = abilityCalc2024.js:14-16 `min(total+4, 25)` Barbarian lv>19 STR/CON, always-on (NOT rage-gated), app cap=25 literal, 5e twin +4 uncapped; Power Word Fortify lv7 pool "120+((slot−7)*5)" no concentration; Regenerate lv7, 4d8+15 initial +1/turn, 1 hour NO concentration; See Invisibility lv2 concentration:false 1h; Sanctuary 2024 concentration:false; Phantasmal Creatures = Illusionist lv6 (lv14 is Illusory Reality); Rage of the Gods = Berserker lv14; Searing Vengeance = CELESTIAL patron lv14; Supreme Healing = LIFE Domain lv17 exclusive; Spell Thief = Arcane Trickster major lv17 (5e grep-zero); Precise Hunter = Ranger CLASS lv17 (majors end lv15); Relentless Hunter = Ranger CLASS lv13; Inspiring Smite latch 2024-only; Mantle of Majesty = Glamour lv6, no SR latch; Elfish/lineage lv3/lv5 spells granted at lv1; app CD pool lv8 = 3/3 (RAW 2); GWF passives surface 5e-gated, 2024 model = +2 dmg (attackCalc2024:548); Dart = only Ranged+Thrown weapon in app data; canonical fighting styles at public/data/fighting-styles.json (no 2024 twin); Ray of Sickness lv1 in 2024; PB recompute per level (lv18=+6).
- **Level-up behavior**: wizard step-2 `Level *` spinbutton reaches lv20 without class re-pick; step-7 subclass survives nav; runtime spell slots stay STALE 0 after level-up — Long Rest before expecting new-tier slots; level-up does NOT bump current slots to new max (x/max + LR surfaces only); keepSpellRow silently hides lv5+ spells on sub-lv9 hosts (honest no-row).
- **Feats/ASI mechanics**: featAbilityChoices keyed "<Feat>-N" {assignment:Ability}; benefit-action rows carry the BENEFIT name not the feat name — handlers must thread `featName || action.name` through prefix-match, else silent fallback to max(CHA,WIS) (discriminator: set chosen ability BELOW CHA). ASI at ability cap hides +1 — pick another face to prove ASI. 2024 ASI lane never sets savingThrowsProficient. Save-proficiency feats MUTATE the SPA-cached feats.json in place (featBuffService.js:626-627 saveType overwrite + fallbackTypes delete) — row order across characters flips which holder is correct; judge grants from a FRESH reload or computeAllFeatBuffs(disk, fresh fetch). Heavy-armor/damage_taken gates must read `inventory.equipped` names × catalog `armor_category` — `playerStats.equipment` scans are permanently dead (full catalog, no equipped flag); speedUtils.checkHeavyArmor/checkAnyArmor are the shared exported predicates. Worn-armor truth = inventory.equipped.
- **Save rig scope (sharpened)**: cs `saving_throws:{dex:{modifier:-19}}` full-store POST does NOT fold into the SavePromptModal PC roll lane (modal uses PlayerStats real mod) — cs-fold is picker/NPC-seam only; PC fail faces = retry-until-face. NPC condition-save bonuses read campaign-NPC `savingThrowBonuses` via `/npcs/:name` PUT (monsterUtils campaign-first) — cs stamps inert there. cs abbrev `saveBonuses.dex` DOES fold at the picker seam. Campaign save modals show honest dice even where inline rigs are inert.
- **Auto-fire passives**: many passive rules are auto-applied with NO press (Primal Knowledge rage-skill swap: cells flip live, revert on rage end — read the `bonus` field not totals; Precise Hunter: cs.concentration{spell:"Hunter's Mark",target} → mode:'advantage' reason "Precise Hunter (Hunter's Mark)"; Skulker/Blur/Foresight defender-dis consumers REAL via contextBuilder-sync; Steady Aim = real mode:advantage, popup Adv/Dis badges cosmetic; Jack-of-All-Trades display bonuses already encode +floor(PB/2) for non-profs; Portent: b.clickable "Portent:" → `.portent-modal` pick → decrement + ability_use {portentDie,diceRemaining}; use `text-is("Portent:")` — "Greater Portent:" row collides; once-per-turn portent latch re-arms only on round wrap/turn-start).
- **Silent flag writes**: turn-start passive flags (Heroic Warrior family), rage ticks, regen ticks, Font-of-Magic conversions (`sorceryPoints` camelCase) write with ZERO log entries — judge by change-data poll; popup passive notes have no slot context (phrase "per spell slot level").
- **Overchannel formula**: backlash dicePerLevel = `Math.max(2, useCount)` (use #N≥2 = N d12 per slot-level) — off-by-one `2+(useCount-1)` was live at 6 sites.
- **Evasion lanes**: unified `rollType:'evasion'` ledger fires success AND fail; AoE save evasion resolves from FULL PlayerStats (evasionUtils.resolveAoESaveEvasion mirrors saveProcessing gate-for-gate incl. CoP stacked + Leading Evasion + Incapacitated exemption) — "Reroll Save (1 FP)" style prompt-note reads full characters while stacked modals compute from cs stubs; note proves nothing, ledger finalDamage/hp_change decisive. Unticked share-lane controls must expect FULL-on-fail; shared-evasion chooser selection is runtime-stamped (`sharedEvasionTargets-<promptId>`) AND threaded synchronously as evasionActive — the runtime stamp is PRUNED the same tick the await resumes (stamp-only gating flips over-grant to under-grant).
- **Immunity/advantage gotchas**: immunity does NOT gate riders ("IMMUNE — 0 damage" popup yet sneak dice + save rider still ride the hit); condition:'magic' per-ability mods are ORIGIN-AGNOSTIC (magic-origin inseparable from non-magic mental gates); `until_next_turn_extendable` has ZERO consumers (no auto-expiry/extension); STR-save advantage static ungated (grants pre-rage); STR-CHECK advantage never produced (automationModifiers isSave-only); "Halt:"/speed_zero passive display-only unless activeConditions:["speed_zero"] stamped; Powerful-Build condition:'grappled' mod leaks generic check-advantage when grappled absent from CONDITION_KEYWORDS (off-state controls confounded on Goliath hosts).
- **combatSummary rows carry NEITHER `speed` NOR `automation` (live FZ 2026-11-09):** witnessed key sets — player rows `[name,type,initiative,armorClass,maxHitPoints,currentHp,targetName,concentration]`, EB monster rows `[ac,concentration,currentHp,immunities,initiative,maxHp,monsterIndex,monsterType,name,resistances,saveBonuses,size,targetName,type,vulnerabilities]`. Consequences pinned live: every per-card `TurnEconomyStrip` renders the 30 ft `DEFAULT_SPEED_FEET` denominator for ALL row types (Wolf 40 reads 0/30; a Speedy-buffed EF card reads 30/30 while the header strip beside it reads 30/40); only the Initiative HEADER strip gets real sheet speed via the `characters.computedStats` lookup — so any name-keyed lookup in the header lane must resolve through the ST holder-remap (`economyNameFor`) or a twin anchor silently falls to defaults; and any refusal/consumer gate that reads `row.automation?.passives` (e.g. `CharReactions.buildOpportunityAttackRefusal` via `hasSpeedyOpportunityDisadvantage`) is DEAD on live boards — resolve through the characters lane (Initiative.jsx:807 idiom `character?.computedStats || character`) instead. The map drag budget reads `player.speedFeet` on map tokens with ZERO app-wide producers (tokens are `{id,name,gridX,gridY}`) → movementBudget caps every mover at 30 ft (`usePlayerDragging.js:52/:134`, `useSelectMove.js:57`) — filed `bug-movement-budget-caps-all-movers-at-30`.

## 14. Victims & rigs (addenda + current roster)
- **Delegation multi-press ledger law (live 2026-11-08 EQ, PT4):** N card-presses spread across M delegated-visitor contexts = exactly N `roll` rows (distinct `rolls` arrays — identical display TOTALS can be real duplicate dice, diff the arrays before crying cached-replay) + one `hp_change` per applied HIT; each tab drains its OWN popup (cross-context walk `page.context().browser().contexts()` shows zero lingering overlays elsewhere after drains); visitor tab shows NO popup for another visitor's roll. Force an active-onto-away state by POSTing top-level `activeCreatureName {value}`; a subsequent EB join re-anchors it to board head.
- **Bandit (AC12 HP11 unsuffixed) = canonical victim**; exact anchor `startsWith('Bandit0.125')` (name+CR concat defeats ^Bandit\b). Unclamped HP999 rig gives clean unclamped hpΔ ledgers. Zero-cond/rider adjudication keys on per-HIT audits, never miss legs.
- **AC-rig**: full-store cs POST victim `ac:N` applies BETWEEN rolls (fresh press re-reads AC; mid-popup doesn't) — manufactures misses far cheaper than RNG hunting; judge targetAc per-press from lastAttack. Only an AC-stamped press is a boundary proof (rig prints the rigged AC). ≥+10 bonus rows: miss windows run blank (~15%/press at nat≤3) — escalate rig AC to bonus+15 for an honest miss in ~1 press; AC-flip tie-geometry: a tie face (nat+bonus==AC) is fixed 5%/press, no rig manufactures it — straddle honestly. +3-vs-AC12 and +4 rows self-serve real misses; +13/+14-vs-AC12 structurally unmissable (miss ONLY via AC-rig). Miss popups self-validate the rig (print the rigged AC).
- **HP rig**: stage currentHp AND maxHp (cs POST maxHp does not lift currentHp; swarms some builds have only 2 HP keys — don't fabricate *HitPoints); stamp ALL FOUR HP keys where present; cross-session rigs persist invisibly — re-read live AC/HP before math; fresh sessions inherit prior rigs. EB-rejoin cs-HP full-store POST can be clobbered by the tab's stale snapshot — settle ~10s, re-stamp, GET-confirm. Ororo/client desync: cs 999 may not reach client combat state — re-read client before trusting |hpΔ|==fd on previously-fought victims. Downed victims clamp hp_change to 0 — judge ledger by roll entries. Initiative-card target-select resets to No-Target after each attack on some hosts — re-arm before EVERY roll.
- **Deterministic save faces**: force-fail = full-word saveBonuses.<word>:-15/-19 + nested saving_throws (both, or lane-specific per §13) in one cs POST; force-success = +19 same shape (popup "+0" cosmetic, total truth); -5@DC10 only ~70% control — judge legs by saveResult not plan order. Recipe to force a MONSTER spell-save fail: cs POST victim saveBonuses.dex:-15 (+nested) → PC caster casts cone/save spell → picker → Results modal stamps saveResult:"failure". NPC AoE ignores rigs entirely (see §13).
- **EB-join side effects**: Join of ANY monster lands the whole saved PC party as 1/1 AC-less placeholder entries — filter `type!=='player'` when picking victims; App.jsx:256 re-seeds cs PCs `maxHp:1` (heal-zero family); "+NPC" joins ship entries with NO monsterIndex (qty1 unsuffixed) — identity proof = disk-exact AC/HP + card content, clobber-check by monsterIndex not suffix; joined NPC combatants SNAPSHOT master stat blocks at join — monsters.json edits need remove+re-join to take effect live; EB join mid-flow auto-navigates to Initiative (stale EB evaluate joins duplicates); loading a saved encounter ≠ join — explicit "Join Encounter" click required; Join with empty selection spawns blank NPC 1; cs names sometimes suffixed ("Mage Apprentice 1"), sometimes bare — dump cs names AND avatar alts before anchoring; cs join order ≠ join-click order, ≠ init order — poll names, never carry idx.
- **Zero-grant proof ladder (strongest→weakest)**: victim change-data STORE KEY ABSENT > zero log lines > condition log lag (lag hides lines, never a key once applyHitClauseConditions ran). Success-leg zero-effect proof: pre-clear victim cd `{activeConditions:[]}` POST then key-stays-empty after sr:success fire; fire the +rig leg FIRST before any fail contaminates victims. Whole-store zero-grant scans must exclude `combat-ui-viewingMonster.actions[i].description` echoes and change-data push greps likewise.
- **Caster pre-flight**: post-admin-clear FIRST sheet cast by any caster THROWS "activeConditions must be an array for caster" AFTER slot burn — seed caster `activeConditions:[]` via full-store POST or EffectAdder add→remove round-trip BEFORE first cast; always pre-flight a free cast before relying on a spell roll for a verdict. Casting at a combatant lacking activeConditions:[] throws — seed [] first.

DELETED: DragonbornTest, DwarfTest, ElfTest, FeyNewTest, LightfootHalfling, Divine_Cleric, TieflingTest — older entries naming them remain valid as evidence; re-host on survivors. Initiative-normal control → EvasiveFighter; ElfTest fear-immunity and min-HP warnings now N/A.

Verify writes by GET: `applied:0` in a scoped-PATCH response is a LIE for char buckets (fields land anyway — only the cs `creatures` merge counter is meaningful).

Throwaway NPC via full-list `POST /api/campaigns/:c/npcs {npcs:[…]}` (`GET /npcs` returns the same `{npcs:[…]}` envelope), clean up with `DELETE /npcs/<urlencoded name>`; CAUTION — the full-list POST re-stamps `updatedAt` on EVERY row including owner fixtures.

## 15. Test/registry conventions & JSON safety
- **Mutable-capture + functional setState updaters is scheduler-dependent (live 2026-11-13 improve batch-cure-pool-running-total):** a batch loop that decrements a mutable `let` counter and pushes via `setLog(prev => …)` PASSES unit tests but shows the counter's FINAL value in every row live — `setPoolRemaining` before the pushes defers ALL functional updaters to the re-render where they close over the shared binding; jsdom's eager-state path (empty queue) masks it for the FIRST updater only. Capture an iteration-local `const` (compute per-index) inside the loop; to mirror live React in a unit rig, make the mocked resource `update()` dispatch a real re-render (useReducer forceRender inside a `useTrackedResource` mock override) — that deferred-updaters test is the pin that catches the mutable-capture variant (negative-proved: fails ONLY that test, 19/20 pass).
- **jsdom stale-closure test idiom (live 2026-11-13 healing-pool stale-closure fix):** to pin a commit-time re-read against a render-closure snapshot, mutate the mutable mock runtime store *between the last state-triggered re-render and the commit click* — any intermediate re-render (pool update, roll) re-bakes the closure and makes the test vacuously pass. Pin a refund lane by asserting the exact `poolUpdate` call sequence (e.g. `[[20],[19],[21]]`), not just the final pool value.
- **Never run `npm run build` concurrently with the vitest suite (live 2026-11-12 improve hex-board-open-put-coalesce):** `sse.test.js` serves `dist/index.html` — the mid-run rewrite flakes ~5 tests spuriously; sequence them.
- **Full vitest suite ~165 s (live 2026-11-14):** exceeds the 120 s foreground shell cap — background `npm run test:run` and poll the log bounded; foreground runs die at the tool cap.
- Render-based data-lock tests MUST be `.test.jsx` (vitest oxc: "JSX syntax is disabled" in .test.js); .js twins only if they never render. `npx vitest run $files` from rg -l output in zsh passes ONE giant filter → false "No test files found" — pipe through xargs. `rg -r` replace-flag garbles dumps — plain grep. Tests co-located, matching source file casing. **CSS-contract harness (live 2026-11-12 improve loading-overlay-blocking-css):** `src/App.css.test.jsx`'s `styleFor`/`parseStylesheet` pattern asserts real stylesheet rules (incl. `::before` selectors and `@keyframes` blocks) — the reusable way to pin visual contracts (position/z-index/backdrop) that jsdom rendering cannot; new co-located `<File>.<aspect>.test.jsx` naming fits it.
- **NEVER json.dump whole monsters.json** (608 monsters: reformats 250+ lines, breaks U+00AD soft-hyphen escapes) — restore via `git show HEAD:path > path` then surgical `str.replace` anchored on unique neighbor bytes with assert count==1; unique app-wide anchors like `"damage_dice_primary": "3d6",\n "damage_type_primary": "Lightning"\n }`; repeated action names ("Rend" ×68, "Vile Slime", "Claw") need FULL description+digits anchors; "from one of two claws" ×3 NOT unique; anchor inside the unique monster object block. Byte-check proposed multi-dice bytes with canRollExpression in step 1. Inserting automation after a last-position description needs the comma appended (anchor strip+`,\n`). Manifest: surgical line-number edit anchored on unique "id" line, never full re-dump; manifest rows VERBATIM from row-detail.mjs (paraphrase = fabricated-text flag); manifest single-line JSON — parse records, windowed grep stitches false; manifest claims are NOT disk-true (attackBonus label ≠ disk; conditions[] extracted ≠ authored) — disk-check every claim before adjudicating.
- **Stale-pin inversion**: fixing a row MUST invert/narrow same-pass every pin hard-locked to its broken state — whole-DB armed-census pins (monsterShapeShift.ma1020-imp.test.js toEqual(['imp/Shape-Shift']), multiattack/spellcast chip censuses), sibling byte-pins naming the pending row (ma1595 tiger test pinned winter-bite hit_conditions undefined), legendary-uses-{androsphinx,animal-lord}.test.jsx header pins, monsterLairActions young-inertness FALSE pins (grep young-<color> + invert ALL same pass; unfixed siblings stay FALSE-locked; lair trio edits index-scoped, never reorder/delete — keys index-bound); data-lock twin tests parametrize+reuse (kissContext pattern). Correction pattern (legacy ref): when a fix lands before browser verify boots, pre-fix repro is impossible (server serves fixed bytes) — adjudicate broken-state axes against ledger, verify fixed behavior live.
- **Mock surface growth**: a NEW EXPORT in heavily-mocked loggedDiceRollUtils.js needs +1 vi.mock pin in ~76 sibling test files (batch-stage test folders); new barrel import in a modal breaks ~50 partial mocks — extend vi.mock (expirations etc.) same pass; adding an exported lane to contextBuilder-sync broke 8 partial mocks; makeDeps mocks must key-dispatch (key===MONSTER_RECHARGE_KEY) or uses-state cross-contaminates; store-as-Map keyed [characterKey][key] kills propertyName-mock blindness; complexity cap 15 forces helper hoisting (gateRecharge, summonPopupStay, selfBuffPopupBody, transport field hoist).
- **Registry discipline**: subagents write docs/test-monster-registry.json DIRECTLY (never .tmp); orchestrator ALWAYS disk-verifies claimed writes and merges only new keys — never wholesale overwrite (stale snapshots); subagents NEVER write the manifest (forbidden; orchestrator diffs post-session); multi-row monsters use `verifiedRow2..N` keys, not ticket-suffixed; json registry append: parse-check BEFORE dump (retry duplicates rows otherwise); quoted-key json.dumps embeds literal quotes — anchor key text raw + git-diff hunk head after append.
- **Git safety**: NEVER git checkout/restore/stash/clean any doc (a subagent's `git checkout -- registry` wiped uncommitted host notes once; reconstruction with markers); never chain `git checkout -- file` after edits; a running fixer may refuse cleanup when injection-poisoned — orchestrator completes bug-file deletion + manifest edits after its own test/lint verification; runtime campaigns dir git-untracked — byte-clean PC restore = re-PUT parsed JSON, shasum before/after; `git diff` noisy on dirty trees — registry delta = dict-diff; manifest innocence via mtime-window + per-row re-read, not diff hunks.
- **Generation-sweep tests need per-case jsdom timeouts (live 2026-11-12 fix adjacent-dungeon-small-grid-crash):** vitest cases that run thousands of procedural generations (grid×seed sweeps, e.g. `it.each` over grids 7..100 × 60 seeds) are ~5× slower under jsdom than bare node (same sweep: 2.8 s node vs >5 s/case default) — set an explicit per-test `{ timeout: ~20000 }` on the heavy sweep cases or they flake-red on time, not on behavior. Keep the sweep bounded (fixed grid spot-list, fixed seed range) so it stays a regression pin, not a fuzz run.

## 16. SSE/change-apply lane (fixed 2026-10-27, 947b9702c)

- **sseClient console severity policy (shipped 2026-11-11 improve sse-benign-error-downgrade):** the shared pool's `onerror` logs `console.debug` while `readyState===CONNECTING` and the streak of consecutive reconnecting errors is <3 (benign reload/tab-close/dev restart — a `page.goto`/close now leaves ZERO `[SSE]` console.error rows); it escalates to `console.error` from the 3rd consecutive reconnecting error (API persistently unreachable under a live tab — loud by ~6s) and immediately on CLOSED (native retry over). `pagehide` pins the face to debug; any open/message resets the streak; no timers. LAW: the SSE URL is `http://<hostname>/subscribe` — it BYPASSES the Vite proxy and hits :80 directly, so an API kill produces CONNECTING network-error retries, never a proxy-500 CLOSED face. Shared-pool probe idiom: in-page `await import("/src/services/ui/sseClient.js")` + `subscribeToSSE(campaign, h)` joins the app's live fan-out (same Vite module URL) — clean POST to SSE round-trip proof without wrapping EventSource. Console collectors miss teardown logs entirely (Chromium drops handler events mid-unload) — clean-buffer-after-reload is the harness truth; prove the mechanism via the kill face. `browser_console_messages` buffer is per CURRENT page (`all:true` = page session, resets on tab switch/navigate). SHARPENED (2026-11-12 improve sse-close-error-tier, premise BLOCKED): the EventSource `error` handler NEVER dispatches during reload/close on this Chromium — a localStorage-surviving teardown probe (listeners write before document destruction, read back from a second same-origin tab) shows pagehide runs while ES error never lands — so a CLOSED-at-unload console.error face is live-UNVERIFIABLE, and any observed error-tier `[SSE] connection error` rows should be attributed to the kill-escalation face instead (once the 3-streak trips under a live tab it console.errors on EVERY ~3s native retry — e.g. inside the supervisor's ~60s dev-restart window, easily mis-timed as caused by adjacent tab closes). Multi-tab instrumentation scope law: `Page.addInitScript` is PAGE-scoped (late `context().newPage()` tabs don't get it) — use `context.addInitScript`; pending `text/event-stream` responses never appear in `performance.getEntriesByType('resource')` — probe EventSource via a constructor wrapper, not resource timing.
- **Raw `/subscribe` frame probe (live 2026-11-11 fix run):** SSE frames carry the event name INSIDE the JSON payload (`data: {"key":"denied-<campaign>-<key>",…}`) — grep `"key":"denied-…`, never `event:` lines (a substring probe on `event:` false-negatived on a real denied-* broadcast). And when counting `write_rejected` rows in the Log DOM, use exact `.log-write-rejected` — the harness `[class*="write-rejected"]` substring selector counts nested elements (~4× per row).
- App.jsx `handleRuntimeEvent` + `handleChangeKeyEvent` had TWO stacked guards dropping null/scalar `change-<campaign>-*` SSE echoes (outer `event.data == null` return + inner object-only return); relaxed so campaign-key null/scalar echoes route to `applyStoreUpdate` (947b9702c) — string/number/null runtime keys (travel-mode, travel-lastMessage, travel-pendingEvent clears) now mirror cross-tab; character-snapshot keys still DROP null as corrupt; object lanes (`saveResult-`, `biPrompt*`) byte-identical. Unit-test rig: capture handler via mocked `Subscriber` in `App.runtime-events.test.jsx`, assert store Map + `notify('campaign')`.
- PITFALL: `browser_run_code_unsafe` does NOT reliably auto-follow `browser_tabs select` from a prior execute block — a silent "tab B" read can run on A; stamp `window.__tab` marker and re-assert tab identity inside every two-tab probe.
- **Producer-side runtime-store clears are cross-client by construction**: a registered CAMPAIGN_KEY written as null by the PRODUCING tab (e.g. `clearAllPendingOas` at the EB-Join/NPC-add seams) emits the null echo that REMOVES the key in every tab — an SSE-observer tab needs no mount-side sweep to evict it (live-verified 2026-11-10); prefer producer-side clears over defensive mount sweeps, which risk retiring legitimately-armed state that must survive remounts.
- Toolbar Travel-tool absorbed-first-press (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-11 improve travel-tool-reentry-parity): the lying `.active` ghost is dead — the button's `.active`/`aria-pressed` and the TravelPanel mount now read ONE committed boolean (`useTravelToolSync` returns `travelPanelOpen` = synced travel-active, fed to both faces in the same render). An absorbed press reads honestly closed/closed; `aria-pressed` alone is now trustworthy panel proof — re-press once instead of the old press→probe→press dance. Related (same improve): reopening a map whose FILE carries a live trip auto-ADOPTS (faces pressed+panel with no press) — for an idle-mount repro cancel via `.travel-panel-close` first; `PUT /maps/:slug/deactivate` 409s when not active (harmless pre-delete).
- Campaign-bucket runtime reads MUST use the literal `'campaign'` characterKey: `getRuntimeValue('campaign', 'travel-mode')` — passing campaignName as the charKey console-ERRORs and misreads (14-error flood discovered live, 2026-10-27 hex-trip fix).
- Per-tab provenance recipe (75f2aa061): shared stamp (`travel-started-by` in campaign change-data) + `sessionStorage` token (survives reload/reopen, unique per tab, UI identity ONLY — zero game state) lets a client distinguish "my trip after refresh" from "another tab's trip"; see `src/services/maps/travelSession.js`. SHARPENED (live 2026-11-11 improve arrival-notice-shared): for a two-tab harness ride, seed `travel-started-by:'<foreign-token>'` (a token NEITHER tab holds) via pre-select curl — BOTH tabs then adopt the pre-seeded trip as observers with zero clobber risk; never press the Travel tool on such a rig (a press with no foreign stamp runs `startPlanning` and replaces the seeds).
- **Echo-adoption UI latency is ms-scale; only GET reads see the debounce lag (live 2026-11-11 improve arrival-notice-shared):** an observer tab's DOM (`.travel-toast`) adopted the GM's arrival press within ~15 ms via the SSE echo — the "travel writes lag 1–2 s, poll before judging" law applies to GET reads of change-data (2 s persist debounce + write round-trip), NOT to echo-driven UI adoption. For cross-tab timing proofs poll the DOM in the harness tab; reserve GET polling for disk truth.
- **Twin economy-strip readout rig (live 2026-11-09, fix of TurnEconomyStrip holder-remap)**: the whole ST-row economy readout ride works on a SETTLED Initiative tab — seed a 3-row full-store cs POST + top-level `activeCreatureName` POST → trusted Next click → curl full-store `turnState` POST charges land live via SSE echo (~2–3 s) and every economy strip re-renders in place; no reload needed to observe readout changes.
- `page.evaluate` cannot reference outer-scope consts in the serialized function string — recompute inside the evaluate.
- **Ops hygiene**: admin clears confirmed by API empties/own curl exit codes, retried individually (`curl -X POST url1 -X POST url2` runs both, prints only the last); `curl | python3 <<EOF` stdin collision kills POST silently — self-contained urllib + GET-confirm; orchestrator-sanctioned clear recipe: POST change-data+log clears, reload, clear again, sleep ~14s, GET-verify []/0 — close resurrecting tabs before final curl (§2 tab resurrection); re-join from a still-mounted EB after a data fix re-copies STALE in-memory monsters.json — full sequence: fetch cache:'reload' → page reload → re-select → re-join → re-stage; console 0 errors expected on clean rows; disk is truth over ticket prose (typo'd prose "rapie r"/"ofa"/"spel1" rides byte-identical cosmetic; RAW "takes 2" vs ticket "SRD 3" → disk wins); adjudication trichotomy: prose-only rider unexpressible = advisory PASS-subset; structured key absent but consumer-accessible = FAIL(b)/DATA; wrong-slot/over-grant/mis-sourced pool = FAIL(a); zero-affordance grep-confirmed no consumer = CODE-GAP (needs new automation.type+te+consumer), not one-field data.
- **Orchestration**: subagents NEVER write docs/automations-manifest.json (orchestrator set-status.mjs only; re-read the row after every mark; queue.txt array order is the ONLY dispatch source; recover adjudications only from live tool results). Subagent verdict contract: verdict-as-only-output, ~15-line cap — prose-without-verdict reports = failed attempts, row stays not-verified, re-dispatch. One shared SSE / no new EventSource (AGENTS.md) — app-wide `Subscriber`/`handleRuntimeEvent` fan-out; per-view handlers must key-gate (`event.key === \`map-activate-${campaign}\``) and self-state-gate.
- **Admin clears via API (skip the UI)**: `curl -X POST localhost:80/api/campaigns/:c/admin/clear-change-data` and `/admin/clear-log` — no dialogs/overlays (server/routes/campaigns-admin.js); verify GET change-data == {}. clear-change-data wipes combatSummary, concentration, and `BattleMasterManeuvers_selection` mid-fight — re-cast/re-pick after. Note `combatSummary` GET route shape: `/change-data/combatSummary` may 404→HTML while POST `/combatSummary {value:cs}` works; whole-store change-data GET returns the object directly; `/change-data/campaign` 404s to HTML (JSON parse SyntaxError).
- **Server/dev ops**: `npm run dev` runs node server.js WITHOUT nodemon — server-route edits need a child restart (kill + nohup npm run dev); Vite HMR reloads client only. Judge console-error fixes by the since-navigation counter (page header "Console: N errors"), not all:true session history. Dev-only HMR false-positive "change in the order of Hooks" on live-edited mounted components — re-verify fresh reload before filing. Known flaky test: mazeHandler.sp080-escape-concentration.test.js is unseeded, fails ~1-in-3 standalone — re-run before attributing. `rg -r` replace-flag misuse produced earlier "grep mangles" artifacts — plain `rg -n` only.
- **Mock-surface growth (re-confirm + counts)**: ANY new export/import into a heavily-mocked module breaks every partial vi.mock in its graph — extend ALL mocks in one pass: changeData.js route imports; useRuntimeState partial mocks delegating to a Map need .has/.get; shared feature helpers get their OWN module (conditionEffects.js export breaks ~58 strict mocks; elfishLineageHandler mocked in ~15 files; spellValidation breaks 3 WizardStepSpells mocks; spellPreparationService export breaks ~12; loggedDiceRollUtils ~76; new barrel-in-modal ~50). `mockReturnValue([])` + consumer `.push` poisons shared arrays — use `mockReturnValueOnce` chains or `mockImplementation(=>[])`. Partial combatData mocks must extend when exports grow. Changing a hook's returned shape breaks all its Map-level partial mocks (add `flushSave: vi.fn()` to each); un-mocked methods throw AFTER routing asserts, masquerading as routing failures. Fake-timer batched-autosave tests: continuous strokes never fire the timer (each move restarts it) — mid-stroke saves need a >1s idle gap. `not.toHaveBeenCalled()` fetch asserts must scope method+URL once a component GETs more on mount. Route tests may run HAND-REPLICATED copies of the route — mirror route changes there or they verify stale logic. When `rulesFactory.getPlayerStats` is mocked, rules-layer speed folds never run — mock `speed` must carry the FOLDED total; invert stale render pins same pass.
- **Char-JSON / campaign data safety**: characters are git-tracked — NEVER hand-restore tracked char JSON with git-show/string surgery; use Admin clear + reload or sanctioned PUT. Subclass-swap cleanup: close/deselect the sheet tab immediately after md5-restoring disk — an open tab holds the swapped config in memory and can re-PUSH full-store POSTs clobbering restored disk. Campaign rig drift between tickets: disk-check `class.subclass`/`race.subrace` before repro; PUT the bug-canonical shape for repro, RESTORE after. Wizard saves can silently re-home `class.fightingStyles` to None — re-check after subclass edits; class re-pick at step-6 CLEARS subclass (that IS the stale-major re-arm) — step-7 must re-pick before Save enables; step-7-only swap suffices when class.major null. 2024 feat PUT: string-form feats only — dict form breaks findFeat name.replace. Feat-less choice keys persist after untick+Save (inert) — clear via PUT. Disk spells[] ⊃ sheet rows (preparedSpells filtering) — pick probe spells from live DOM. Manifest handler/lane paths are frequently STALE (grep the real chain before trusting them; "Empowering Spell" was a mis-named real feature — rg the actual feature name before declaring fabrication). Enumerate class majors via node JSON walk (`majors[].features[]`, `class_levels[].features[]`) before dispatching name-uncertain rows; absent-from-all-data = STATIC-FAIL, no dispatch.
- **Wizard UI mechanics**: edits persist disk ~10-15s debounce; FEATURE ROWS rebuild from class data — reload after swap; wizard Save updates disk+runtime but NOT attack-cell renders — reload+re-select before judging cells. Spell selection = `.list-item-checkbox-trigger` click (row-body click only EXPANDS or no-ops); feat rows = `.feat-item .list-item-checkbox-trigger`; feat-cap "N of 5" is SOFT on some hosts, hard-blocks on others — deselect an owned feat if blocked; ASI chooser step-9 `.bg-ability-select`; skill/feat choice pool step-10 ("X of N", "Elevate" button = expertise face); Inventory step textarea: fill→Tab→Save (blur commits; `browser_type` wipes it; execCommand('insertText') for mastery equips), names with commas wrap in double quotes; click the AC-contribution popup away (blocks wizard nav). Spell-slot pip cells are TOGGLES (clicking a spent pip RESTORES) — click exactly `available` pips, GET-verify between rounds. Refusal popup at zero needs ALL eligible levels zero (getAvailableSpellSlotLevel loop). Resource tracker spinbuttons (`TrackedResourceInput`) fill+Enter refill stale 0 stamps. Sheet Long/Short Rest buttons click through while overlays linger.
- **Duplicate-name guard architecture (shipped behavior)**: quests/factions persist via GUID-keyed FULL-ARRAY POST (no per-entity PUT) — server guard = `validateList` option on `createJsonEntityCrud` rejecting case-insensitively-equal names (400 "A …with that name already exists"); rename-away+delete in one array passes (correct). Settlements/Sessions use custom PUT upsert routes that BYPASS validateList — they need their own PUT collision guard (npcs.js rename-guard pattern) AND validateList on the POST collection route. Client guard: hoisted `hasDuplicateName(list, editingId, name)` to module scope (component at eslint complexity cap 15); duplicate Save renders `.quests-form-error`/`.factions-form-error`/`.settlements-form-error` inline, modal stays open, NO POST hits the network. Dup-repro names must preserve whitespace when case-flipping ("qa dupa", not "qadupa"). Modal open on rejection intercepts list-row clicks — Cancel first.
- **Cross-client turn-seam double-fire (FIXED 2026-11-08 5049bfad1 + LIVE FIX-VERIFIED run EO):** with TWO clients mounted on Initiative, turn-START automation used to fire once PER CLIENT (byte-identical `ability_use` rows; visitor POSTed `/FeyRanger` + `/__initiative__`). Guard = `createSseEventHandler` takes `isLocalhost` (fail-closed) and `applyTurnStartPass` early-returns for non-GM clients. EO re-ride: 5 seams × GM+LAN = exactly 1 row, visitor writes since mount = ZERO; **two-localhost-window control (GM↔GM twins, both pass the guard) also 1 row / 1 buff append — the dedupe ref/shared stamp already holds per-origin**. Witness rig: settled-page re-POST of a small hand board (§72), `Clear` nulls `dreadAmbushSpeedActive` itself (no PATCH needed post-Clear), DIFF `ability_use` rows by `timestamp > t0` marked before the re-POST.
- **Visitor drains the GM save-listener bucket (FILED run EO, `bug-visitor-drains-pending-save-listener-bucket`):** every GM-side `createSaveListener` save resolution makes the VISITOR's `useLoggedDiceRollEventHandlers.syncListenerPromptFilters` (zero isLocalhost guards in that file) attempt the drain-to-evict `DELETE /pendingSaveListenerPrompts` → 403 "Access denied: GM-only feature" (guard lives on `campaigns-character.js:268` DELETE `:file` wildcard which refuseUnlessGm BEFORE `next()`-ing non-`.json` — the change-data DELETE route itself has NO GM guard, containment is route-order) + a console error per LAN device per adjudicated save. Same window, the visitor also landed a SUCCEEDING full-store `POST /<Victim>` stale-mirror snapshot (`{activeConditions, pendingExpirations:[]}`) — character buckets are visitor-writable for delegation, so that face is a key-loss race hazard, not noise. Visitor-tab baseline law sharpened: mount stamp `POST /__campaign__` only; delegated-card rolls POSTing `/Goblin`/`/lastAttack`/`/<Target>`/`/log` are BY DESIGN — the regression witness is writes the visitor makes while IDLE mirroring someone else's resolution.
- **ST-guard must cover the SSE echo lane too (pitfall, 2026-11-09 fix 4dc24883b):** the `activeCreatureName` name echo runs `expireStaleEffects(newActive)` on the WRITING client *and* every mounted tab via `sseHandlers.js` — an ST-law fix that only guards navigationHandlers still materializes the phantom `{pendingExpirations:[]}` char bucket under the synthetic `(Second Turn)` label. The guard now lives in `applyEchoBoundaryPasses` (twin echoes skip `expireStaleEffects` + the turn-start pass); after both legs, a single-tab twin walk leaves zero `*Second Turn*` keys in change-data. Any future seam-law fix must audit BOTH the navigation writer and the echo boundary.
- **Victory banner floats above confirmation dialogs (FILED run EO, `bug-victory-banner-above-confirm-dialogs`):** `.victory-banner` z-1300 > `.modal-shell-overlay` z-1000 — an undismissed banner's `.victory-card` renders OVER a later-opened dialog and silently swallows the centered confirm button (Playwright: `victory-card … intercepts pointer events`, 30s click timeout; banner card click dismisses, so the SECOND attempt works). 29e67520e made the SCRIM click-through but the CARD tier above modals remains. Live proof recipe: Clear → (banner up) → Clear again → try `Yes, Clear Board` → hit-test names the intercepting subtree.
- **MutationObserver + cross-client floaters (run EO law):** React mounts the floater as a `.hp-feedback` WRAPPER whose added node's className lacks `hp-floater` — a className-only observer reads zero forever (two false-negatives this run). Scan addedNodes AND `node.querySelectorAll('.hp-floater')`. The real delegated-roll leg PASSES: visitor HIT (AC-12 token target for odds) → Done → visitor writes land → GM-TV observer captures `hp-floater hp-floater-damage −N`. Wrong-chip trap: `.mc-overlay .mc-dice-link` indices are saves(0–5)/skills(6)/attacks(7+) on the Goblin card — anchor by row text, not index.
- **`pendingOAs` CAMPAIGN_KEYS registration (FIXED 2026-11-09, `pendingOAs` added to the set — see `resolved/bug-pending-oas-key-not-registered-echo-misroutes.md`):** unregistered, the `change-<c>-pendingOAs` SSE echo failed `isCampaignKey` in `applyStoreUpdate` and dispatched to a ghost CHARACTER store named "pendingOAs", and the drain-to-evict null DELETE echo dropped — same misroute family as `lastCombatResult`/`pendingStarryChaliceHeal` (campaignKeys.js comments). Consequence for rigs (now cured): cross-tab/curl `pendingOAs` marker writes live-update settled tabs within the SSE tick (~2–3 s), object echo = full-bucket mirror, null echo = key REMOVE. CORRECTED wake claim: an unrelated registered-key echo does NOT wake a stale mirror (re-POSTing enforcement left the ghost store unread — `notify('campaign')` can't inject a value never stored campaign-level); pre-fix the only cure was a remount/reload seed. Rig budget note obsolete: no settle budget needed after campaign-select beyond normal seed hydration.
- **LAN map-view + fog parity (run EO):** visitor nav "Map"; zero active map = "Waiting for the GM to open a map…`Check Again`"; replacing the viewed map NEVER hijacks a mounted view (App.jsx:226 pinned-view contract) — the viewed map shows the honest advisory `GM deactivated this map — view continues read-only` and the visitor must navigate away/back to auto-open the NEW active map (zero reload). Fog sandbox (cp owner fixture, `revealed:[]`, wall row, 1 token, fogged-half npc placedItem → POST maps → activate): visitor DOM = `205 .fog-cell-player` + fogged token label ABSENT; GM = `205 .fog-cell` + label VISIBLE. `GridAndWalls` filters fogged WALLS by isLocalhost; player-side class is `fog-cell-player`.
