## 18. Folded lane notes (maps/travel, AI authoring, campaign mgmt, monster/PC addenda)

Distilled from the 2026-10-10 → 2026-11-05 run logs; raw provenance + more detail in the archive.

Maps / hex / travel lane:
- **Indoor editor paint lane (live 2026-11-12 improve map-put-erasure-guard):** clicking a `.grid-cell` in the indoor editor with no tool armed is a SILENT no-op — arm the Paint/Erase tool toggle first before driving wall-paint probes.
- **Map deactivate (SUPERSEDING route, 2026-11-04)**: `PUT /api/campaigns/:c/maps/<slug>/deactivate` (GM lane; plain curl to localhost passes the Host gate) clears memory `activeMaps` + stamps `__map__ → {activeMapName:null}` + publishes `map-activate-<c>` `{activeMap:null}` + `maps-list-<c>` `{action:'deactivated'}`; every tab disarms on the explicit-null echo. True-gridless no longer needs close-tabs→restart→DELETE-key. Refusals honest: non-active/409, missing/404, no-active/409. Any new maps-list mutation must clear/set `activeMaps` AND `stampActiveMapContext` AND publish BOTH channels.
- **Stale gridless ritual (pre-deactivate era) — keep as fallback**: a `POST __map__ {value:null}` while a tab still has the map ACTIVE is RESURRECTED by that tab's MapContextSync (and by campaign re-select reading memory-only activeMaps). Old full ritual: close ALL tabs → restart API → `DELETE /__map__` → reopen (MapContextSync mounts, stamps null). Verify via GET change-data IMMEDIATELY before the press.
- **Fog LOS semantics**: `computeVisibility` (services/maps/lineOfSight.js) has NO vision radius — occlusion is walls/closed-doors ONLY. One token on a wall-free grid ⇒ whole map visible, and the GM-only exploration-memory merge (Map.jsx:378 effect) persists it — SHARPENED live 2026-11-11 run IE: the stamp fires on the GM **mount itself, no item edit needed** (an 8×8 sandbox went revealed 2→52 purely from opening the board view as GM); a LAN tab's mount never stamps (`if (!isLocalhost) return`). Check `walls`+`revealed` on disk BEFORE blaming a fog/sprite failure; a never-revealed map (`revealed:[]`, e.g. item-showcase fixture) shows LAN players a full-fog map. 3D `fogCells` census = cells neither revealed nor currently LOS-visible (NOT the raw unrevealed count — 12/64 on a wall-ringed 8×8 with 2 corner tokens, live IE). **Fog-pocket staging (live 2026-11-11 improve hidden-vs-fogged-dim, better than revealed-PUT surgery):** to get a guaranteed fogged-but-visible cell on a fresh sandbox, wall the target cell's sight line from the only token (e.g. token at (1,1), item at (7,7): walls at (6,6)+(6,7)+(7,6) so no Bresenham ray slips past) — fog = ¬(revealed ∪ current LOS). **Hidden-vs-fogged tiers now DOM-distinct (shipped same improve):** GM-hidden (`visible:false`) markers keep the 0.4 dim AND gain a static dashed amber sibling `circle/rect.hidden-item-ring` (stroke #f39c12, `stroke-dasharray:6 3`, pointer-transparent) on props/doors/tokens/ghosts; fogged-visible items stay full-opacity under the `.fog-cell` veil and NEVER get the ring; LAN clients render neither tier (ring zero). Live ring probe: `getComputedStyle(el).strokeDasharray` → `"6px, 3px"` (jsdom tests must assert class + opacity attr — stylesheets don't load there). 3D/hex lanes intentionally keep the single shared dim law.
- **Owner fixtures in test-campaign git maps (READ-ONLY)**: battle-arena, item-showcase (23 props, revealed:[]), poi-showcase (9 POIs, 16×8 outdoor), test-map. Map disk path `public/campaigns/<c>/maps/`. battle-arena restore recipe: GET→save bytes→modify→PUT→later PUT saved bytes back (server re-serializes stringify(…,2), md5 round-trips).
- **Regen byte-identity witness without a pre-mutation snapshot (live 2026-11-12 improve maps-list-generation-opts):** inside `page.evaluate`, `await import('/src/services/maps/dungeonGenerator.js')` and compare `JSON.stringify(generator(stamp))` against the disk doc — a third independent deterministic run of the same generator, no need to snapshot before mutating.
- **Hex-loader test-fixture laws (live 2026-11-12 improve hex-board-open-put-coalesce):** hook-level zero-PUT pins MASKED the live 3-PUT churn — jsdom fixtures never mount HexMap's view-normalize layout pass, so an adopted-but-pre-reset baseline compared clean in tests yet re-dirtied in the browser; hook fixtures must be SETTLED docs (pan at the loader's clamp fixed point) or they honestly earn their one settling write (and the keyed-remount unmount-flush makes it visible on map switches — container tests that mock `useZoomPan` keep masking). Any test mocking `outdoorConfig.js` for the hex loader chain MUST include `HEX_SIZE` — `hexViewMath.js` reads it (undefined → NaN view bytes).
- **Map PUT hygiene**: full-doc PUT requires the `players` array (missing → 400) — round-trip GET→edit→PUT keeping EVERY top-level field. `normalizePlayerIds` assigns lowercased-name ids to id-less entries, `-2/-3` suffixes collisions, 400s entries with neither id nor name. `useMapLoader` reconciles loaded `players[]` against CAMPAIGN CHARACTER NAMES on every editor open and autosaves the pruned doc — API-rig tokens with non-character names vanish seconds after open; use REAL character names for duplicate-name rigs. POST /maps accepts players in any shape but editor truth is `{id,name,gridX,gridY}`. **One-shot token-drop (live 2026-11-06 improve; NOT re-observed 2026-11-09 FX):** the create POST historically could materialize the doc WITHOUT the `players` array (GET/disk show no key; the 200 lied) — and on a token-less active map `isWithinRange` gridless-fallbacks LENIENT so every radius-gated scan false-passes (phantom adjacency). FX's `POST /maps {name,type:'indoor',gridSize:20,players:[{id,name,gridX,gridY}]}` LANDED players byte-exact (GET + editor drag worked first try), so the drop is no longer blanket law — but ALWAYS GET/disk-verify the tokens before trusting ANY range evidence (the players-clobber 400 guard only fires when the STORED doc already carries players — the create-drop path passes through).
- **Item/sprite DOM anchors**: placedItems carry `title=placedItemLabel(item)` on both lanes — anchor via `g[title="<label>"] rect.item-hit-area` (barrel/firepit are circle exceptions); `g.placed-item[title]` counts include ghosts. Hidden GM face = opacity 0.4 on the `<image>`, player face = element ABSENT. Unregistered `placedItems` types render a visible `?` placeholder (GenericPropSVG) with working Hide/Delete/Rotate-hidden (`isRotationInvariant` consults `itemSpriteHref`/ART_TYPES in `services/maps/itemSprites`). Context menu renders INSIDE the map SVG as `g.item-context-menu` with `text.menu-option` leaves — div `[class*=context-menu]` probes are FALSE NEGATIVES; open via trusted right-click on `.item-hit-area` centre, act on the leaf in a SEPARATE call (React flushes between tool calls, not within one synthetic-event task), close via the `text.menu-close` ✕ leaf (Escape does NOT close it). Furniture Delete is two-stage: leaf → `Delete from Map` modal → `button.delete-confirm-btn`. Ghost Rotate write-but-no-visual = fixed (hidden for ghost types); registered-but-circle props keep the menu entry yet skip the transform.
- **Drop lanes**: `browser_drop({target:'svg.grid-svg', data:{'text/plain':'<type>'}})` lands at the element-centre cell; synthetic `new DragEvent('drop',{dataTransfer,...})` honors px→cell. `dispatchEvent` returning false on a cancelable drop is the SUCCESS signal (React preventDefault). Out-of-grid drops CLAMP to edge (clampToGrid) + console.error. Items-panel char list EXCLUDES already-placed characters. HexMap `character:` drop branch DELETED (5f995d5c6) — hex drop is POI-types only; marching order rides MarchingOrderPanel buttons; `useMapLoader` ALWAYS seeds marchingOrder + default-centre partyPosition when keys absent.
- **Map read cache**: GET (and HEAD, since 2026-11-04) on campaign reads carry `no-store`; client `loadMapData` fetches `cache:'reload'` (byte-pinned in BOTH mapsService test files — sweep all fetch-byte pins on lane changes). `curl -sI` sends HEAD — probe the real method in header audits.
- **Map.jsx caps + pins**: Map.jsx sits at the lint statement/complexity cap — new statements need an offsetting consolidation; pass whole objects into child hooks, not `x?.y`. `Map.test.jsx` pins the exact `<defs>` id census — extend that array same-pass.
- **Hex3D/maps-manager locators**: rows are `li.maps-manager-item`; `getByRole('button',{name:'Open',exact:true})` misses (icon-child accessible-name quirk) — use `row.locator('button',{hasText:/^Open$/})` or `aria-label="Open \"…\""` (row TITLE text click does NOT open). Fuzzy parentElement-scope scans grab the WRONG row's Open (live 2026-11-11 pace-odds run: landed on Battle Arena instead of POI Showcase) — anchor on the `aria-label="Open \"…\""` button inside the intended `li`. MapsManager row buttons are unscoped globally — scope the click to the `li` containing the slug; activating a row auto-demotes the wrong one (one activeMap per campaign). Rows show DISPLAY names ("Item Showcase"). Player nav label is "Map" (singular); GM's is "Maps". Deactivated-map advisory face: `.map-deactivated-hint[role=status]` (indoor 2D toolbar only) ≤5s via SSE. PITFALL (live 2026-11-10 improve): row innerText swallows Font Awesome glyphs ("GM Advi ory Sandbox") — match row labels with relaxed `startsWith`/`includes` clauses, never the exact displayName.
- **Maps generate-modals (procedural, live SD 2026-11-12):** `.generate-dungeon-btn` relabels by the active map-type — Indoor→`Generate Dungeon`, Outdoor→`Generate Terrain`. The type `<input type=radio name=mapType>` is VISUALLY-HIDDEN inside a styled `label.map-type-option`, so `locator.check()` TIMES OUT AT 30s — click the `label.map-type-option` (hasText `Outdoor`/`Indoor`) instead. Dungeon modal `.dungeon-gen-modal` / Terrain modal `.terrain-gen-modal`: name is `input[type=text]` nth 0, seed is nth 1, grid is `input[type=number]`; shared child classes moved to the neutral `gen-modal-*` prefix (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-12 improve gen-modal-class-prefix: former `.dungeon-gen-*` CHILDREN are now `.gen-modal-*` (e.g. `.gen-modal-generate-btn`, `.gen-modal-hint`, `.gen-modal-option-btn`, `.gen-modal-mode-btn`), defined once in `gen-modal-shared.css` and scoped per-twin under the surviving ROOTS `.dungeon-gen-modal`/`.terrain-gen-modal`; parity pinned by `generateModalParity.test.js`). `.gen-modal-generate-btn` is DISABLED on empty name and the create is PROCEDURAL (no AI/network — omlx :8000 is irrelevant to these lanes despite the map's loose "AI" label). Success face: modal detaches, new row appears, disk `public/campaigns/<c>/maps/<slug>.json` lands `walls:[N]` (dungeon seed42/grid20 — CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-12 VG fix-verify: post-shuffle-fix anchors, both re-proved node==Chromium byte-identical — BSP default mode 256 walls + 34 placedItems (SHA `b6174ccc4c92f146`, `337a603de` anchor), Room-Adjacent Standard/Compact/Balanced/roomCount-8 199 walls + 42 placedItems (SHA `d4cd577f35031f1d`, `d88c3cfb4` anchor); wall entries are `"x,y"` **strings**, not objects — disk-extent probes must parse accordingly) or `terrain["x,y"]` (terrain seed7/grid30 → 1800 hexes, 2N×N). SEED-PLACEHOLDER TWINS UNIFIED (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-12 improve gen-modal-seed-placeholder-copy, VX re-lived: terrain twin reads `Random if empty`): both twins share the `SEED_INPUT_PLACEHOLDER` constant (`'Random if empty'`, `src/services/maps/seedInput.js`) — one placeholder selector is symmetric across the modals. The VG-era drift (terrain `Random`) is retired. `.gen-modal-generate-btn` also disables during `generating`; close = plain-text `Cancel` button (no class). Dungeon grid-size hint FIXED (b3f72f3ef) to describe the real map: `{5N} ft × {5N} ft ({N²} squares)` via canonical `FEET_PER_CELL` (grid 30 → `150 ft × 150 ft (900 squares)`, Room-Adjacent branch too) — any probe pinning the old `{N} ft × {N} ft ({N} squares)` copy is stale. Non-numeric seed → dungeon `mulberry32(NaN)` silently coerces to seed-0 (NO hang/crash — verified in a hard-bounded `node` process; shell timeout is the safety net for "would an in-page generator loop forever") and stores `seed:null` (JSON drops NaN). Two rename-lane Playwright gotchas: `page.getByDisplayValue` is ABSENT on this MCP page object (scope the open rename input via `.maps-manager-item input[type=text]` — the create field lives in the `.maps-manager-create` header, not a row), and an `aria-label` with embedded `"` must NOT be a CSS attribute selector (double-serialization mangles it) — use `getByRole('button',{name:'Rename "<n>"',exact:true})`.
- **Room-Adjacent generator opts fully consumed (fixed 2026-11-12 bug-adjacent-room-size-corridor-controls-inert):** all four adjacent-mode opts are live — `roomCount`, `layoutStyle`, Room Size→`opts.minRoom`/`maxRoom` (clamped into the `[2, roomMaxSpan]` envelope), Corridor Length→room spacing (compact 1 / moderate 2 / sprawling 3). Wide gaps join rooms with the existing centre-to-centre carved L-corridor and **zero door items** — a door-count census discriminates sprawling from compact live. Byte law: default/compact output replays byte-identical to pre-fix. Note: sprawling honestly packs fewer rooms into small grids (spacing eats capacity — fewer floors, not more).
- **Drag press-point law (live 2026-11-10 improve)**: on a settled board the svg-origin+`gx*40+20` recipe DRIFTS after the first drag (pan/zoom shift) — derive every press point from the token's own `getBoundingClientRect` (`svg.grid-svg circle.creature-circle`) and read the landing cell from the circle's `cx` attr (`(cx−20)/40 = gx`). SHARPENED same day (same-cell stacking improve): a press MISS on empty grid silently starts a MAP PAN and shifts every subsequent press in the tab — re-derive rects from the classed circles (`circle.creature-circle`/`circle.npc-circle`) after ANY crowd mutation, and beware the FIRST `circle` inside a token group is the `<defs><clipPath>` circle (zero-size rect) — always anchor on the classed circle.
- **Same-cell token stacking invariant (shipped 2026-11-10 improve same-cell-token-overlap)**: overlapping tokens fan out by paint order (player layer bottom → monster rows → npc rows top, `services/maps/tokenStackOffsets.js`, 10px steps capped at 2); buried groups carry `transform="translate(−10k,−10k)"`, stack tops and single tokens are attribute-absent, and crowded-cell placed tokens render `circle.placed-hit-area` instead of the 40×40 rect (painted hit = grab surface, paint order alone decides). Consequence for probes: `elementFromPoint` at a BURIED token's rect centre legitimately returns the token ABOVE it (that pixel shows the top token's art) — grab low tokens at their peek arc (~0.66r toward the fan direction).
- **Travel rigs**: travel tool state is per-mount LOCAL: opening an outdoor board with no live trip in the store does NOT restore a previously-pressed Travel tool — a fresh `[aria-label*="Travel mode"]` press is required per mount (laws bullet 10's zero-press auto-mount only applies when the store carries a live trip). Exhaustion grind = all-char buckets `POST /<Char> {value:{exhaustionLevel:N}}` + `POST /travel-accruedCost {value:10}` + `POST /travel-dailyBudget {value:2}` → exhausted banner without seeding dayExhausted/mode; Forced March adds +1 EX to every bucket EXCEPT self-restoration holders (live KV 2026-11-11: 12 of 13 PCs stamped, Disciplined_Monk honestly skipped by the `hasSelfRestoration` gate). Extreme-weather rig: POST throwaway outdoor map (`{name,type:"outdoor",gridSize:20}`, whole grid plays `plains`; CORRECTED IN PLACE live run KV 2026-11-11 re-verifying 1b28c3752: create DOES stamp a centred `partyPosition` (gridSize 20 → {q:20,r:10}) at writeFileSync — no create-then-PUT dance needed, GET-verify is immediate; the JT-era "client seeds defaults at editor-open" face is RETIRED; gridSize N ⇒ hexCols 2N × hexRows N) + PUT full body with `weather:{condition:'extreme',moveCostMod:null,budgetMod:0,…}`; advance refuses honestly (`travel/extreme_weather` row — CORRECTED IN PLACE: the row CARRIES the party hex + terrain (q29,r11 plains live KV; the old hex/terrain-NULL claim predates the 2026-10-27-G fix), plus panel message "Extreme weather makes travel impossible. Camp and wait it out." and a dedicated `.travel-panel-exhausted` camp row; dayExhausted UNREACHABLE under cost-null). Campaign-bucket reads use literal charKey `'campaign'` (`getRuntimeValue('campaign','travel-mode')`) — passing campaignName as charKey console-errors.
- **Travel console live-lane laws (run JT 2026-11-11, full ride):** trip keys seeded PRE-select adopt cleanly into the mounted console; a Travel-tool press on a fresh map WITHOUT tool-adoption runs `startPlanning` and REPLACES them (destination→null, path→[], new `travel-started-by` stamp). Honest plan lane = Travel tool + hex-click destination. Destination-click geometry (SHARPENED IN PLACE live run KV 2026-11-11, simplest proven lane): inside page.evaluate `const m = document.querySelector('.hex-grid-layer').getScreenCTM()` (the LAYER group's own CTM — the JT-era warning is about the SVG ROOT only) → `screen = m.a*x + m.c*y + m.e` with `x = 30*sqrt(3)*(q + r/2), y = 45*r` (HEX_SIZE 30, pointy-top axial) → `page.mouse.click`. GUARD mandatory: `document.elementFromPoint(x,y).tagName === 'path'` BEFORE clicking — the floating `.travel-panel` occupies the lower-left grid area and absorbs destination clicks (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-11 improve travel-panel-click-feedback: a planning-await chrome click is no longer silent — it fires the `.travel-toast` "Select a destination hex on the map" via pointerup detection (not click — the header drag preventDefaults pointerdown); probes must not treat that toast as an arrival signal and must keep the elementFromPoint guard so a panel-absorbed click isn't misread as a failed arm). The centroid-affine two-rect law and the trusted `locator.nth(r*hexCols+q).click({force:true})` lane both remain valid alternatives. Arrival law GET-proved: `travel-destination/travel-path/travel-pathIndex` REMOVED (per-key DELETE, no null ghosts), `travel-started-by` → null (release is a null-POST — key PRESENT as null, not deleted; CANCEL also null-releases destination/mode rather than DELETE), day-state keys (`travel-accruedCost/-dailyBudget`) + `travel-lastMessage` SURVIVE arrival, party position lands in the MAP FILE, `.travel-toast[role=status]` "Arrived at destination — travel complete" (2.5 s) renders on the presser tab AND — since 5c403d502 — on every observer tab via the echo lane (live KV 2026-11-11: LAN isolated-tab toast appeared ~12 ms after the GM's arrival press; presser byte-unchanged), honest advance×N + arrived `travel` rows + `session_phase` pair. Whole-doc `weather` PUT adopts LIVE mid-session (panel row re-renders; per-hex cost = 0.75 plains base × moveCostMod: clear 0.75, rain 0.9375 GET-deltas) and `__hexmap3d.census` answers `weather:"<condition>"` GM + LAN — CORRECTED IN PLACE (115cd8f7f, live KV re-verify): census stamps the doc's verbatim condition, `clear` reads "clear"; null is reserved for no-weather. `travel-pathIndex` writes lag ~1–2 s — poll after EVERY advance; a 500 ms read false-stalls. Cross-client trap (filed + FIXED 2026-11-11 `bug-travel-cross-client-advance-replays-hex`): after an external advance echo, the adopting tab's stale `pathIndexRef` used to make its NEXT press replay the last hex (budget +cost, duplicate `travel`/`advance` row, pathIndex unchanged) — `useTravelManagement` now backfills the advance refs from the synced path/pathIndex echo (adoption-only, idempotent on local writes), so the adopting tab arrives on its first press; GET idx + accrued per press anyway before concluding cache. POI truth field is `label` (not `name`). POSTing ANY unregistered `travel-<name>` name creates a LITERAL change-data key (own `travel-weather` phantom, DELETE-swept) — §362 law again. **Event + day-state console laws (first live ride, run KV 2026-11-11, all faces GET/DOM-proved):** (1) An advance that triggers an event logs `travel`/`advance_with_event` (carries eventTitle), sets `travel-mode:'paused'` + `travel-pendingEvent` (object) + panel message `⚡ <title>`, and mounts `.event-dialog-overlay .event-dialog` (type/title/description/`.event-dialog-terrain` + type-colored header); Accept = `.event-btn-accept`, DECLINE = `.event-btn-skip`, `.event-btn-reroll` with `.reroll-count` `(N)` (title "Re-roll (N left today; resets when you Camp)"; at 0 disabled with "No re-rolls left today — Camp to start a new day"). Reroll decrements `travel-rerollsRemaining`, replaces pendingEvent with a FRESH random roll (duplicates possible), logs `event_reroll` naming the DISCARDED event. (2) SKIP mid-route resumes the interrupted mode (`traveling` — `modeBeforePauseRef`); ACCEPT keeps the legacy PLANNING restore on every type (documented in useTravelManagement :652-656 — do not fold); both clear lastMessage; non-combat accept logs exactly one `event_accept` row (eventType+eventTitle, battleMap null). `npc`-type dialogs carry the hint "Accepting only records this encounter — the GM adjudicates off-map." (3) COMBAT accept stages into the campaign's SINGLE reusable **"Travel Encounter Stage"** indoor map (30-grid, `parentHex`, wiped+reseeded per accept: 13 player fan-out tokens + the encounter's monsters as `type:'npc'` placedItems + decor), the `event_accept` row carries `battleMap:"Travel Encounter Stage"`, and the app NAVIGATES the GM to the stage board (panel unmounts, tool face follows on the hex view). With `enforcement.autoEncounters` OFF the lane writes ZERO initiative state (no combatSummary beyond the mount-merge 13-PC placeholder artifact — CV/CW law). CLEANUP: the stage map row appears in Maps-manager — Delete (role=dialog, confirm `Yes, Delete Permanently`) it at exit. (4) WEATHERCHANGE accept re-rolls the map-doc weather via handleGenerateWeather and re-derives `travel-dailyBudget` from the NEW roll (differential face: Fast budget 5 → 3 under storm budgetMod 0.75); plains-table weatherChange weight is 3/100 — do NOT chase it on the RNG: POST a synthetic `travel-pendingEvent` `{value:{type:'weatherChange',title,description,terrain}}` + `travel-mode:'paused'` onto a SETTLED tab — the reactive useSyncedState keys adopt live and the dialog mounts (sharpens §54: the "curl keys don't refresh a loaded tab" law belongs to NON-reactive getRuntimeValue consumers; every `travel-*` hook key renders live from the echo). (5) Exhausted-banner producer split: on a uniform-cost map the mount/echo MIRROR flags `travel-dayExhausted` the moment the budget is spent (accrued==budget) — NO lastMessage, NO `day_exhausted` row (banner copy "Travel budget exhausted — camp or forced march?" + `.travel-budget-hint` "X / N used today[ — carried from earlier trips today]"); the press-reject face (message + `day_exhausted` row) needs an unaffordable ROUTE next-hex with an affordable neighbor, unreachable on all-plains — judge the pressed-refuse family on the extreme-weather face instead. Camp (`.travel-btn-camp`, banner OR extreme row): re-rolls doc weather, accrued→0, budget re-derived from NEW roll × pace, rerolls→3, dayExhausted false, forcedMarchHours 0, PAUSED→PLANNING if paused, message "A new day dawns. Travel budget refreshed.", `camp` row (weather field = PRE-roll). (6) Forced March (`.travel-btn-march`): +1 exhaustionLevel to each PC bucket (self-restoration exempt), `travel-forcedMarchHours`+1, accrued 0, budget = floor(base×budgetMod×(5/6)^hours) — the penalty is BUDGET-ONLY: per-hex cost and "Next hex" time stay base (per-hex `applyExhaustionSpeedPenalty` RETIRED 2026-11-11 e2e-improve — copy, budget and per-hex numbers now tell one story), status row `.travel-panel-exhaustion` "N / 6 stacks Daily budget: 83%" (budget-framed copy, `.travel-exhaustion-budget` span; panel lastMessage "…Daily budget reduced to N%."), honest `forced_march` log row. (7) Max-EX gate (any PC exhaustionLevel ≥6): `.travel-panel-hint` "A party member is at maximum exhaustion — rest required" + Advance disabled with the same tooltip; over the exhausted banner the copy flips to "Travel budget exhausted — camp to rest." and `.travel-btn-march` disables with title "Cannot forced march — a party member has reached maximum exhaustion" — the gate is a RENDER-TIME read (`partyHasMaxExhaustion()` via non-reactive getRuntimeValue): a foreign bucket write flips it only when something re-renders the panel — a same-value chip press does NOT tick it, any real change (pace/frequency to a different value) does. (8) Pace chips (`.travel-pace-btn` Slow/Normal/Fast) re-derive `travel-dailyBudget` (floor(hexesPerHour×8): 2/4/5 ×weather budgetMod) + "Next hex" time (e.g. Fast 1h 8m vs Normal 1h 30m plains), clear dayExhausted + FM hours, KEEP accrued (same-day pool); horseback (`.travel-horseback-btn`) halves time ("1h 8m"→"34 min", label Walking↔Horseback + tooltips) without touching per-hex cost. (9) The travel console is 2D-ONLY: the 3D hex view renders the tube + party passively (no `.travel-panel`), `hex3dScene.js:1097` `void pathIndex` is reserved-for-v2 (NO behind/ahead colouring in 3D) and census `travelPath` is a presence flag 0/1 — cross-client advance/cancel adopt the mounted 3D scene live without remount (cancel flipped census travelPath 1→0 in KV). (10) Tool/panel parity (fd3b30cf5, re-verified ×4): opening a map whose store carries a live trip auto-mounts panel + pressed face with ZERO presses; arrival/cancel auto-unpresses (aria-pressed false, panel unmounted); re-entry is a single press into PLANNING with the honest "Select a destination" empty-path label.
- **HexMap.jsx statement/complexity ceiling (live 2026-11-11 improve travel-panel-click-feedback)**: the HexMap component body sits EXACTLY at the eslint max-statements(60)/complexity(15) ceiling — any new in-body derivation or `useCallback` tips it over; hoist to module-level helpers/consts instead.
- **Player waiting-pane**: `Waiting for the GM to open a map…` + `Check Again` renders even when `__map__` is non-null (auto-open fires only on NEW activation SSE events while blank; never-hijack for an already-OPEN map is PINNED by App.map-activate.test.jsx — neither is a bug; click Check Again). Self-heal rides server-truth auto-check (fixed 2026-11-05 maps-waiting-pane-self-heal).
- **Outdoor pan-clamp hides edge parties (live 2026-11-11 fix run)**: with `MIN_ZOOM=2` and `MARGIN_X=(hexRows−1)/2`, `clampPan` can pin the viewBox left edge PAST a low-q party — a party at (5,3) sat permanently off-screen and toolbar zoom-out clicks changed nothing visible. Recipe: seed the sandbox map's `partyPosition` near grid centre, or whole-doc PUT a new `partyPosition` — the settled board adopts via the map-data echo and the marker moves live. Related: Map-delete in the Maps manager is the role=dialog tier (confirm `Yes, Delete Permanently`, no native confirm), but a still-mounted board tab RE-STAMPS a deleted map ACTIVE (MapContextSync) — move that tab off the Map view before deleting. SHARPENED (live 2026-11-11 improve-run PT2 re-verify): the move-off cure is INSUFFICIENT after row **Open** — viewing a non-active map makes the client stamp `__map__.activeMapName` to the viewed map and that stamp survives a later DELETE even with the tab moved off first; close the tab (or clear `__map__`) and GET-verify before trusting activation state after deleting a map you opened. Row-button attribute selectors need the FULL quoted label (`button[aria-label='Deactivate "JV Insert Sandbox"']` — the bare unquoted form silently times out) and the label quotes the row's DISPLAYED name, not the file slug — a `Deactivate "slug"` selector no-ops whenever displayName≠slug (live 2026-11-11 improve marching-order-insert).
- **Cheapest map-history armer (live 2026-11-11 improve back-to-maps-label):** `handlePoiEnter` gates only on linkedMap *existence*, so an **outdoor→outdoor POI pair** (two throwaway outdoor maps with mutually linked POIs, party at `hexDistance===1` of the POI) arms the App map-history stack AND lands you on a HEX toolbar with the stack popped — no indoor handoff needed. The dynamic "Back to <map>" label (App `useMapHistory` → `backMapTarget` prop → both toolbars via `backToMapLabel`) reads `Back to <displayName||title-cased slug>` while armed, byte-identical `Back to maps` when the stack drains (reverts without remount).

AI authoring lane:
- **Referee/AI testing rigs**: deterministic proposal driving = `page.route('**/ai/referee*', r=>r.fulfill({ok:true, ruling:{…}}))` — everything downstream (card, picker gating, applyRuling, te writes, audit row) runs for real. But route-stubs answer AFTER server schema validation — to exercise invalid-output legs (retry→`needs_human`) fake the MODEL instead: keyless loopback OpenAI-compat server (e.g. :8123) steered by QA markers in the prompt. aiConfig rig: `POST /api/campaigns/:c/aiConfig {value:{mode,endpoint,model,provider:'openai'}}` — `provider:'local'` FAILS the validator; localhost endpoints only; restore = `DELETE` (GET then reports `{"value":null}`, configured:false). POST accepts unknown keys; merge-on-save preserves row-external keys; Decoding=probe REMOVES jsonSchema, blank Temperature REMOVES temperature. Admin AI badge/section SSE-live (no reload).
- **Grammar-echo fingerprint (oMLX + *-mtp builds)**: with aiConfig.jsonSchema=TRUE, refine + blueprint-repair faces return the input entity VERBATIM (server audits ok:true attempts:1 — never trust attempts alone); client `proposedNoChange()` echo-detect is the honest surface; server audits `ai_refine {noOp:true}` → Log row "… no change proposed — the model echoed the input". Keep jsonSchema:false for refine lanes.
- **AI audit log rows**: `ai_*` rows render prose CLIENT-SIDE via `AI_AUDIT_DESCRIBERS` in `src/components/log/log-utils.js` — server entries are structured-only; log-copy tickets belong there, not route files. `ai_ruling` (proposal, keeps model's original bad key) + `ai_ruling_applied` (GM-repaired key, `overridden:true`) pair is by design. Designer resume SUCCESS audits show `attempts:0` + created{} — machine truth = created counts + snapshot name, never attempts.
- **AI-Dress**: ops land as `placedItems` entries (type is a free string — "add wall" = wall-TYPE item; walls/paint/fog/image layers untouchable) — verify apply via placedItems array; `AiDressButton` toolbar trigger + dialog are mutually-exclusive render arms (mid-preview re-click structurally impossible); pre-disable when aiConfig unset/off (mode && mode!=='off', AiRefineButton.jsx precedent); no-config face (post-29e362837, live-verified CY): dock opens with `p.ai-instruction-hint[role=status]` "AI is not enabled for this campaign — configure it in Campaign Admin → AI Referee, then reopen this dialog." and the Dress map submit is DISABLED even with a typed instruction (`!aiLive` term in disabled expr, `aiLive = mode && mode!=='off'` read from runtime aiConfig live) — zero POST, zero 403, zero console error (the old fire-then-403 dance is retired). Without a model backend the dress POST honestly 200s an `AI_UNAVAILABLE` refusal — nothing saves. Applied POIs/tokens render live via SSE; dress fake-model loopback steered by a `dress`/`placedItems` marker in the prompt.
- **Encounters are ONE doc**: `data/encounters.json` `{encounters:[…]}`; AI drafts live there with `draft:true` (approve strips in place). Raw-API draft rigs MUST stamp `id: crypto.randomUUID()` — an id-less row can never be deleted/rejected by id. Faction full-array POST needs `{factions:[…]}` WRAPPER + echo ALL rows (partial array 409s). Banner-self-erase fix: `reload({keepError:true})` on SSE paths only. Mid-loop-erase batches order the FAILING row FIRST or the regression is vacuous.
- **Forced-draft face without a model ride + designer forced-face rigs (live 2026-11-13 fix designer-kind-word-name):** `PUT /api/campaigns/:c/npcs/<name>` with `draft:true` in the body stores verbatim (+stamp) through the CRUD lane — instant on-disk draft row (entity files are read from disk each GET, no debounce); `DELETE /npcs/<name>` restores md5 byte-exact. PITFALL: the planted row POISONS `entitySchemas.test.js` (it validates the REAL test-campaign rows) — remove repro artifacts before running the suite. `POST /ai/designer/generate` takes `scope` directly (curl-driveable, no chat/plan walk), but the reasoning model will NOT reliably emit a kind-word name even when the brief demands it (it answered a name-"npc" ask with a compliant empty blueprint at attempts:1) — pin repair-turn/stop faces with the scripted-model rig (`designer.test.js` pattern, `cfg.schemaName === 'draft_npc'`), never model roulette. The kind-word/placeholder NAME LAW is server-ENFORCED since 2026-11-13 (`src/services/ai/entityNameLaw.js` → `validateEntity` + `approveDraft` refusal), superseding the 2026-10-31 "SOFT prompt fix only — validators untouched" ruling.
- **Drafts-panel decision faces + world-keeper kind laws (live 2026-11-13 explore run ZC):** Approve-guard refusals are CLIENT-side throws surfaced in the CampaignBuilder error banner (single: `Not approved — name "npc" is a kind word — …`; batch `Approve all npcs`: `N of M npcs rows failed — they were NOT saved: “<name>”: <law>`) — row stays `draft:true`, intentional console.error fires. `Reject` CORRECTED IN PLACE (live 2026-11-13 explore run AD, `5fb8a2236`): Reject now arms the shared `DeleteConfirmModal` role=dialog — title `Reject Draft`, body names the draft (`This will permanently reject the AI draft '<name>'…`), buttons `Cancel`/`Yes, Delete Permanently`; Cancel writes NOTHING (row stays `draft:true`, zero audit rows), confirm destroys + audits. Modal-family law (CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-11-13 improve modal-escape-dismiss, `3f41dc16b`): `DeleteConfirmModal` routes Escape and a PURE backdrop click to `onCancel` (backdrop needs mousedown+click fully on the overlay — a panel→backdrop drag-release is guard-inert); Cancel/Escape/backdrop all write nothing, the confirm button stays the sole destructive exit, Tab trap unchanged. `ai_draft_approved`/`ai_draft_rejected` rows fire ONLY on success (a race-loser's failed DELETE writes none). TWO-GM-TAB race rig: `page.context().newPage()` (same context = second localhost GM tab), navigate/select in its own bounded run; with the modal tier, arm BOTH modals first (locator clicks fine — SSE disable never reaches modal buttons), then `Promise.all` the two `Yes, Delete Permanently` clicks. Loser copy CORRECTED IN PLACE (AD, `32744761f`): the raw `NPC not found` is now mapped to `Draft "<name>" was approved or removed in another tab or session since you loaded it — the drafts list has already refreshed — nothing to do here.` (both panels resync empty; any other error still passes through verbatim). Deterministic single-tab face of the same 404 lane (AD): arm the Reject modal, externally `DELETE /npcs/<name>` while it sits open, confirm → same conflict copy — no second tab needed. Snapshot zips live at `public/campaigns/.snapshots/` (SIBLING of campaign dirs — `public/campaigns/<c>/.snapshots` never matches; dist has its own triplet — count "pre-existing" per folder). Designer chat `Send` button textContent is `" Send"` (leading icon space, `^Send$` misses — same family as sidebar headers); `Generate drafts` busy label appends `(this can take a while)`; full-set generate ~60–120 s on the reasoning rig, poll the banner text across bounded runs. PROVENANCE LAW (live 2026-11-13 explore run AD, `931ae7be8`): when the GM's edited plan totals diverge from the model's proposal, the generate `ai_designer` row's `description` reads `AI designer drafted N entities per the edited plan (N planned, model proposed M, restore point <zip>)` and the row stamps `planned`/`proposed`; the PLAN row always keeps the raw model-proposed total; agreement face keeps the simple copy. Live bait failure twice (ZC + AD): a compliant Qwen honors the EDITED counts at attempts:1 even when the chat brief explicitly demands a zero-count kind (faction-sheet ask, plan `factions:0` → drafted exactly the 3 planned npcs, zero off-plan rows) — the TRIM-DROPPED live face is model-roulette, rely on the unit pin. World keeper: narrative kinds (recap/rumor/event/npc_dialogue) `Log it` = log row ONLY (`applyWorldProposal.js` narrativeApply — zero entity dirt, swept by the exit log clear), receipt badge `Applied: <kind>`; quest_draft receipt (live AD, `dd18b5fc3`): `Add quest to ledger` → badge `Applied: quest '<name>'` + `ai_world_applied` description `… quest '<name>' added directly to the ledger` — restore `DELETE /quests/<FULL-uuid>` → md5 5055fb78; the keeper CONTEXTS on the campaign log — a logged rumor/event surfaces verbatim-ish in later proposals (cross-ride continuity). Post-`355ebed53` the `.ai-world-advisory` element is ABSENT (not empty) when all names ground — faction canon and `'s`-possessives (Consortium's, Ironhaven's) no longer flag; presence-with-names still legitimately means truly-unknown — but quest TITLES are out of the diffed ledger (npcs+settlements+factions only), so a quest_draft card honestly naming an existing quest ALWAYS carries an advisory (AD live face: `check spelling: Lost Artifact`) — never file it as a bug.
- **409-conflict rig (deterministic)**: `page.route('**/api/…/<kind>', hold POST ~700ms once)` + a Node-`fetch` write from inside run_code_unsafe (:80 bypasses page routes) makes stale-stamp conflicts 100% reproducible; conflict banners quote the entity name so body.innerText "row gone" waits self-match — scope to `.campaign-builder-row:has-text(…)` with `state:'detached'`. Two-tab same-ms race: `page.context().pages()` + one `Promise.all(pages.map(p=>p.evaluate(click)))`.

Campaign management / wizard / auth lanes:
- **Confirm-modal tiers**: Regenerate Adventure, friendly-fire arming, seed modals, map/POI/item deletes, snapshot rollback are role=dialog custom modals (`[role=dialog]`, `aria-modal`, `aria-labelledby` — shared `common/DeleteConfirmModal.jsx` chrome; friendly-fire = FriendlyFireConfirmModal; regenerate anchor `data-testid="regenerate-adventure-btn"` — checklist-step aria no longer contains "Regenerate"). Clear Change Data / Clear Log / Full Reset + the three Data-Management natives (handleClear/handleRemoveNpc/handleAddNpc in createCreatureHandlers.js) stay `window.confirm` — owner-blessed, do NOT migrate without an owner decision. VERBATIM COPIES (all four legs captured live DG 2026-11-13 via the override pattern): +NPC `Add NPC with placeholder AC 10 / HP 10? Edit on the card.` (Cancel leg inert — no row), remove-NPC `NPC 1 has 10 HP. Remove anyway?` (accept removes the row), Clear Change Data `This will clear all runtime state for "test-campaign" including HP, conditions, spell slots, death saves, and target effects. You will need to re-establish combat state. Continue?`, Clear Campaign Log `This will permanently delete the campaign log for "test-campaign". Roll history will be lost. Continue?`. Dialog-confirm button labels MUST differ from the trigger text to keep `getByRole('button',{name})` unique across backdrop+dialog.
- **Admin status/notice lane**: StatusDisplay now carries `role=status aria-live=polite` (2a4b9ec0b) — post-action success matches `[role=status]`; `.admin-status--success/-error` classes coexist. Post-delete success toast = one-shot `sessionStorage.postDeleteNotice` consumed+removed on CampaignSelection mount → `.success-message[role=status]` via `useEphemeralToast(4000)`; rig it WITHOUT a real delete: setItem key → reload → assert + auto-hide legs.
- **Campaign-switch law (FIXED, App.jsx)**: `handleCampaignSelect` resets activeView+activeCharacter — the stale-sheet cross-campaign mutation bug is closed; the ONLY POST a campaign-switch legitimately makes to the NEW campaign is MapContextSync's one-time `__campaign__` context stamp (regression tests must exclude that key). `/admin` or URL-bar nav reloads to campaign-select — re-select.
- **Zero-char starter lane**: paint-order probe without creating a campaign = `page.route` mock `**/api/campaigns/<c>` → `{files:[]}` + `starter-seed` → wrapped fake, ABORT non-GETs; rAF-sample `.app-body`/wizard overlay. Codified: wizard decision + `combatSummaryLoaded` gate release are ONE React batch — checklist can never beat the wizard overlay. `page.route` handlers PERSIST across `goto` in the same tab — stale delay-routes fabricate phantom lag until unrouteAll. Quick-create default names are LOCAL-date (`localDateStamp()` in seededCampaign.js). Regen cascade order: POST /maps(new) → PUT maps → DELETE old → PUT waves → POST quests → PUT settlements → PUT starter-seed; checklist aside re-paints live via starter-seed SSE.
- **Cross-client adopt witness**: prove mounted consumers adopt external writes with a DISPLAY-ANCHORED key — `currentHitPoints` re-paints <1s after a curl full-store POST, no reload. `activeBuffs` aura effects are INERT-BY-DESIGN witnesses (aura holders have no badge handler) — absence of a badge ≠ failed adoption. LAN player surface = `browser_tabs {action:'new', isolatedContext:'lan', url:'http://<LAN-IP>:<vite-port>/'}`; from run_code_unsafe reach other tabs via `page.context().browser().contexts()` scanning `pages()`; no-reload adoption proof = `performance.getEntriesByType('navigation')` delta.
- **Wizard/character ops**: character create = `POST /api/campaigns/:campaign` (PUT `/:Name.json` is edit-only; the PUT dup-name overwrite residual needs a maps-rename-guard twin). `.rules-option` click auto-advances steps — a "failed" Add Character click may already have opened the overlay; wizard overlay × = `getByRole('button',{name:'Close character wizard'})` (testid lane) / `.character-creation-wizard-overlay button.close-btn` (no aria-label in some hosts — `[...overlay.querySelectorAll('button')].pop()` hits a sidebar tab instead). Fast-walk: whole create flow in ONE run_code (selectOption + loop Next gated on `disabled`); subclass required before Next at class step. Step headings self-renumber; internal `steps-config` numbers differ from UI headings (skills = internal 10). Point-buy overspend holds value inline (`.points-feedback`).
- **Server/dev ops addendum**: dev API is plain `node server.js` (no nodemon) — server-route edits are INVISIBLE until trio restart (confirm process start time > edit time before trusting a server-layer probe); restarting the trio can move Vite to **:5174** — read the port out of the dev log, don't assume 5173. `ps eww -p <pid>` recovers the live CAMPAIGN_LOCK verbatim before pkill+relaunch. Named CJS imports (`import { isEqual } from 'lodash'`) resolve UNDEFINED under plain-Node ESM — server-side shared modules need subpath default interop (`lodash/isEqual.js`).

Monster lane addenda (not in §§5–6/§10):
- **Constrict ongoing-damage rider (MA-1842 template)**: `escape_dc:N` + `grapple_ongoing_damage:{dice,damage_type}` → parseGrappleOngoingDamage (structured-key-only) → handlePlainDamage registers te `constrict_grapple` {source,dc} + ONE rounds:100 backstop clock after the condition leg → constrictGrappleService turn-start tick runs PRE-playerStats-guard (PC+monster victims), untyped direct-HP loss at the VICTIM's turn-start while held; release = lazy strip when Grappled gone (escape badge / rest / GM removal) + `constrict_grapple_released` log; grappler-dead stops ticks. ATTACK-LANE-only — a save-lane grapple (via `save_effect:"Grappled"`) has NO ongoing-damage producer, and its grants carry no dc → no escape badge (accepted residual).
- **save-lane te transport**: row `save_target_effect:"<registered te>"` → parseSaveTargetEffect → grantSaveTargetEffectClause on FAIL face ONLY + ONE anchored addExpiration `expireOnCreatureName:"<Monster N>"` (anchor's next turn-start = RAW "until start of its next turn", expiryRounds:null). Do NOT co-author with the MA-0711 prose lane on one row (disjoint te grants need separate lanes; two clocks on DISTINCT keys = no race). Attacker-anchored; victim-anchored prose lane = `parseSlowedClauses(save_effect)` ("can't take Reactions" byte-exact → te no_reactions, /next turn/ fork → rounds:2).
- **hit_choice chooser (MA-0855 template)**: structured `hit_choice:{options,note}` on row → `buildHitChoiceOffer` → HIT-popup ChoiceOffer → `resolveHitChoiceSelection` grants ONLY the chosen via single merged setRuntimeObject (activeConditions+meta{source,dc:null}) + `hit_choice_selected`; `buildHitConditionClause` SUPPRESSES the hit_conditions auto-grant while hit_choice is armed; unpicked Done = zero grants + `hit_choice_undecided` advisory, NEVER over-grant both. Charmed-class grants can end same-hit via the engine-wide took-damage-clears rule — adjudicate at pick-time.
- **Save-lane "Failure or Success:" clauses**: `parseBothOutcomesClause` (MonsterCardHelpers) requires the byte-exact marker in `save_effect` AND is PICKER-only (SaveAttackAoeModal); single-target save path is fail-only — missing marker = condition never lands on success.
- **Caster fold (summons)**: `summonSpiritHandler.resolveMonsterActions` folds caster spellAbilities (attack_bonus backfill, WIS/spellcasting-mod AND "spell level"→slotLevel) into spawned combatants; `normalizeSigns` collapses "+N+-M"→total — any new token-fold seam MUST normalize signs. Single-variant summons auto-confirm with NO chooser. Draconic Spirit has its own 2024 spell entry.
- **5e cantrip damage lane**: 5e cantrips carry `damage_at_character_level` while `spellDamageFormulaAtLevel` reads `damage_at_slot_level` (2024 twins) — monster-card 5e cantrip spell-attacks refuse honestly "no damage formula"; read caster-level dice from spells.json when structuring legendary cantrip rows. Live findMonsterSpell loads 5e THEN 2024 fallback (2024-only spells cast fine for 5e-joined monsters; the ruleset-branch mock pitfall is in-test only).
- **Composite attack+save chips**: on `attack_bonus` rows, ANY rollable-dice token + the word "damage" inside `save_effect` flips `saveLegIsConditionRider` false → the standalone DC chip goes UNCLICKABLE or double-charges. Recurring/ongoing damage prose MUST stay in `description`; rider-only `save_effect` carries the canonical condition word + non-damage clauses with ZERO dice.
- **Legendary chips**: numeric legendary CHILDREN self-suppress the "Expend Legendary" chip (the +N/DC chip IS the gated press surface — absence ≠ dead economy); "Costs 2/3 Actions" in names is name-text (engine spends 1/press, advisory floor); Expend-Legendary chips can absorb 2+ clicks — diff counter/log per click.
- **Reaction addenda**: parry-class prose without `automation{effect:'parry'}` = inert DATA one-field fix; `usage:"1/Day"` STRING (not `uses`) renders no counter EVER (formatActionUsage reads `usage` dict only); absorb-class (concentration-target/check-based) reactions are advisory-floor — carry full RAW numerics inside `advisory_message`; `lastAttack.isSpellDamage:true` is cosmetic on non-spell gazes; external POST of lastAttack reaches client runtime only after reload (seedCampaignRuntimeData seeds once/load) — live reaction proofs need reload-per-event.
- **Save-conditions ARE live on EB-NPC victims** via saveProcessing.processNpcSave→applyDamagelessSaveConditions (older "statusEffects-only" note is superseded); compound attack+save rows resolve as TWO chips (attack=always-full damage, save chip=condition/semi-damage). The NPC-inline adjudicator IGNORES stamped cs/activeBuffs bonuses (even nat-20 may read "failed" — pre-existing defect): prove save-success legs via the PC `.sp-overlay` prompt seam; `save-damage` `total` may be POST-hALVE — judge `finalDamage==|hpΔ|`.
- **Auras (PC side)**: Corona of Light (`sunlight_aura`) is SAVE-lane ONLY (handleNpcSaveDamage/handlePlayerSaveDamage/aoeService/DivineSparkModal; attack-lane resolver removed — do not re-add); fires vs ANY Fire/Radiant save spell ANYONE casts (authored text is caster-agnostic; `skipRangeCheck` left the 60-ft "Bright Light" clause unmeasured until the 2026-11-04 measured fork). Aura utils with a skipRangeCheck escape hatch MUST keep the measured/lenient decision INSIDE the util (wolf/lion `accumulateAuraCounts` convention) — dropping the flag at callers silently DISABLES the aura gridless. Power of the Wilds Lion stamp = `{effect:'power_of_wilds_option',optionName:'Lion',range:'5 ft',duration:'while_raging'}` (byte = lionAuraUtils matcher); Path stances: Rage `{effect:'stance',duration:'until_next_turn_extendable'}`, lv3 modal `animal_rage_option`, radios em-dash-labelled in `.sp-modal`; stance rows are `b.clickable` with trailing colons on chooser rows. Monster-lane aura gaps see archive — UPDATE 2026-11-10 (GB fix run): the Lion "another Barbarian who has this option active" exemption is now IMPLEMENTED (`lionAuraUtils.js` pre-loop `targetIsExemptFromLionAuras` — a target carrying its own `optionName:'Lion'` stamp is exempt from EVERY bearer's aura; bearer-self skip retained; both lanes ride the one util so sheet-sync/monster-card parity is structural) — exempt faces flip `mode:"normal"` with NO "rage aura of <bearer>" row; non-holder targets still penalized. Two-holder face recipe (live-proved): full-store bucket stamps on two characters carrying the canonical Lion buff bytes above (e.g. ElderPaladin + DraconicDragon) + HP keys on every victim face + an UNPLACED EB-joined monster attacker (gridless ⇒ `resolveMonsterLionAura` skipRangeCheck ⇒ no map/tokens needed); enemy face = third stampless PC (control, disadvantage lands); judge by the roll-row `mode` + the "rage aura of <bearer>" automation row (phantom-second-die law: rows read rolls:[a,b] on every monster press — mode is the only truth).
- **Save-AoE picker route (sheet casts)**: Actions spell name cell → spell-detail popup → Cast Spell → (metamagic chooser) → SaveAttackAoeModal picker with `overlay-<id>` tokens armed from spell-overlay POST (pre-checks exactly overlay-hit tokens); `handleAoeDamage`/`processAoeNpcs` are NOT reached from this surface — route fixes belong in the shared `resolveAllSavesAndDamage` hub. Picker prompts persist as change-data `savePrompt-<Target>-<ts>` (payload carries disadvantage/saveDc/saveType/rawDamage); `mode` (NOT forcedMode) + `saveRawRolls[0]` = normal; unresolved `.sp-overlay` prompts block ALL lanes minutes later — drain queue in Z-ORDER: Leading-Evasion chooser (`.sp-overlay--evasion`) stacks ABOVE the save prompt and its full-viewport `.sp-modal` intercepts the prompt's Roll Save click (Playwright click times out at 30s — always dismiss evasion Skip FIRST) → Roll Save → Next Save → Done → Results Close; picker overlay stays mounted with live "(N)" confirm until Results closes. `mode` (NOT forcedMode) + `saveRawRolls` = machine truth; normal-mode saveRawRolls can carry 2 entries (both dice logged, one kept) — read `mode`+`saveRoll`, never dice-count. Spell-overlay POST takes campaign as QUERY param (`?campaign=`, body-only 400).
- **In-page service probe (Vite dev)**: `await import('/src/services/...js')` inside page.evaluate calls the exported pure function against the LIVE runtime store — perfect seam isolation to split "resolver broken" from "caller broken". PITFALL (live 2026-11-11 weather-visibility run): the probe serves the MODULE CACHE — after editing the module, a plain reload-or-import returns the OLD export; append a `?cb=<ts>` query to the dynamic import path to force Vite re-transform (no page reload needed).

Playwright / tooling addenda (on top of §12):
- **Current MCP API**: click/evaluate take `target` (snapshot ref or unique selector), NOT `ref`; refs gain frame prefixes after navigations — regex `[ref=[a-z0-9]*e\d+\]`; mandatory keys: `browser_console_messages.level`, `browser_network_requests.static`, `browser_take_screenshot.scale` (`"css"`); `browser_run_code_unsafe` code must be an `async (page) => {...}` expression (bare IIFE → `__fn__ is not a function`); `browser_select_option` is now safe on the friendly-fire lane. `locator.click()` defaults to 30s — pass `{timeout:5000}`.
- **Dialog ops**: exactly ONE acceptor per stack — `page.on('dialog')` registered inside run_code PERSISTS for the page session; the wrapper still reports "Modal state…" after an in-run accept and the follow-up `browser_handle_dialog` then errors harmlessly — judge dialogs by post-state (API/board), never by the echo. Native confirm BEFORE the click: `page.once('dialog', d=>d.accept())` in the SAME call.
- **Screenshots**: land them in gitignored `tests/e2e/screenshots/` (read tool denies external temp; relative paths otherwise drop PNGs into the repo).
- **Test-side vitest addenda**: Vitest 4 + oxc parse-errors on dense nested vi.fn one-liners inside vi.mock factories — extract named helpers; default reporter swallows console.log even with --silent=false (write proof to a temp file); `vi.useFakeTimers({toFake:['Date']})` for jsdom date tests (full fake timers freeze waitFor); narrow-mock a util that gains exports with importOriginal spread or you get a late unhandled "No export defined on mock" in a DIFFERENT file; runtime-store mocks for `__map__.activeMapName` must return `null` not `[]` ([] is truthy — measured branches leak into gridless tests); random-threshold boundary tests must use the EXACT float the shared formula computes (`0.12 + 0.1` sums to `0.22000000000000003` — a hand-written `0.22` fires instead of refusing); co-located encounter tests importing saveProcessing use `../../hooks/combat/`; saveProcessing tests must NOT import MonsterCardModal.jsx (barrel handle-export requirement); complex-15 arithmetic: `?.` and `||` count as branches, CampaignSelection/MonsterCardModal/Map.jsx are at cap — hoist child components (CampaignListArea/DeleteNotice precedent).
- **Ops addenda**: full-array faction POST strips `draft` cleanly and re-stamps; Sessions DELETE by URL-encoded name = 200 no dialog; `window.confirm = m=>{seen.push(m); return bool}` override + synchronous dispatch inside ONE evaluate drives deterministic confirm faces (restore after); music cleanup without admin clear = scoped `DELETE /<Char>`-style `DELETE /api/campaigns/:c/music` (close tab → wait past debounce → DELETE → two-pass verify); `GET /changeData` (one word) silently hits the `:key` wildcard → false-`{"value":null}` — the route is hyphenated `/change-data`; API restart resets in-memory change-data to `[]` even when disk had content (lazy hydration) — empty GET post-restart ≠ empty disk; app load seeds combatSummary/`__campaign__`/<Char> within ~2.5s — "nothing written" checks clear AFTER the browser leg.
- **savePrompt probe lane (EP 2026-11-08)**: raw `POST /api/campaigns/:c/savePrompt-<Target>-<promptId>` `{value:{targetName, saveType:'Constitution', saveDc:15, source, condition, promptId}}` lands the store key but the SAME tab's SavePromptModal stays shut (it scans the `change-<camp>-savePrompt-` localStorage mirror at MOUNT + storage events) — surface it via GM-tab reload/reselect (prompt survives); field is `saveDc` (payload `dc` renders "DC" blank). Drain-witness rig for the pendingSaveListenerPrompts lane: seed `pendingSaveListenerPrompts:['<id>']` via POST, resolve via Roll Save → GM fires `DELETE /pendingSaveListenerPrompts` (evict) and both keys evaporate; fixed-build visitor fires ZERO writes (565e07581 verified). Enforcement face: POST /enforcement `{value:{mode:'on'}}` arms the gate; sweep via DELETE.
- **Multi-tab witnessing**: `globalThis.__vp` stashes do NOT survive between separate `browser_run_code_unsafe` calls — re-find the visitor page every call via `page.context().browser().contexts().flatMap(c=>c.pages()).find(p=>p.url().includes('<lan-ip>'))`; per-tab `vp.on('request'|'console'|'response')` collectors registered in each call DOUBLE-count if re-registered — register once per witness window and read the array in the SAME or a later call (arrays live in the Node wrapper scope, not globalThis).
